
## Name: Commoner
**Tier:** 1/4
**Origin:** Native (Human)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 15 (Con 5 × 3, Fragile)
**DR:** 0 (no armor)
**Speed:** 30 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 5 / Agil 5 / Dex 4 / Int 4 / Wis 5 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Background:** Laborer
**Skills:** Might 3, Athletics 3, Devices 3, Craft: Mason 3, Wilderness 3, Perception 3, Empathy 3, Society 3, Melee Combat 2
**Senses:** Normal
**Creature Type:** Humanoid (no special immunities)
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Languages:** Local (region-dependent)
### Attacks:
Club [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 2), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 8 damage (4 + 4 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 8 damage, DR halved (round up)
Alternative Actions:
Grapple/Shove: Str 4 + Athletics 3 (opposed by target's Str + Athletics)
  On success, can grapple (restrain) or shove (knock prone or push 5 ft)
### Feats:
Fast Healer: Short rest: gain +Con HP; long rest: gain double HP.
Forager: Find food and water for 6 people per day. With a Survival (hard) success:  find medicinal herbs worth 1d8 × 5 gp.
Quick Draw: Once per turn you may draw or stow a weapon as a [Free] action.
### Description
Everyday working people - laborers, dockworkers, farmers in off-season, craftspeople, servants. No combat training whatsoever. Strong and hardy from physical work (hence Con 5) but completely inexperienced in fighting.
### Tactics
Commoners avoid combat whenever possible. If forced to fight, they use improvised weapons (clubs, tools, pitchforks) and rely on numbers. More likely to grapple, shove, or flee than engage in actual weapon combat.
### Equipment
Club (improvised weapon), work clothes, work gloves, work boots.
### Variants
Farmer: Use Farmer background (Wilderness 3, Might 3, Athletics 3, Craft: Cook 3)
Dock Worker: Same stats, carries cargo hook instead of club (same damage)
Servant: Use Servant background (Empathy 3, Society 3, Perception 3, Stealth 3, Deception 3, Pattern A with +40 XP), lower Str to 3, raise Dex to 4
Craftsperson: Use an appropriate Artisan background and Craft skill at rank 3 or 4.

## Name: Gang Member
**Tier:** 1/2
**Origin:** Native (Human)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 16 (Con 4 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** 0 (no armor)
**Speed:** 30 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 4 / Agil 7 / Dex 6 / Int 3 / Wis 3 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Stealth 3, Thievery 4, Deception 3, Intimidate 3, Perception 3, Melee Combat 3, Ranged Combat 2, Athletics 3, Medicine 3
**Background:** Criminal
**XP:** 24 XP (Thievery 3 → 4)
**Senses:** Normal
**Creature Type:** Humanoid (no special immunities)
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Languages:** Local (region-dependent)
### Attacks
Dagger [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 8 damage (4 + 4 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 8 damage, DR halved (round up)
Thrown Dagger [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 6 + Ranged Combat 2), Range: 30/60 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 10 damage (4 + 6 Dex)
- **Margin 2:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up)
### Feats
Swift Movement: You can Disengage or Hide using only 1 [Minor] action instead of a full action.
Exploit Weakness: Once per round, when you attack with an ally within 5 feet of your target OR you subtract from your attack dice (from any source), add your Dexterity score to damage.
Quick Draw: Once per turn you may draw or stow a weapon as a [Free] action.
Purchased Feats: None (0 at TR 1/2)
### Description
Dockside toughs, smugglers' runners, and hired muscle who know how to make a ship's crew regret refusing a demand. Gang members are dangerous in alleys, taverns, and cramped holds where several attackers can reach the same target.
### Tactics
Gang members use Swift Movement to dart between cover and allies. They trigger Exploit Weakness through numbers or by attacking a target already under pressure, then disengage before the target can retaliate. They surrender or flee when their leader falls.
### Equipment
Dagger, club, crowbar, dark work clothes, belt pouch


## Name: Militia Member
**Tier:** 1/2
**Origin:** Native (Human)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 20 (Con 5 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** S: 2, P: 1, B: 3 (aketon)
**Speed:** 30 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 5 / Agil 7 / Dex 5 / Int 3 / Wis 4 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 3, Athletics 3, Intimidate 3, Perception 3, Wilderness 3, Medicine 3, Society 3, Ranged Combat 2
**Background:** Soldier
**Senses:** Normal
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Humanoid (no special immunities)
**Languages:** Local (region-dependent)
### Attacks
Spear [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 5 ft
One-handed:
- **Margin 1:**: 10 damage (6 + 4 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up)
Two-handed (versatile):
- **Margin 1:**: 10 damage (6 + 4 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up)
### Reactions
Shield Block (from Buckler): [Reaction] when hit while wielding the buckler, choose one: gain +4 DR against this attack or force the attacker to reroll one success die.
Shield Master: Once per combat as a [Reaction], completely negate one attack that would hit you.
### Feats
Defensive Stance: Can enter a defensive stance as a toggle. While in defensive stance, opponents add 1 to both attack dice when attacking you. Can enter or leave this stance at the start of your turn.
Danger Sense: Subtract 1 from both dice on Reflex saves against effects you can see.
Shield Master: Subtract 1 from both dice on Reflex saves while using a shield. Once per combat as a [Reaction], completely negate one attack that would hit you.
### Description
Local militia members called to defend their town or region. Part-time soldiers - farmers, craftsmen, merchants, and workers who train together regularly for local defense. More disciplined and organized than bandits but less professional than full-time soldiers. Loyal to their community and willing to defend it, though they may break and flee if facing overwhelming odds or supernatural threats. They know the local area well and can call for reinforcements from fellow militia or town guards.
### Tactics
Militia members use defensive tactics, forming shield walls with Defensive Stance when outnumbered. They use spears to maintain reach, keeping enemies at bay while supporting each other. Shield Master allows them to negate one devastating attack per fight - typically saved for critical moments. They fight in coordinated groups when possible, using their numbers and defensive training to overcome individual skill gaps. Against ranged attackers, they advance in formation, shields raised.
### Equipment
Spear, buckler, aketon armor, uniform, bedroll, high boots, common clothes
### Variants
Militia Archer: Swap the combat ranks (Ranged Combat 3, Melee Combat 2), use Dex as the attack attribute (raise Dex to 7, lower Agil to 5), use a shortbow instead of a spear, replace Shield Master with Precise Shot
Militia Sergeant: Add +1 to Cha (becomes 3), add Intimidating Presence as a purchased feat (requires Cha 4 - would need to adjust attributes or wait for TR 1 version)

## Name: Pirate
**Tier:** 1
**Origin:** Native (Human)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 20 (Con 5 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** 0 (no armor)
**Speed:** 30 ft, Swim 15 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 5 / Agil 8 / Dex 4 / Int 3 / Wis 3 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Athletics 3, Acrobatics 3, Perception 3, Wilderness 3, Melee Combat 4, Ranged Combat 3, Stealth 3, Thievery 4
**Background:** Sailor
**XP:** 48 XP (Melee Combat 3 → 4; Thievery 3 → 4)
**Senses:** Normal
**Creature Type:** Humanoid (no special immunities)
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Languages:** Local (region-dependent)
### Attacks
Scimitar [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 4), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 10 damage (6 + 4 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up)
Thrown Dagger [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 4 + Ranged Combat 3), Range: 30/60 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 8 damage (4 + 4 Dex)
- **Margin 2:** 8 damage, DR halved (round up)
### Feats
Quick Draw: Once per turn you may draw or stow a weapon as a [Free] action.
Power Attack: Declare before making a melee attack roll. Add 1 to both die results and deal +4 damage on hit.
Exploit Weakness: Once per round, when you attack with an ally within 5 feet of your target OR you subtract from your attack dice (from any source), add your Dexterity score to damage.
### Description
A practiced raider who has survived enough boarding actions to become dangerous. Pirates may serve a captain, prey on isolated vessels, or work as privateers whose legality depends on which flag is flying.
### Tactics
Pirates open with thrown daggers, then cross the gap quickly. They use Power Attack against isolated targets and Exploit Weakness when an ally is adjacent. They cut ropes, take hostages, and use the ship's rail as both cover and a weapon. Pirates break when the captain is clearly defeated unless greed gives them a reason to stay.
### Equipment
Scimitar, dagger, coil of rope, sailor's clothes, grappling hook

## Name: Pirate Captain
**Tier:** 2
**Origin:** Native (Human)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 20 (Con 5 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** S: 2, P: 1, B: 3 (reinforced coat)
**Speed:** 30 ft, Swim 15 ft
**Attributes:** Str 5 / Con 5 / Agil 8 / Dex 4 / Int 3 / Wis 3 / Cha 5 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Athletics 3, Acrobatics 3, Perception 3, Wilderness 3, Melee Combat 5, Ranged Combat 4, Society 3, Deception 3, Intimidate 3
**Background:** Sailor
**XP:** 56 XP (Melee Combat 3 → 5)
**Senses:** Normal
**Creature Type:** Humanoid (no special immunities)
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Languages:** Local (region-dependent)
### Attacks
Longsword [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 5), Reach: 5 ft
One-handed:
- **Margin 1:**: 13 damage (8 + 5 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 13 damage, DR halved (round up)
Two-handed (versatile):
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (9 + 5 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up)
Thrown Dagger [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 4 + Ranged Combat 4), Range: 30/60 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 8 damage (4 + 4 Dex)
- **Margin 2:** 8 damage, DR halved (round up)
### Reactions
Command the Crew: Once per round when an ally within 30 feet fails an attack or save, the captain may use a [Reaction] to grant that ally +1 to both dice on a reroll of the same attempt. The reroll stands.
### Feats
Defensive Stance: Can enter a defensive stance as a toggle. While in defensive stance, opponents add 1 to both attack dice when attacking you. Can enter or leave this stance at the start of your turn.
Power Attack: Declare before making a melee attack roll. Add 1 to both die results and deal +4 damage on hit
Rally Cry: You give a rousing speech to inspire your companions. Choose up to 6 allies who can hear and understand you. Each gains Temp HP equal to your Tier + your Charisma score. These Temp HP last until their next long rest.
Intimidating Presence:  As 1 [Combat] action, choose one creature within 30 feet that can see or hear you. Target makes Will save opposed by your Charisma + Intimidation. If you win, target gains the Frightened condition until end of your next turn.
Battle-Hardened: When a creature hits you with an attack, that creature adds 2 to all subsequent attack dice against you until the start of your next turn.
### Description
The commander of a pirate vessel, mutinous crew, or coastal raiding band.
### Tactics
The captain stays near the center of the crew, opening with Rally Cry or Intimidating Presence before choosing a vulnerable target. Defensive Stance protects the captain while the crew closes. Power Attack is reserved for an enemy already isolated or cornered against the rail. A captain who loses half the crew may bargain, flee, or surrender rather than die for cargo.
### Equipment
Longsword, daggers, reinforced coat, spyglass, whistle, grappling hook, chart case

## Name: Press Gang Enforcer
**Tier:** 1
**Origin:** Native (Human)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 20 (Con 5 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** S: 2, P: 1, B: 3 (aketon)
**Speed:** 30 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 5 / Agil 8 / Dex 5 / Int 3 / Wis 3 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 4, Athletics 3, Intimidate 3, Wilderness 3, Perception 3, Society 3, Ranged Combat 3, Medicine 3
**Background:** Mercenary
**XP:** 24 XP (Melee Combat 3 → 4)
**Senses:** Normal
**Creature Type:** Humanoid (no special immunities)
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Languages:** Local (region-dependent)
### Attacks
Club [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 4), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 8 damage (4 + 4 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 8 damage, DR halved (round up)
### Reactions
Shield Block (from Buckler): [Reaction] when hit while wielding the buckler, choose one: gain +4 DR against this attack or force the attacker to reroll one success die.
Shield Master Negation: Once per combat as a [Reaction], completely negate one attack that would hit you.
### Feats
Defensive Stance: Can enter a defensive stance as a toggle. While in defensive stance, opponents add 1 to both attack dice when attacking you. Can enter or leave this stance at the start of your turn.
Shield Master: Subtract 1 from both dice on Reflex saves while using a shield. Once per combat as a [Reaction], completely negate one attack that would hit you.
Formation Fighting: When you and at least two allies adjacent to you are all wielding shields, all of you gain +2 DR. When an adjacent ally is attacked, you can use a [Reaction] to grant them -2 to both dice on their defense roll.
### Description
A recruiter, debt collector, or naval officer who fills a ship by force. Press-gang enforcers are trained to seize people quickly, break resistance, and keep frightened captives moving toward the docks.
### Tactics
Enforcers work in pairs. One blocks escape while the other uses Intimidate and grapples. They prefer clubs and restraints when the goal is capture, but abandon restraint when the target fights back.
### Equipment
Club, buckler, aketon armor, manacles, uniform, whistle

## Name: Sailor
**Tier:** 1/2
**Origin:** Native (Human)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 16 (Con 4 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** 0 (no armor)
**Speed:** 30 ft, Swim 15 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 4 / Agil 7 / Dex 6 / Int 3 / Wis 4 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Athletics 3, Acrobatics 3, Perception 3, Wilderness 3, Melee Combat 3, Ranged Combat 2, Society 3, Devices 3
**Background:** Sailor
**XP:** 0 XP beyond background
**Senses:** Normal
**Creature Type:** Humanoid (no special immunities)
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Languages:** Local (region-dependent)
### Attacks
Scimitar [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 10 damage (6 + 4 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up)
Thrown Dagger [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 6 + Ranged Combat 2), Range: 30/60 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 10 damage (4 + 6 Dex)
- **Margin 2:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up)
### Feats
Quick Draw: Once per turn you may draw or stow a weapon as a [Free] action.
Fast Healer: Short rest: gain +Con HP; long rest: gain double HP.
Forager: Find food and water for 6 people per day. With a Survival (hard) success: find medicinal herbs worth 1d8 × 5 gp.
Purchased Feats: None (0 at TR 1/2)
### Description
Deckhands, riggers, navigators, and ordinary crew who know how to keep a vessel moving when the weather turns foul. Sailors are not necessarily soldiers, but shipboard labor builds balance, endurance, and a practical instinct for danger.
### Tactics
Sailors fight as a crew. They use the rail, rigging, and narrow decks to limit enemy movement, throw daggers before closing, and shove enemies toward the water. They avoid prolonged fights against trained soldiers and retreat toward their ship when separated from the crew.
### Equipment
Scimitar, dagger, rope, work clothes, belt knife, mariner's tools

## Name: Thug
**Tier:** 1/2
**Origin:** Native (Human)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 16 (Con 4 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** 0 (no armor)
**Speed:** 30 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 4 / Agil 7 / Dex 6 / Int 3 / Wis 3 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Stealth 3, Thievery 4, Deception 3, Intimidate 3, Perception 3, Might 3, Athletics 3, Ranged Combat 2, Medicine 3, Melee Combat 3
**Background:** Criminal
**XP:** 24 XP (Thievery 3 → 4)
**Senses:** Normal
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Humanoid (no special immunities)
**Languages:** Local (region-dependent)
### Attacks:
Dagger [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 8 damage (4 + 4 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 8 damage, DR halved (round up)
With Exploit Weakness: When ally within 5 ft of target OR subtracting from attack dice, add Dex 5 to damage
Thrown (Range 30/60 ft): Use Dex 5 + Ranged Combat 3
- **Margin 1:**: 8 damage (4 + 4 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 8 damage, DR halved (round up)
### Feats
Swift Movement: Can Disengage or Hide using only 1 [Minor] action instead of a full action. Perfect for hit-and-run tactics.
Danger Sense: Subtract 1 from both dice on Reflex saves against effects you can see. Represents street-smart reflexes.
Exploit Weakness: Once per round, when attacking with an ally within 5 feet of target OR subtracting from attack dice (from any source), add Dexterity score (5) to damage.
Purchased Feats: None (0 at TR 1/2)
### Description
Street criminals - muggers, gang members, hired muscle for criminal organizations. More dangerous than commoners due to actual combat training (Melee 3) and willingness to use violence. Criminal background gives them underworld contacts for fencing stolen goods.
### Tactics
Thugs rely on numbers and dirty fighting. They use Swift Movement to dart in, attack with allies nearby (triggering Exploit Weakness for +5 damage), then Disengage as a [Minor] action to avoid retaliation. Prefer ambushes from stealth (Stealth 3) and will flee if facing organized opposition or taking heavy damage. 
### Equipment
Dagger, crowbar, common clothes, belt pouch, wool hood, low boots
### Variants
Pickpocket: Leave Thievery at 4, leave Melee Combat at baseline rank 2 (omit it from the stat block), more focused on theft than combat, use Swift Movement to escape after stealing.
Enforcer: Use Mercenary background for better combat training, represents gang muscle who collects debts and intimidates.
Burglar: Raise Stealth 3→4 (24 XP), replace Danger Sense with Supreme Stealth feat when qualified at TR 2, focused on breaking and entering.

## Name: Town Guard
**Tier:** 1
**Origin:** Native (Human)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 20 (Con 5 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** S: 6, P: 5, B: 2 (chain mail)
**Speed:** 30 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 5 / Agil 8 / Dex 4 / Int 3 / Wis 4 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 4, Perception 3, Intimidate 3, Society 3, Athletics 3, Wilderness 3, Medicine 3, Ranged Combat 3
**Background:** Guard
**Senses:** Normal
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Humanoid (no special immunities)
**Languages:** Local (region-dependent)
### Attacks:
Longsword [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 4), Reach: 5 ft
One-handed:
- **Margin 1:**: 12 damage (8 + 4 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up)
Two-handed (versatile):
- **Margin 1:**: 12 damage (8 + 4 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up)
With Power Attack (declared before roll): Add 1 to both dice, deal +4 damage on hit
### Reactions
Ranged Defense (from heater shield): [Reaction] when targeted by a ranged attack, force Misfortune on that attack.
Shield Block (from heater shield): [Reaction] when hit while wielding the shield, choose one: gain +4 DR against this attack or force the attacker to reroll one success die.
Shield Master Negation: Once per combat as a [Reaction], completely negate one attack that would hit you.
### Feats
Defensive Stance: Can enter defensive stance where opponents add 1 to both attack dice when attacking you. Toggle on/off at start of turn. Used when outnumbered or protecting others.
Danger Sense: Subtract 1 from both dice on Reflex saves against effects you can see. Represents trained awareness.
Power Attack: Declare before rolling - add 1 to both die results and deal +4 damage on hit. Risk/reward for ending fights quickly.
Shield Master: Subtract 1 from both dice on Reflex saves while using shield. Once per combat as a [Reaction], completely negate one attack that would hit you
Purchased Feats: Power Attack (1 at TR 1)
With Heater Shield: Gains Fortune on defensive melee rolls
### Description
Professional city guards - trained, disciplined, paid soldiers who take their duty seriously. Unlike militia (part-timers) or thugs (criminals), these are career guardsmen with years of experience. They know the law, recognize troublemakers, and maintain order. Respect from citizens, feared by criminals. Will show mercy to those who surrender but are hardened enough to use lethal force when necessary. Loyal to their city and fellow guards - won't abandon posts or comrades easily.
### Tactics
Town guards are professional soldiers who patrol cities and enforce laws. They use formation tactics, working in pairs or small groups. When facing threats, they use Defensive Stance to protect citizens while calling for backup. Against dangerous foes, they use Power Attack to end fights quickly before reinforcements arrive. The heater shield provides excellent defense against both melee (Fortune on defense rolls) and ranged ### Attacks (force Misfortune on attackers).
Guards prioritize de-escalation and arrest over lethal force when possible, using Intimidate 3 to command compliance. They know the local area (Society 3) and can quickly summon help. Against multiple weak opponents, they hold defensive positions. Against single strong opponents, they use Power Attack aggressively while one guard runs for help.
Shield Master's once-per-combat negation is saved for critical moments - blocking a potentially fatal blow to themselves or buying time for civilians to escape.
### Equipment
Longsword, heater shield, chain mail, uniform, manacles, work gloves, work boots
### Variants
Gate Guard: Replace Intimidate 3 with Investigate 3, focused on spotting smugglers and checking papers
Archer Guard: Replace Melee 4 with Ranged 4, use longbow instead of longsword, swap Power Attack for Precise Shot
Sergeant: Add +1 Cha (becomes 3), can lead 4-6 guards effectively

## Name: Dog

**Origin:** Native (Beast)
**Size:** Small
**HP:** 6 (Con 2 × 3, Fragile)
**DR:** 0
**Speed:** 40 ft
**Attributes:** Str 2 / Con 2 / Agil 5 / Dex 3 / Int 3 / Wis 5 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 1, Perception 3, Stealth 3, Athletics 2
**Senses:** Keen Smell
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Beast
**Languages:** None

Bite [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 1), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 5 damage (3 + 2 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 5 damage, DR halved (round up)
### Special Abilities
Keen Smell: Scent-based Perception checks have Fortune.
Pack ### Tactics: When an ally is within 5 feet of the target, subtract 1 from both attack dice.

## Name: Dog, Guard
**Tier:** 1/4
**Origin:** Native (Beast)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 15 (Con 3 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 1 (dense coat)
**Speed:** 40 ft
**Attributes:** Str 3 / Con 3 / Agil 5 / Dex 4 / Int 3 / Wis 5 / Cha 2 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 1, Perception 4, Stealth 3, Athletics 3
**Senses:** Keen Smell
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Beast
**Languages:** None
### Attacks
Bite [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 1), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 8 damage (5 + 3 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 8 damage, DR halved (round up)
### Special Abilities
Keen Smell: Scent-based Perception checks have Fortune.
Hold the Intruder: When the Guard Dog hits with Margin 2, the target must make a Fortitude save opposed by the dog's Str 4 + Athletics 3. On a loss, the target is knocked Prone.
Trained Guard: The Guard Dog does not bolt when frightened or injured while protecting its assigned area. It still follows the ordinary mount rules if ridden, though it is not a mount by default.
### Tactics
Guard Dogs alert their handlers, flank intruders, and attack the nearest creature that crosses the boundary they are protecting. They hold a target in place rather than pursuing beyond their post.

## Name: Dog, Hunting
**Tier:** 1/4
**Origin:** Native (Beast)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 15 (Con 3 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 0
**Speed:** 50 ft
**Attributes:** Str 3 / Con 3 / Agil 6 / Dex 4 / Int 2 / Wis 5 / Cha 2 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 1, Perception 4, Stealth 4, Wilderness 4, Athletics 3
**Senses:** Keen Smell
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Beast
**Languages:** None
### Attacks
Bite [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 1), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 8 damage (5 + 3 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 8 damage, DR halved (round up)
### Special Abilities
Keen Smell: Scent-based Perception checks have Fortune.
Track by Scent: The Hunting Dog has Fortune on Wilderness checks to follow a creature that has passed through the area within the last day.
Flush Prey: Once per encounter, when the Hunting Dog hits a Hidden or Concealed target, that target loses Hidden and cannot benefit from Concealed against the dog's allies until the start of the dog's next turn.
### Tactics
Hunting Dogs work ahead of their handlers, locate prey, and drive it toward waiting hunters. They avoid trading bites with heavily armored creatures and use speed to keep wounded prey from escaping.

## Name: Horse
**Tier:** 1/4
**Origin:** Native (Beast)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 12 (Con 3 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** 0
**Speed:** 60 ft
**Attributes:** Str 3 / Con 3 / Agil 5 / Dex 4 / Int 2 / Wis 5 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Athletics 3, Perception 3
**Senses:** Normal
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Beast
**Languages:** None
### Attacks
Hooves [Combat]: Only when cornered or directed by a combat-trained rider. Melee attack (Agil 4 + Melee Combat 2), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 6 damage
- **Margin 2:** 6 damage, DR halved (round up)
### Special Abilities
Sure-Footed: The Horse has Fortune on Athletics checks made to keep footing on ordinary slopes, loose ground, or a moving deck.
Gallop: When directed by its rider, the Horse can use two [Move] actions to move up to double its speed.
### Description
Working mount. The horse is not combat-trained and uses the ordinary bolt rules when frightened or injured while carrying a rider. It can carry a rider and normal travel gear; use its Strength and Large size for encumbrance.
### Tactics
An ordinary horse flees danger rather than seeking combat. Its primary value is speed, carrying capacity, and reliable travel.

## Name: Insect Swarm
**Tier:** 1/8
**Origin:** Native (Beast, Swarm)
**Size:** Swarm (5 × 5 ft)
**HP:** 6 (Con 2 × 3, Fragile)
**DR:** 0
**Speed:** 20 ft, Fly 30 ft
**Attributes:** Str 1 / Con 2 / Agil 5 / Dex 3 / Int 3 / Wis 6 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 1, Stealth 4, Perception 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 30 ft, Vibration Sense
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Swarm (Tiny insects)
**Languages:** None
### Attacks
Stinging Cloud: Opposed Reflex save using Agil 4 + Melee Combat 2 against every creature sharing the swarm's space.
Target loses: 6 piercing damage and gains Poisoned (Weak) for 1 minute.
Target wins: 3 piercing damage.
At half HP or lower, deal 3 piercing damage on a loss and 1 piercing damage on a win; Poisoned (Weak) still applies on a loss.
### Special Abilities
Distracting Buzz: A creature that takes full damage adds 1 to both dice on its next attack before the end of its next turn.
Swarm Body: The Insect Swarm can occupy another creature's space and move through openings large enough for its constituent insects.
Light Avoidance: In bright light, the Insect Swarm cannot Hide unless it has cover.
Swarms: Swarms occupy a 5 × 5 ft space, halve physical damage, ignore Grappled, Restrained, Prone, and Bleeding, and resolve their ### Attacks as opposed Reflex saves. Each entry lists full damage and reduced damage once the swarm is at half HP or lower.
### Tactics
Insect Swarms seek exposed faces, eyes, and gaps in clothing. They harry spellcasters and crew members trying to extinguish fires, then scatter when forced into open bright ground.

## Name: Jellyfish Swarm
**Tier:** 1/2
**Origin:** Native waters (Beast, Swarm)
**Size:** Swarm (5 × 5 ft)
**HP:** 12 (Con 4 × 3, Fragile)
**DR:** 0
**Speed:** Swim 30 ft
**Attributes:** Str 3 / Con 4 / Agil 6 / Dex 5 / Int 3 / Wis 5 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 3, Stealth 4, Perception 4
**Senses:** Blindsight 30 ft in water
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Swarm (Jellyfish)
**Languages:** None
### Attacks
Stinging Tendrils: Opposed Reflex save using Agil 4 + Melee Combat 3 against every creature sharing the swarm's space.
Target loses: 8 lightning damage and must make a Fortitude save with 1 success required or become Poisoned for 1 minute.
Target wins: 4 lightning damage.
At half HP or lower, deal 4 lightning damage on a loss and 2 lightning damage on a win; the Poisoned save still applies on a loss.
### Special Abilities
Transparent Body: In clear water, ### Attacks against the Jellyfish Swarm add 1 to both dice unless the attacker is adjacent or using an area effect.
Drifting Colony: The Jellyfish Swarm ignores difficult terrain caused by water currents and can move through spaces occupied by creatures.
Swarm Body: The Jellyfish Swarm cannot be Grappled, Restrained, Prone, or Bleeding.
Swarms: Swarms occupy a 5 × 5 ft space, halve physical damage, ignore Grappled, Restrained, Prone, and Bleeding, and resolve their ### Attacks as opposed Reflex saves. Each entry lists full damage and reduced damage once the swarm is at half HP or lower.
### Tactics
Jellyfish Swarms drift with currents until they encounter movement, warmth, or light. They surround swimmers and deckhands near the waterline, forcing characters to choose between taking the sting and leaving the water.

## Name: Kelpbound Colossus
**Tier:** 4
**Origin:** Native waters (Plant)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 4 (dense kelp and waterlogged wood)
**Speed:** Swim 30 ft, Walk 15 ft
**Attributes:** Str 6 / Con 6 / Agil 7 / Dex 6 / Int 3 / Wis 7 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Might 5, Stealth 4, Perception 3
**Senses:** Blindsight 60 ft through water and living vegetation
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Plant
**Languages:** None
### Attacks
Kelp Slam [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 15 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 16 damage (10 + 6 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 16 damage, DR halved (round up); target is Grappled
Multiattack [Combat]: The Kelpbound Colossus makes two Kelp Slam ### Attacks against one or two targets.
Shared Attribute Roll (Agil 4): Roll Agility once and apply it to both ### Attacks. Roll Melee Combat separately for each attack.
### Special Abilities
False Appearance: While motionless in a kelp forest, mangrove channel, or wreck covered in vegetation, the Kelpbound Colossus is indistinguishable from the surrounding growth. A creature that succeeds on a Hard Perception check notices movement or an unnatural shape before it ### Attacks.
Kelp Snare (Recharge 5-8) [Combat]: Kelp erupts in a 20-foot square within 60 feet. Creatures in the area make Reflex saves opposed by the Colossus's Might 5 + Melee Combat 6. On a loss, a creature becomes Restrained until it succeeds on a Hard Athletics or Acrobatics check as a [Combat] action. The area is Difficult Terrain for 1 minute.
Tide-Fed Renewal: At the start of its turn while submerged in natural water, the Kelpbound Colossus regains 4 HP. This does not function while it is on fire or while it has taken fire damage since the end of its last turn.
Plant Resilience: The Kelpbound Colossus is immune to Blinded, Deafened, and Exhaustion. It does not need air, food, or sleep.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Tactics
The Kelpbound Colossus waits motionless in a kelp forest or around a wreck, then uses Kelp Snare to separate swimmers from their boats. It grapples one target while its second slam controls the rescue attempt. It retreats into dense vegetation when exposed to fire and returns once the threat is gone.

## Name: Leviathan
**Tier:** 8
**Origin:** Deep ocean (Monstrosity)
**Size:** Gargantuan
**HP:** 240 (Con 8 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 8 (ancient plates)
**Speed:** Swim 100 ft, Walk 10 ft
**Attributes:** Str 8 / Con 8 / Agil 8 / Dex 7 / Int 5 / Wis 7 / Cha 6 / Luck 6
**Skills:** Melee Combat 8, Athletics 8, Perception 7, Intimidate 7, Wilderness 6
**Senses:** Darkvision 240 ft, Tremorsense 120 ft in water
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Monstrosity
**Languages:** None
### Attacks
Devouring Maw [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 8), Reach: 20 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 22 damage (14 + 8 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 22 damage, DR halved (round up); target is Grappled and pulled into the maw
Colossal Tail [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 8) against every creature within 20 feet behind the Leviathan
- **Margin 1:**: 22 damage (14 + 8 Str); target is pushed 20 ft
- **Margin 2:** 22 damage, DR halved (round up); target is pushed 40 ft and knocked Prone
Multiattack [Combat]: The Leviathan makes one Devouring Maw attack and one Colossal Tail attack.
Shared Attribute Roll (Agil 5): Roll Agility once and apply it to both ### Attacks.
### Special Abilities
World-Turning Wake: When the Leviathan moves through water, it creates a 30-foot-wide wake. Creatures and unsecured objects in the wake make a Reflex save or are pushed 30 feet, knocked Prone, and take 8 bludgeoning damage. A successful save halves the damage and movement.
Shipbreaker: The Leviathan's ### Attacks deal double damage to vessels, structures, and other objects. A vessel reduced to 0 HP begins sinking immediately.
Call of the Deep (Recharge 5-8) [Combat]: Every creature that can hear the Leviathan within 120 feet makes a Will save. On a failure, it gains Frightened and must use its next [Move] action to approach the water or the Leviathan. On a success, it is immune to this Leviathan's Call of the Deep for 24 hours.
Aquatic Body: The Leviathan can breathe water indefinitely. On land, it moves at half speed and adds 1 to both attack dice, but it cannot use World-Turning Wake.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Tactics
A Leviathan is a moving disaster rather than a normal predator. It ### Attacks ships, coastal fortifications, and anything that disturbs the deep. It opens with Call of the Deep, breaks the vessel with Shipbreaker, and devours creatures attempting to escape. It does not pursue prey far from its territory once the threat has been destroyed.

## Name: Mule
**Tier:** 1/4
**Origin:** Native (Beast)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 16 (Con 4 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** 0
**Speed:** 40 ft
**Attributes:** Str 3 / Con 4 / Agil 5 / Dex 3 / Int 3 / Wis 4 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Athletics 3, Perception 3
**Senses:** Normal
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Beast
**Languages:** None
### Attacks
Kick [Combat]: Only when cornered. Melee attack (Agil 3 + Melee Combat 2), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 6 damage
- **Margin 2:** 6 damage, DR halved (round up)
### Special Abilities
Sure-Footed: The Mule has Fortune on Athletics checks made to keep footing on steep, loose, or uneven ground.
Pack Animal: Treat the Mule as one size category larger for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting. This benefit does not change its combat size.
Stubborn: The Mule has Fortune on saves against Frightened caused by environmental danger, but not against magical fear.
### Description
 Working animal. The Mule is not combat-trained and uses the ordinary bolt rules when frightened or injured while carrying a rider. Its stubbornness grants Fortune on checks to resist being forced into obviously dangerous movement.
### Tactics
A Mule avoids combat, refuses obviously suicidal commands, and kicks only when cornered. It is valued for carrying supplies through terrain where a horse would tire or lose its footing.

## Name: Piranha Swarm

Target loses: 8 piercing damage.
Target wins: 4 piercing damage.
At half HP or lower, deal 4 piercing damage on a loss and 2 piercing damage on a win.
### Special Abilities
Blood Frenzy: Against a creature below half HP, the Piranha Swarm subtracts 1 from both attack dice.
Schooling: The Piranha Swarm can move through the spaces of other creatures without provoking opportunity ### Attacks.
Blood Scent: The Piranha Swarm has Fortune on Perception checks to locate a creature that has taken damage in the water.
Swarms: Swarms occupy a 5 × 5 ft space, halve physical damage, ignore Grappled, Restrained, Prone, and Bleeding, and resolve their ### Attacks as opposed Reflex saves. Each entry lists full damage and reduced damage once the swarm is at half HP or lower.
### Tactics
Piranha Swarms attack the most wounded swimmer first and stay on that target until it escapes the water or stops bleeding. They avoid armored creatures if an unarmored target is available, but their numbers make them dangerous around capsized boats and wrecks.

Rat Swarms pour through gaps, surround isolated targets, and retreat through cracks when exposed to fire or area effects. They are common aboard poorly maintained ships, in docks, and below warehouses.

## Name: Sea Monster
**Tier:** 5
**Origin:** Native waters (Monstrosity)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 210 (Con 7 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 5 (armored hide)
**Speed:** Swim 70 ft, Walk 20 ft
**Attributes:** Str 7 / Con 7 / Agil 7 / Dex 5 / Int 4 / Wis 6 / Cha 5 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Athletics 7, Perception 6, Stealth 4, Intimidate 5
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Tremorsense 60 ft in water
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Monstrosity
**Languages:** None
### Attacks
Maw [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 15 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 19 damage (12 + 7 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 19 damage, DR halved (round up); target is Grappled
Tail Sweep [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 7) against every creature within 15 feet behind the Sea Monster
- **Margin 1:**: 17 damage (10 + 7 Str); target is pushed 10 ft
- **Margin 2:** 17 damage, DR halved (round up); target is pushed 20 ft and knocked Prone
Multiattack [Combat]: The Sea Monster makes one Maw attack and one Tail Sweep attack.
Shared Attribute Roll (Agil 4): Roll Agility once and apply it to both ### Attacks.
### Special Abilities
Tidal Wake: When the Sea Monster moves at least 30 feet through water, each creature and unsecured object within 10 feet of its path must make a Reflex save or be pushed 10 feet and knocked Prone. A creature that succeeds is pushed 5 feet instead.
Ramming Bulk: If the Sea Monster moves at least 30 feet straight toward a vessel or structure, it deals 30 bludgeoning damage to the target and makes a Maw attack against one creature on or beside it.
Aquatic Body: The Sea Monster can breathe water indefinitely. On land, it moves at half speed and adds 1 to both attack dice.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Tactics
Sea Monsters attack ships as territory violations, striking the hull first and then targeting the crew that reaches the deck. They use Tidal Wake to scatter defenders before biting through the most isolated target. They do not retreat from a vessel they have claimed unless driven off by severe injury.

## Name: Shark, Reef
**Tier:** 1/2
**Origin:** Native waters (Beast)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 20 (Con 4 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 1 (rough hide)
**Speed:** Swim 50 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 4 / Agil 7 / Dex 6 / Int 2 / Wis 4 / Cha 2 / Luck 5

Blood Scent: The Reef Shark has Fortune on Perception checks to locate a creature that has taken damage in the water.
Feeding Frenzy: When an ally is adjacent to a wounded creature, the Reef Shark subtracts 1 from both attack dice against that creature.
Aquatic Body: The Reef Shark can breathe water indefinitely. On land, it moves at half speed and adds 1 to both attack dice.
### Tactics
Reef Sharks circle below swimmers and attack wounded targets first. They withdraw briefly when injured, then return when blood or movement draws them back. A lone shark avoids a full crew, but a pack can keep a vessel's survivors from reaching shore.

## Name: Strider
**Tier:** 1
**Origin:** Native desert (Beast)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 25 (Con 5 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 1 (shaggy hide)
**Speed:** 60 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 5 / Agil 7 / Dex 5 / Int 2 / Wis 5 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Athletics 4, Perception 4, Wilderness 4, Melee Combat 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 30 ft, Heat Sense
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Beast
**Languages:** None
### Attacks
Bite [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 10 damage (6 + 4 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up)
Rear Kick [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 5 ft; only against a creature behind the Strider
- **Margin 1:**: 12 damage (8 + 4 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up); target is pushed 5 ft
### Special Abilities
Heat Endurance: The Strider ignores ordinary penalties from extreme heat and can travel for a day on half the water required by a horse.
Long Stride: Difficult terrain made of sand costs the Strider only 1.5 times its movement instead of double movement.
Sure-Footed: The Strider has Fortune on Athletics checks made to cross dunes, scree, or narrow desert ledges.
### Description
Desert mount. A Strider is combat-trained enough to follow a rider's direction under fire and does not automatically bolt when injured, though extraordinary fear can still force a control check.
### Tactics
Striders carry desert nomads between distant water sources and can fight while encircled. They use their speed to break through a line rather than stand and trade ### Attacks.

## Name: Squid, Giant

Tentacle [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 20 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 16 damage (10 + 6 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 16 damage, DR halved (round up); target is Grappled
Beak [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 18 damage (12 + 6 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 18 damage, DR halved (round up)
Multiattack [Combat]: The Giant Squid makes four Tentacle ### Attacks against up to four targets. It cannot make a Beak attack during the same action.
Shared Attribute Roll (Agil 4): Roll Agility once and apply it to all four ### Attacks.
### Special Abilities
Ink Cloud (Recharge 5-8) [Combat]: The Giant Squid releases a cloud in a 30-foot radius. The area becomes heavily obscured for 1 minute, and the squid may immediately Hide without cover. The cloud disperses in strong current or after the squid leaves it.
Crushing Grip: A creature Grappled by a Tentacle takes 6 bludgeoning damage at the start of the squid's turn. A creature can escape with a Hard Athletics check.
Aquatic Body: The Giant Squid can breathe water indefinitely. On land, it moves at half speed and adds 1 to both attack dice.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Tactics
Giant Squids attack from below, opening with Ink Cloud before separating a vessel's crew. They grapple swimmers and drag them away from rescue, then use the Beak on anyone who closes. They flee if badly wounded unless defending a nest or wreck.

## Name: Welkislon
**Tier:** 5
**Origin:** Native (Beast)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 210 (Con 7 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 5 (thick hide)
**Speed:** 40 ft
**Attributes:** Str 8 / Con 7 / Agil 6 / Dex 5 / Int 4 / Wis 6 / Cha 5 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Might 8, Athletics 7, Perception 4
**Senses:** Keen Smell, Tremorsense 30 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Beast
**Languages:** None
### Attacks
Tusks [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 15 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 20 damage (12 + 8 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 20 damage, DR halved (round up); target is pushed 10 ft
Trunk Slam [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 10 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 16 damage (8 + 8 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 16 damage, DR halved (round up); target is Grappled
Stomp [Combat]: Melee attack against a Prone or Grappled creature in an adjacent space (Agil 3 + Melee Combat 6)
- **Margin 1:**: 20 damage (12 + 8 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 20 damage, DR halved (round up)
Multiattack [Combat]: The Wlkislon makes one Tusks attack and one Trunk Slam attack, or one Stomp attack against a qualifying target.
Shared Attribute Roll (Agil 3): Roll Agility once and apply it to both ### Attacks.
### Special Abilities
Powerful Build: The Wlkislon counts as Gargantuan for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting.
Trample (Recharge 5-8) [Combat]: The Wlkislon moves up to its speed in a straight line. Each creature in its path makes a Reflex save opposed by the Wlkislon's Str 8 + Athletics 7. On a loss, the creature takes 16 bludgeoning damage and is knocked Prone; on a success, it takes 8 damage and may move 5 feet aside. A creature can be targeted only once per Trample.
Memory of the Herd: The Wlkislon has Fortune on saves against Frightened while another Wlkislon is within 60 feet.
Siege Beast: The Wlkislon deals double damage to unattended structures, vehicles, and vessels.
Desription
The Welkislon is a siege-scale mount and working beast. It counts as Gargantuan for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting. A trained Wlkislon can carry a howdah or platform with several riders, but it acts on the rider or handler's initiative only when specially trained; otherwise it has its own turn and cannot be directed in combat by an untrained rider.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Tactics
Wlkislons protect their herd and handlers first. They use Trample to break formations, then seize isolated targets with the trunk and finish them with Stomps. A Wlkislon does not pursue beyond the safety of its herd unless enraged or directed by a trusted handler.

## Name: Wolf Pack Leader

Pack ### Tactics: When an ally is within 5 feet of the target, subtract 1 from both attack dice.
Keen Hearing and Smell: Sound and scent-based Perception checks have Fortune.

The pack leader coordinates wolf ### Attacks, using Pack ### Tactics and Coordinated Strike to bring down large prey. It targets spellcasters and archers first, attempting to knock them prone for the pack to tear apart.

## Name: Owlbear

**Size:** Large
**HP:** 30 (Con 6 × 5, Tough)

**Skills:** Melee Combat 5, Perception 5

**Creature Type:** Monstrosity (no special immunities)
### Attacks
Beak [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 5), Reach: 10 ft (Large creature)
- **Margin 1:**: 15 damage (10 + 5 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 15 damage, DR halved (round up)
Claws [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 5 vs opponent's defense), Reach: 10 ft (Large creature)
