Owlbears charge aggressively, attempting to trigger Aggressive Charge to knock prey prone. They use Multiattack every round, trying to trigger Maul for devastating damage. Wounded owlbears become frenzied and reckless.

## Name: Basilisk
**Tier:** 3
**Origin:** Native (Monstrosity)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 30 (Con 6 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 4 (thick scales, natural armor)
**Speed:** 20 ft
**Attributes:** Str 5 / Con 6 / Agil 6 / Dex 5 / Int 3 / Wis 6 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 5, Stealth 4, Perception 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Monstrosity (no special immunities)
### Attacks
Bite [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 5), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 13 damage (8 + 5 Str) + Poisoned
- **Margin 2:** 13 damage, DR halved (round up) + Poisoned
Poison Bite: On any hit, target becomes Poisoned. Duration: 1 hour OR until cured by Medicine [Hard] check or magical healing
### Special Abilities
Petrifying Gaze (Passive, at-will): When a creature that can see the basilisk's eyes starts its turn within 30 feet, the basilisk can force it to make a Fortitude [Easy] save:
0 successes: Begin turning to stone. Restrained. At end of affected creature's next turn, make another Fortitude save. On failure, turned to stone (Petrified).
### Tactics
Basilisks rely on their petrifying gaze to control the battlefield. They wait for enemies to fail saves and become Restrained, then close for poisonous bites. Against multiple foes, they position to catch as many in their gaze as possible. Smart enemies avert their eyes but suffer severe combat penalties.

## Name: Stone Golem
**Tier:** 5
**Origin:** Native (Construct)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 210 (Con 7 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 6 (stone body, natural armor)
**Speed:** 30 ft
**Attributes:** Str 8 / Con 7 / Agil 6 / Dex 6 / Int 3 / Wis 8 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Blindsight 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Construct (Immune to poison damage, Poisoned condition, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified. Mindless: immune to mind-affecting effects, fear, psychic effects. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.)
### Attacks
Slam [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 10 ft 
- **Margin 1:**: 20 damage (12 + 8 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 20 damage, DR halved (round up)
### Special Abilities
Large **Size:** Occupies 10×10 ft space, 10 ft reach.
Magic Resistance: Subtract 1 from both dice on saving throws against magical effects.
Slow (Recharge 6-8) [Combat]: Targets up to 3 creatures within 10 feet. Targets make Will [Easy] save adding 1 to both Will save dice:
0 Successes: Can only take 2 actions per turn (instead of 3) and cannot take reactions for 1 minute. Affected creatures can repeat save at end of each turn.
Immutable Form: Immune to any weave that would alter its form (transformation, Beastial Transformation, etc.)
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Tactics
Stone golems advance inexorably, using Slow to cripple multiple enemies before crushing them with massive slams. They ignore most damage types and magic, making them terrifying juggernauts. Golems follow orders literally and never retreat.

## Name: Whispervane
**Tier:** 1/8
**Origin:** Elemental (Air)
**Size:** Tiny
**HP:** 6 (Con 2 × 3, Fragile)
**DR:** 0
**Speed:** 0 ft, Fly 40 ft (Hover)
**Attributes:** Str 1 / Con 2 / Agil 6 / Dex 3 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 1, Perception 3, Stealth 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Blindsight 10 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Elemental; Immune to lightning damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Gust [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 1), Reach: 5 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 2 damage (1 + 1 Str); target loses any unsecured held item lighter than 1 lb.
- **Margin 2:** 2 damage, DR halved (round up); target's next ranged attack adds 1 to one die of their choice from wind interference.
### Special Abilities
Wind Whisper: The Whispervane carries a spoken message of up to 25 words to any creature within 300 feet it can perceive. Message delivered as a whisper only the target can hear. Transit time is 1 round. It executes this without fail.
Near-Invisible: Perception checks to spot the Whispervane by sight alone add 2 to both dice except in strong light or dusty conditions.
### Description
A Whispervane is a messenger and a mild nuisance. It has no meaningful combat presence. A summoner who calls one for a fight has misread what they summoned. Called to deliver a message without being seen, locate a specific sound within a building, or harass an archer's concentration, it performs reliably. Its intelligence is sufficient to understand a destination and a recipient — not sufficient to improvise if circumstances change.

## Name: Dust Devil
**Tier:** 1/8
**Origin:** Elemental (air)
**Size:** Small
**HP:** 8 (Con 2 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** 0
**Speed:** 30 ft, Fly 40 ft (Hover)
**Attributes:** Str 2 / Con 2 / Agil 6 / Dex 3 / Int 3 / Wis 4 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 1, Perception 2
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Elemental (immune to poison, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious; immune to lightning damage; does not need air, food, drink, or sleep)
### Attacks
Buffet [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 1), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 5 damage (3 + 2 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 5 damage, DR halved (round up)
### Special Abilities
Whirlwind Form: The Dust Devil can pass through spaces as narrow as 1 inch without squeezing. Creatures attempting to grab or grapple it add 1 to both dice.
Sand Scatter: Once per turn as a [Free] action, the Dust Devil can kick up grit and debris in a 5-foot diameter. Creatures in range make a Reflex [Easy] save or gain the Dazzled condition until the start of their next turn.
### Tactics
Dust Devils harass and distract. They position to use Sand Scatter on spellcasters or archers, then slip away before retaliation. A Friendly Dust Devil takes these instructions naturally; an Indifferent one drifts toward whoever seems most interesting.

## Name: Gust Sprite
**Tier:** 1/4
**Origin:** Elemental (Air)
**Size:** Small
**HP:** 9 (Con 3 × 3, Fragile)
**DR:** 0
**Speed:** 0 ft, Fly 50 ft (Hover)
**Attributes:** Str 2 / Con 3 / Agil 6 / Dex 5 / Int 3 / Wis 4 / Cha 2 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 2, Perception 3, Stealth 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Blindsight 10 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Elemental; Immune to lightning damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Wind Slash [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 2), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 5 damage (3 + 2 Str).
- **Margin 2:** 5 damage, DR halved (round up); target's next ranged attack adds 1 to both dice from wind disruption.
- **Margin 3:** 5 damage, DR halved (round up); target knocked Prone from a sudden concentrated gust.
### Special Abilities
Air Cushion: The Gust Sprite surrounds one adjacent creature with a pocket of calm air. That creature has Fortune on Reflex saves against area effects until the start of the Sprite's next turn. No action cost.
Speed Burst (1/short rest): Fly speed increases to 80 ft until the end of its next turn.
### Description
Gust Sprites dart between reach distances, disrupt ranged attackers, and occasionally knock someone flat with a well-timed concentrated blast. They are fragile enough that a determined opponent can end them, fast enough that catching them requires commitment. A Cooperative Gust Sprite uses Air Cushion to protect whoever the summoner indicates between its own ### Attacks — a small but genuine defensive contribution at low tiers.

## Name: Cyclone
**Tier:** 1/2
**Origin:** Elemental (Air)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 20 (Con 4 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 1 (compressed air shell)
**Speed:** 0 ft, Fly 60 ft (Hover)
**Attributes:** Str 3 / Con 4 / Agil 7 / Dex 5 / Int 3 / Wis 4 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 2, Perception 4, Stealth 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Blindsight 20 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Elemental; Immune to lightning damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Wind Strike [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 2), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 7 damage (4 + 3 Str); target makes Fortitude [Easy] save or pushed 5 feet.
- **Margin 2:** 7 damage, DR halved (round up); target pushed 10 feet; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 7 damage, DR halved (round up); target pushed 10 feet and knocked Prone.
### Special Abilities
Whirlwind Form: Can move through spaces as narrow as 1 inch. Grapple attempts against it add 1 to both dice.
Buffeting Winds: Creatures ending their turn within 10 feet make a Fortitude [Easy] save or are pushed 5 feet directly away.
Momentum Defense: While the Cyclone has moved at least half its fly speed this turn, ### Attacks against it add 1 to both attack dice.
### Description
Cyclones control space by making it uncomfortable to occupy. Buffeting Winds keeps melee fighters pushed back while Wind Strike punishes anyone who closes. Against spellcasters and archers they use their speed to negate distance. The Momentum Defense makes them genuinely difficult to hit while moving, so they rarely stop moving. A summoner who needs a chokepoint held by something that cannot itself be easily held will find them useful.

## Name: Gale Specter
**Tier:** 1
**Origin:** Elemental (Air)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 20 (Con 5 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** 0
**Speed:** 20 ft, Fly 60 ft (Hover)
**Attributes:** Str 3 / Con 5 / Agil 8 / Dex 5 / Int 3 / Wis 4 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 4, Ranged Combat 3, Perception 4
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Blindsight 30 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Elemental (immune to poison, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious; immune to lightning damage; does not need air, food, drink, or sleep)
### Attacks
Wind Strike [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 4), Reach: 10 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 9 damage (6 + 3 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 9 damage, DR halved (round up); target makes Fortitude save (1 success) or is pushed 10 feet
- **Margin 3:** 9 damage, DR halved (round up); target pushed 10 feet and knocked Prone (no save)
Lightning Arc [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 5 + Ranged Combat 3), Range: 60 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 12 lightning damage (7 + 5 Dex)
- **Margin 2:** 12 lightning damage, DR halved (round up)
### Special Abilities
Storm Body: The Gale Specter is difficult to hold. Grapple attempts add 1 to both dice. If successfully grappled, it makes a Fortitude [Easy] save at the start of its turn to break free automatically.
Momentum Defense: While it has moved at least half its fly speed this turn, ### Attacks against it add 1 to both attack dice.
### Tactics
Gale Specters use their fly speed aggressively, staying mobile to maintain Momentum Defense. They use Wind Strike to push melee fighters away from protected targets and Lightning Arc to pressure at range. A Cooperative Gale Specter will follow target priority; an Indifferent one picks whoever seems most challenging.

## Name: Stormcall
**Tier:** 1
**Origin:** Elemental (Air)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 20 (Con 5 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** 2 (compressed air shell)
**Speed:** 0 ft, Fly 70 ft (Hover)
**Attributes:** Str 3 / Con 5 / Agil 8 / Dex 5 / Int 3 / Wis 4 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 4, Ranged Combat 3, Perception 5
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Blindsight 30 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Elemental; Immune to lightning damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Wind Blade [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 4), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 9 damage (6 + 3 Str); target pushed 10 feet.
- **Margin 2:** 9 damage, DR halved (round up); target pushed 10 feet and knocked Prone; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 9 damage, DR halved (round up) + Prone + Stunned until end of their next turn from a concentrated wind impact.
Lightning Arc [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 5 + Ranged Combat 3), Range: 60 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 12 lightning damage (7 + 5 Dex).
- **Margin 2:** 12 lightning damage, DR halved (round up); target gains Dazed condition until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 3:** 12 lightning damage, DR halved (round up) + Dazed + Stunned until end of their next turn.
### Special Abilities
Momentum Defense: While the Stormcall has moved at least half its fly speed this turn, ### Attacks against it add 1 to both attack dice.
Storm Body: Grapple attempts add 1 to both dice. If successfully grappled, the Stormcall makes a Fortitude [Easy] save at the start of its turn to break free automatically.
### Description
Stormcalls fight from altitude, opening with Wind Blade against the closest threat, then spending a second action on Lightning Arc against anything at range, and using a third action to reposition while maintaining Momentum Defense. The MAP on that third action is real but the Stormcall accepts it — staying in motion matters more than clean dice. Agil 7 means they act before most of what they face. A summoner who gives a Stormcall a target designation and stays out of the way will find it requires little management.

## Name: Tempest
**Tier:** 2
**Origin:** Elemental (Air)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 30 (Con 5 × 6, Massive)
**DR:** 3 (gale-force shell)
**Speed:** 0 ft, Fly 80 ft (Hover)
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 5 / Agil 8 / Dex 5 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 3, Ranged Combat 2, Perception 6
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Blindsight 30 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Elemental; Immune to lightning damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Gale Strike [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (10 + 4 Str); target pushed 15 feet.
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up); target pushed 15 feet and knocked Prone; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up) + Prone + Stunned until end of their next turn; all creatures within 5 feet of target when they land take 4 bludgeoning damage.
Chain Lightning [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 5 + Ranged Combat 2), Range: 120 ft, up to 3 targets within 30 feet of each other.
- **Margin 1:**: 14 lightning damage (9 + 5 Dex).
- **Margin 2:** 14 lightning damage, DR halved (round up) + Stunned until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 3:** 14 lightning damage, DR halved (round up) + Stunned + Dazed for 1 minute.
### Special Abilities
Vortex Presence: Creatures starting their turn within 15 feet make a Fortitude [Easy] save or are pulled 10 feet directly toward the Tempest. Flying creatures add 1 to both their save dice.
Momentum Defense: While the Tempest has moved at least half its fly speed this turn, ### Attacks against it add 1 to both attack dice.
Storm Body: Grapple attempts add 1 to both dice. If successfully grappled, the Tempest makes a Fortitude [Easy] save at the start of its turn to break free automatically.
### Description
Tempests open against grouped enemies with Chain Lightning (2 actions), then spend the third on Gale Strike against the nearest pulled-in threat. On subsequent turns they split between two Gale Strikes and position, accepting the MAP on the third action to maintain Momentum Defense. Vortex Presence does the work of bringing enemies into range; the Tempest handles what arrives. A summoner deploying one near structures should be aware that Vortex Presence does not distinguish between enemies it is dragging in and unsecured objects it is also dragging in.

## Name: Elder Gale
**Tier:** 3
**Origin:** Elemental (Air)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 4 (ancient storm-shell)
**Speed:** 0 ft, Fly 100 ft (Hover)
**Attributes:** Str 5 / Con 6 / Agil 8 / Dex 4 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 6, Ranged Combat 5, Perception 7
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Blindsight 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Elemental; Immune to lightning damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Thunderstrike [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 6), Reach: 15 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 12 damage (7 + 5 Str) + 4 lightning damage (Half DR); target pushed 15 feet.
- **Margin 2:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 lightning damage; pushed 15 feet + Stunned until end of their next turn; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 lightning damage + Stunned; target also Deafened for 1 minute.
Storm Surge [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 4 + Ranged Combat 5), 60-foot line, 5 feet wide.
- **Margin 1:**: 16 lightning damage (12 + 4 Dex).
- **Margin 2:** 16 lightning damage, DR halved (round up) + Stunned until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 3:** 16 lightning damage, DR halved (round up) + Stunned + knocked Prone.
### Special Abilities
Eye of the Storm: The Elder Gale designates up to 6 creatures within 30 feet as protected. Protected creatures are immune to Vortex Presence and cannot be pushed or knocked Prone by any of the Elder Gale's ### Attacks or abilities. Designation changes as a [Free] action.
Vortex Presence: Creatures starting their turn within 30 feet make a Fortitude [Hard] save or are pulled 10 feet directly toward the Tempest. Flying creatures add 1 to both their save dice.
Momentum Defense: While the Elder Gale has moved at least half its fly speed this turn, ### Attacks against it add 1 to both attack dice.
Storm Body: Grapple attempts add 1 to both dice. If successfully grappled, the Elder Gale makes a Fortitude [Easy] save at the start of its turn to break free automatically.
Resilient Defense (2/day): When the Elder Gale fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
Elder Gales are the first Air Elemental that actively protects allies rather than simply fighting. Eye of the Storm ensures the summoner and designated companions are never accidentally caught in Vortex Presence or thrown by Thunderstrike's pushback — a meaningful distinction at a tier where battlefield chaos becomes genuinely dangerous. A typical turn spends two actions on Thunderstrike against priority targets and a third on Storm Surge when a line of enemies presents itself, or repositioning to maintain Momentum Defense. Agil 8 means the Elder Gale acts before virtually everything it faces.

## Name: Storm Sovereign
**Tier:** 4
**Origin:** Elemental (Air)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 5 (primordial storm-shell)
**Speed:** 0 ft, Fly 120 ft (Hover)
**Attributes:** Str 6 / Con 6 / Agil 8 / Dex 3 / Int 6 / Wis 6 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Ranged Combat 6, Perception 8, Intimidate 6
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Blindsight 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Elemental; Immune to lightning damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Gale Fist [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 15 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (8 + 6 Str) + 4 lightning damage (Half DR); target pushed 20 feet and knocked Prone; no save.
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 lightning damage; Prone + Stunned until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 3:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 lightning damage + Stunned; all creatures within 10 feet of target take 8 lightning damage from the discharge.
Tempest Bolt [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 6), Range: 180 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 18 lightning damage (15 + 3 Dex) + Dazed until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 2:** 18 lightning damage, DR halved (round up) + Stunned + Dazed for 1 minute.
- **Margin 3:** 18 lightning damage, DR halved (round up) + Stunned + Dazed + Deafened for 1 minute; Fortitude [Hard] save to reduce Deafened to 1 round only.
### Special Abilities
Storm Sovereign Aura: Creatures within 20 feet that are not designated must make a Fortitude [Hard] save at the start of their turn. Failure: pushed 15 feet and Deafened for 1 round. The summoner and designated allies are immune.
Lightning Immunity Sharing: The summoner and up to 4 designated allies gain immunity to non-magical lightning damage while within 60 feet.
Eye of the Storm: The Storm Sovereign designates up to 6 creatures within 30 feet as protected. Protected creatures are immune to Storm Sovereign Aura and cannot be pushed or knocked Prone by any of the Storm Sovereign’s ### Attacks or abilities. Designation changes as a [Free] action.
Momentum Defense: While the Storm Sovereign has moved at least half its fly speed this turn, ### Attacks against it add 1 to both attack dice.
Resilient Defense (3/day): When the Storm Sovereign fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
A Storm Sovereign's Aura displaces enemies constantly. A typical turn spends two actions on Gale Fist against whoever Storm Sovereign Aura failed to clear and a third on Tempest Bolt against anything trying to engage from range — three actions, the third at MAP but still at Ranged Combat 7 and Agil 8, which makes that penalty manageable. Lightning Immunity Sharing is a notable battlefield benefit — a summoner who builds their party around a Storm Sovereign can deny an entire damage type to grouped enemies without any additional resource cost. Clear target priority is the only instruction it strictly requires; the Sovereign determines execution from there.

## Name: Elder Stormcaller
**Tier:** 4
**Origin:** Elemental (Air)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 3 (diffuse air body)
**Speed:** 20 ft, Fly 80 ft (Hover)
**Attributes:** Str 5 / Con 6 / Agil 8 / Dex 6 / Int 4 / Wis 5 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Ranged Combat 6, Perception 6
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Blindsight 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Elemental (immune to poison, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious; immune to lightning damage; does not need air, food, drink, or sleep)
### Attacks
Wind Blade [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 15 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (9 + 5 Str) + 4 lightning damage.
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 lightning damage; target pushed 15 feet.
- **Margin 3:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 lightning damage; target pushed 15 feet and Stunned until end of their next turn.
Chain Lightning [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 6 + Ranged Combat 6), Range: 120 ft, up to 4 targets. Targets must be within 30 ft of each other
- **Margin 1:**: 18 lightning damage (12 + 6 Dex).
- **Margin 2:** 18 lightning damage, DR halved (round up).
- **Margin 3:** 18 lightning damage, DR halved (round up) + Stunned until end of their next turn.
### Special Abilities
Storm Presence: When the Elder Stormcaller takes its turn, all creatures within 30 feet must make a Fortitude [Easy] save or have their movement speed halved until the start of their next turn.
Momentum Defense: While it has moved at least half its fly speed this turn, ### Attacks against it add 1 to both attack dice.
Eye of the Storm: Allies the Stormcaller designates are immune to Storm Presence's effects.
Lightning Immunity Aura: Creatures the Stormcaller designates as protected gain resistance to lightning damage (+4 DR) while within 30 feet.
Resilient Defense (2/day): When the Stormcaller fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Tactics
Elder Stormcallers command battlefields from altitude, using Storm Presence to slow enemy movement while delivering Chain Lightning to clustered targets. They descend for Wind Blade strikes when softening up isolated threats. A Friendly Elder Stormcaller is a genuine tactical partner; even an Indifferent one tends to find the most dramatic target and hammer it.

## Name: Voidgale
**Tier:** 5
**Origin:** Elemental (Air)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 210 (Con 7 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 6 (world-storm shell)
**Speed:** 0 ft, Fly 140 ft (Hover)
**Attributes:** Str 6 / Con 7 / Agil 8 / Dex 3 / Int 5 / Wis 6 / Cha 6 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Ranged Combat 6, Perception 8, Intimidate 7
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Blindsight 120 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Elemental; Immune to lightning damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
World Wind [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 20 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 15 damage (9 + 6 Str) + 5 lightning damage (Half DR); target pushed 30 feet and knocked Prone; no save.
- **Margin 2:** 15 damage, DR halved (round up) + 5 lightning damage; Prone + Stunned; all creatures within 15 feet of target take 10 lightning damage from discharge.
- **Margin 3:** 15 damage, DR halved (round up) + 5 lightning damage + Stunned + Blinded until end of their next turn from the lightning discharge at face range.
Skyfire [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 6), Range: 200 ft, 20-foot diameter.
- **Margin 1:**: 20 lightning damage (17 + 3 Dex).
- **Margin 2:** 20 lightning damage, DR halved (round up) + Stunned.
- **Margin 3:** 20 lightning damage, DR halved (round up) + Stunned + knocked Prone; no save.
### Special Abilities
Primordial Vortex: Creatures within 30 feet that are not designated must make Fortitude [Hard]  saves at the start of their turn. Failure: pushed 20 feet, knocked Prone, and Deafened for 1 minute. The summoner and designated allies are immune.
Cyclone Manifestation (1/day) [Combat]: As 2 [Combat] actions, the Void Gale establishes a sustained cyclone in a 40-foot diameter centered on itself. For 1 minute, all non-designated creatures treat the area as Difficult Terrain, must make Fortitude [Hard] saves each round or become Restrained by wind. Ranged ### Attacks into or through the area add 2 to both dice. The Void Gale moves normally through its own cyclone.
Lightning Immunity Sharing: The summoner and up to 8 designated allies gain immunity to non-magical lightning damage while within 60 feet.
Eye of the Storm: The Void Gale designates up to 6 creatures within 30 feet as protected. Protected creatures are immune to Primordial Vortex and cannot be pushed or knocked Prone by any of the Void Gale’s ### Attacks or abilities. Designation changes as a [Free] action.
Momentum Defense: While the Void Gale has moved at least half its fly speed this turn, ### Attacks against it add 1 to both attack dice.
Resilient Defense (3/day): When the Void Gale fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
Void Gales open with Cyclone Manifestation (2 actions) to dominate terrain, then spend the third action on World Wind against the summoner's designated priority target. Subsequent turns run two World Wind strikes against priority targets and a third on Skyfire when enemies cluster — or reverse that order if a formation presents itself first. Primordial Vortex handles anyone attempting to hold ground between turns. A Void Gale does not need tactical guidance — it has existed long enough that it understands what victory requires in most situations and moves toward it. What it needs from the summoner is target designation, ally identification, and confirmation of the rules of engagement. Everything else it handles.


## Name: Gale Kin
**Tier:** 1/2
**Origin:** Nephilim (Air)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 20 (Con 4 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 2 (wind-compressed skin)
**Speed:** 40 ft, Fly 30 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 4 / Agil 7 / Dex 4 / Int 3 / Wis 4 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 2, Perception 4, Athletics 3, Intimidate 2
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Nephilim (Giant); Immune to lightning damage. Resistance to cold damage (+2 DR). Powerful Build. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Wind Strike [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 2), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 10 damage (6 + 4 Str); target pushed 5 feet.
- **Margin 2:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up); target pushed 10 feet and knocked Prone; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up) + Prone + Stunned until end of their next turn.
Gust Hurl [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 4 + Ranged Combat 1), Range: 40 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 10 damage (6 + 4 Dex); target makes Fortitude save (1 success) or pushed 5 feet.
- **Margin 2:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up); target pushed 10 feet; no save; target knocked Prone.
### Special Abilities
Wind Aura: Ranged ### Attacks passing within 10 feet of the Gale Kin add 1 to one die of the attacker's choice from wind interference.
Powerful Build: The Gale Kin counts as Huge for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting.
### Description
Gale Kin are mobile and hit harder than their Strength alone suggests because their strikes carry wind force behind them. The pushback on Wind Strike makes positioning enemies against terrain a viable tactic even at this tier. Wind Aura provides passive protection against ranged attackers. A summoner who uses terrain intelligently — placing the Gale Kin between enemies and a cliff edge, a wall, or a body of water — will find the pushback mechanics considerably more punishing than they appear in flat terrain.

## Name: Stormborn
**Tier:** 1
**Origin:** Nephilim (Air)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 25 (Con 5 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 3 (storm-compressed skin)
**Speed:** 40 ft, Fly 50 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 5 / Agil 8 / Dex 3 / Int 3 / Wis 4 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 3, Perception 5, Athletics 4, Intimidate 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Nephilim (Giant); Immune to lightning damage. Resistance to cold damage (+2 DR). Powerful Build. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Thunder Blow [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 9 damage (5 + 4 Str) + 3 lightning damage (Half DR); target pushed 10 feet.
- **Margin 2:** 9 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 lightning damage; pushed 10 feet + Stunned until end of their next turn; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 9 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 lightning damage + Stunned + Deafened for 1 minute from the thunderclap at impact.
Lightning Throw [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 2), Range: 60 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 11 lightning damage (8 + 3 Dex); target gains Dazed until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 2:** 11 lightning damage, DR halved (round up); target Stunned until end of their next turn; no save.
### Special Abilities
Wind Aura: Ranged ### Attacks passing within 15 feet of the Stormborn add 1 to one die of the attacker's choice from wind interference.
Powerful Build: The Stormborn counts as Huge for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting.
Momentum: When the Stormborn moves at least 20 feet before making a melee attack, subtract 1 from both attack dice on that attack.
### Description
Stormborn are fast and hit with enough force to stun reliably at Margin 2. The combination of Momentum and their fly speed means they can build approach distance quickly and deliver Thunder Blow at subtract-1 to both dice consistently. A summoner who gives one space to maneuver will find it applies Stunned more frequently than the raw numbers suggest — the action economy value of reliably stunning a target for a round is significant at this tier.

## Name: Thunder Giant
**Tier:** 2
**Origin:** Nephilim (Air)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 150 (Con 5 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 4 (storm-dense flesh)
**Speed:** 40 ft, Fly 60 ft
**Attributes:** Str 5 / Con 5 / Agil 8 / Dex 3 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 5, Perception 5, Athletics 5, Intimidate 5
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Nephilim (Giant); Immune to lightning damage. Resistance to cold damage (+2 DR). Powerful Build. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Storm Fist [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 5), Reach: 15 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 11 damage (6 + 5 Str) + 3 lightning damage (Half DR); target pushed 15 feet.
- **Margin 2:** 11 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 lightning damage; pushed 15 feet + Stunned until end of their next turn; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 11 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 lightning damage + Stunned + Deafened for 1 minute; all creatures within 10 feet of target take 4 lightning damage from the discharge.
Chain Bolt [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 4), Range: 80 ft, up to 2 additional targets within 20 feet of the first.
- **Margin 1:**: 13 lightning damage (10 + 3 Dex).
- **Margin 2:** 13 lightning damage, DR halved (round up) + Dazed until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 3:** 13 lightning damage, DR halved (round up) + Stunned until end of their next turn.
### Special Abilities
Storm Aura: Ranged ### Attacks passing within 15 feet of the Thunder Giant add 1 to both dice from severe wind interference. Creatures starting their turn within 10 feet make a Fortitude [Easy] save or are pushed 5 feet directly away.
Powerful Build: The Thunder Giant counts as Gargantuan for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting.
Momentum: When the Thunder Giant moves at least 20 feet before making a melee attack, subtract 1 from both attack dice on that attack
Resilient Defense (1/day): When the Thunder Giant fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
Thunder Giants hit hard enough that their pushback mechanics start repositioning groups rather than individuals. Chain Bolt's secondary targeting means they can apply Dazed or Stunned to clustered enemies with a single ranged action. Storm Aura's wind interference makes them naturally difficult for archers to engage while the storm presence pushes melee fighters back. A summoner who positions one between enemies and open ground will find the battlefield steadily pushed in a direction that favors the summoner's allies.

## Name: Tempest Lord
**Tier:** 3
**Origin:** Nephilim (Air)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 5 (ancient storm-flesh)
**Speed:** 45 ft, Fly 80 ft
**Attributes:** Str 5 / Con 6 / Agil 8 / Dex 4 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 6, Perception 6, Athletics 6, Intimidate 6
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Nephilim (Giant); Immune to lightning damage. Resistance to cold damage (+2 DR). Powerful Build. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Tempest Blow [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 6), Reach: 15 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 12 damage (7 + 5 Str) + 4 lightning damage (Half DR); target pushed 20 feet.
- **Margin 2:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 lightning damage; pushed 20 feet + Stunned until end of their next turn + knocked Prone; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 lightning damage + Stunned + Prone; all creatures within 15 feet of target take 8 lightning damage and are pushed 10 feet from the shockwave.
Lightning Lance [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 4 + Ranged Combat 5), Range: 100 ft, 5-foot-wide line.
- **Margin 1:**: 16 lightning damage (12 + 4 Dex) + Dazed.
- **Margin 2:** 16 lightning damage, DR halved (round up) + Stunned until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 3:** 16 lightning damage, DR halved (round up) + Stunned + Deafened for 1 minute.
### Special Abilities
Vortex Aura: Creatures starting their turn within 20 feet make a Fortitude [Easy] save or pushed 10 feet directly away. Ranged ### Attacks passing through the aura area add 1 to both dice.
Powerful Build: The Tempest Lord counts as Gargantuan for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting.
Momentum: When the Tempest Lord moves at least 20 feet before making a melee attack, subtract 1 from both attack dice on that attack
Intimidating Presence: As a [Combat] action all creatures within 40 feet that can hear it make a Will [Hard] save. On failure: target gains the Frightened condition for 1 minute. Creatures immune to fear are unaffected.
Resilient Defense (2/day): When the Tempest Lord fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
Tempest Lords are authority figures among Air Nephilim — they communicate in complete sentences, evaluate the battlefield intelligently, and will offer tactical observations whether asked or not. The Vortex Aura keeps enemies constantly displaced, Lightning Lance clears lines, and Tempest Blow at Margin 2 sends targets flying far enough to take falling damage against walls or off ledges. A summoner who briefs one on the full situation rather than just issuing target instructions will find it performs significantly better — Tempest Lords do things with context that they cannot do without it.

## Name: Tempest Giant
**Tier:** 3
**Origin:** Deamon (Nephilim)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 30 (Con 6 × 5, Tough)

**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Blindsight 30 ft

**Creature Type:** Nephilim (Huge size, Powerful Build; immune to lightning damage; does not need air, food, drink, or sleep)
### Attacks
Wind-Forged Greatclub [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 5), Reach: 15 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 12 damage (7 + 5 Str) + 4 lightning damage (Half DR)
- **Margin 2:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 lightning damage; target is pushed 15 feet
- **Margin 3:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 lightning damage; target pushed 15 feet and knocked Prone
Lightning Bolt [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 4 + Ranged Combat 4), Range: 120 ft, 5-foot-wide line
- **Margin 1:**: 16 lightning damage (12 + 4 Dex)
- **Margin 2:** 16 lightning damage, DR halved (round up)
### Special Abilities
 Storm Step (Recharge 6-8): As a [Move] action, the Tempest Giant teleports up to 60 feet and arrives in a crack of thunder. All creatures within 10 feet of its arrival point make a Fortitude save (1 success) or be knocked Prone.
 Powerful Build. Counts as Gigantic for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting.
Wind Wall (1/day): As 2 [Combat] actions, create a 60-foot-long, 20-foot-high wall of roaring wind that persists for 1 minute. Ranged ### Attacks through the wall add 2 to both dice. Small and Tiny creatures cannot pass through it. The Tempest Giant can dismiss it as a [Free] action.

Tempest Giants open with Lightning Bolt to soften clustered enemies, then close to melee using Storm Step to arrive at the perfect angle.

## Name: Storm Titan
**Tier:** 4
**Origin:** Nephilim (Air)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 6 (primordial storm-flesh)
**Speed:** 50 ft, Fly 100 ft
**Attributes:** Str 6 / Con 6 / Agil 8 / Dex 3 / Int 5 / Wis 5 / Cha 5 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Perception 7, Athletics 7, Intimidate 7
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Nephilim (Giant); Immune to lightning damage. Resistance to cold damage (+2 DR). Powerful Build. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
World Storm Fist [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 15 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (8 + 6 Str) + 4 lightning damage (Half DR); target pushed 20 feet + Prone; no save.
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 lightning damage; pushed 20 feet + Prone + Stunned until end of their next turn; all creatures within 15 feet of target take 10 lightning damage from discharge.
- **Margin 3:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 lightning damage + Prone + Stunned; area discharge increases to 16 lightning damage; all affected creatures also Deafened for 1 minute.
Skyfire Bolt [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 6), Range: 150 ft, 15-foot diameter.
- **Margin 1:**: 18 lightning damage (15 + 3 Dex) + Dazed.
- **Margin 2:** 18 lightning damage, DR halved (round up) + Stunned until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 3:** 18 lightning damage, DR halved (round up) + Stunned + Prone; no save.
### Special Abilities
Cyclone Aura: Creatures starting their turn within 20 feet make a Fortitude [Hard] save or pushed 20 feet directly away and knocked Prone. Ranged ### Attacks through the aura add 2 to both dice.
Lightning Immunity Sharing: The summoner and up to 3 designated allies gain immunity to non-magical lightning damage within 60 feet.
Powerful Build: The Storm Titan counts as Gargantuan for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting.
Momentum: When the Storm Titan moves at least 20 feet before making a melee attack, subtract 1 from both attack dice on that attack
Intimidating Presence: As a [Combat] action all creatures within 40 feet that can hear it make a Will [Hard] save. On failure: target gains the Frightened condition for 1 minute. Creatures immune to fear are unaffected.
Resilient Defense (3/day): When the Storm Titan fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
Storm Titans are Agil 8 — they act before almost everything they face and maintain that advantage through their entire engagement. Cyclone Aura at Hard task difficulty continuously displaces enemies regardless of how many actions they spend trying to hold position. Skyfire Bolt handles grouped formations at 150 feet. World Storm Fist's discharge damage at Margin 2 and 3 means the area around the primary target takes significant lightning damage as well. A summoner who keeps clear of the Cyclone Aura radius and lets the Storm Titan work will find most engagements resolved decisively and quickly.

## Name: Void Tempest
**Tier:** 5
**Origin:** Nephilim (Air)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 210 (Con 7 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 7 (world-storm flesh)
**Speed:** 55 ft, Fly 120 ft
**Attributes:** Str 7 / Con 7 / Agil 8 / Dex 3 / Int 5 / Wis 5 / Cha 6 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Perception 8, Athletics 8, Intimidate 8
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Blindsight 60 ft (air pressure)
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Nephilim (Giant); Immune to lightning damage. Resistance to cold damage (+2 DR). Powerful Build. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Worldstorm Strike [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 20 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 15 damage (8 + 7 Str) + 5 lightning damage (Half DR); target pushed 30 feet + Prone; no save.
- **Margin 2:** 15 damage, DR halved (round up) + 5 lightning damage; pushed 30 feet + Prone + Stunned; all creatures within 20 feet of target take 12 lightning damage + Dazed from the discharge.
- **Margin 3:** 15 damage, DR halved (round up) + 5 lightning damage + Prone + Stunned; discharge area takes 20 lightning damage + Stunned; no save on area.
Heaven's Bolt [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 6), Range: 200 ft, 20-foot diameter.
- **Margin 1:**: 20 lightning damage (17 + 3 Dex) + Stunned until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 2:** 20 lightning damage, DR halved (round up) + Stunned + Deafened for 1 minute.
- **Margin 3:** 20 lightning damage, DR halved (round up) + Stunned + Deafened + knocked Prone; no save.
### Special Abilities
World Vortex Aura: Creatures within 30 feet that are not designated make a Fortitude [Hard] save at the start of their turn. Failure: pushed 30 feet, knocked Prone, and Deafened for 1 minute. 1 success: pushed 15 feet only.
Powerful Build: The Void Tempest counts as Gargantuan for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting.
World-Storm Presence (1/day) [Combat]: As 2 [Combat] actions, the Void Tempest manifests its full elemental nature in an 80-foot diameter. All non-designated creatures make a Reflex [Hard] save .
- **Margin 1:**: 12 damage only. Buildings and structures take structural damage.
- **Margin 2:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + Stunned until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 3:** 12 lightning damage, DR halved (round up) + Stunned for 1 minute + Deafened permanently (Fortitude save, 2 successes, to reduce to 1 hour).
Lightning Immunity Sharing: The summoner and up to 6 designated allies gain immunity to non-magical lightning damage within 60 feet.
Intimidating Presence: As a [Combat] action all creatures within 40 feet that can hear it make a Will [Hard] save. On failure: target gains the Frightened condition for 1 minute. Creatures immune to fear are unaffected.
Resilient Defense (3/day): When the Void Tempest fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
A Void Tempest is an apex Air Nephilim that has existed long enough to understand mortal warfare at a strategic rather than tactical level. World-Storm Presence on arrival; World Vortex Aura maintaining continuous battlefield control; Heaven's Bolt suppressing enemy formations at range while Worldstorm Strike removes priority targets. It will communicate its assessment of the engagement openly and expects the summoner to engage with that assessment rather than simply repeat instructions. A summoner who treats a Void Tempest as a tool will find it technically compliant. One who treats it as a partner will find it does things with that relationship that compliance alone cannot produce.

## Name: Court Noble
**Tier:** 4
**Origin:** Fae (Air)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 4 (fae resilience, accumulated centuries)
**Speed:** 40 ft, Fly 40 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 6 / Agil 5 / Dex 4 / Int 6 / Wis 6 / Cha 7 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Perception 7, Deception 7, Persuasion 7, Intimidate 6, Arcane 6, Empathy 7
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Low-Light Vision, Perfect Sight 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Fey; Fortune on saves vs charm effects. Immune to mind-affecting effects from non-Fae sources. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Fae Court Blade [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 5 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 12 damage (8 + 4 Str) + 4 psychic damage (Ignore DR).
- **Margin 2:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 psychic damage; target Charmed for 1 minute treating the Court Noble as a trusted acquaintance, cannot attack it; Will [Hard] save to resist.
- **Margin 3:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 psychic damage + Charmed; no save; Charmed target also cannot target the Court Noble's designated allies with ### Attacks or harmful abilities for the duration.
Glamour Bolt [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 4 + Ranged Combat 6), Range: 60 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 16 psychic damage (12 + 4 Dex); target Disoriented until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 2:** 16 psychic damage, DR halved (round up) + Dazed for 1 minute; Will [Easy]save each round to end early.
- **Margin 3:** 16 psychic damage, DR halved (round up) + Charmed for 1 minute; no save.
### Special Abilities
Fae Authority: The Court Noble's words carry weight that transcends ordinary persuasion. When it speaks to a creature directly and that creature fails a Will save (opposed by Cha 8 + Persuasion 7), the creature follows one reasonable instruction as if it had received a Command weave. "Reasonable" is defined by the Court Noble, whose standards differ significantly from mortal ones.
Ancient Compact: The Court Noble is bound by Fae law to honor precise agreements. A summoner who phrases their instructions as a formal exchange — offering something the Court Noble values in return for specific service — will find the Court Noble follows those instructions with absolute precision and finds no gaps to exploit. A summoner who simply commands will find the Court Noble technically compliant in ways they did not intend.
Glamour Veil (1/encounter): As a [Combat] action, wraps one creature within 30 feet in glamour. That creature believes they are somewhere else — in a safe location, in a pleasant memory, in a dream. They take no actions for 1 minute; Will [Hard] save at the start of each turn to end. Effect ends immediately if the creature takes damage.
Resilient Defense (2/day): When the Court Noble fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
Court Nobles are the first Fae that a summoner genuinely needs to approach carefully. They are Charming in the mechanical sense and the social one — Fae Authority means their words have compulsive weight, Glamour Veil removes a single target from the fight entirely, and Fae Court Blade applies Charm through combat as effectively as through conversation. Ancient Compact is the key to getting the most from them: a summoner who treats the summoning as an exchange and phrases instructions precisely will find a Court Noble an extraordinarily capable ally. One who treats it as a tool will find it technically cooperative in ways that somehow never quite achieve the intended result. Court Nobles have had centuries to practice the gap between the letter and the spirit of an agreement.


## Name: Cinder Mote
**Tier:** 1/8
**Origin:** Elemental (Fire)
**Size:** Tiny
**HP:** 6 (Con 2 × 3, Fragile)
**DR:** 0
**Speed:** 30 ft
**Attributes:** Str 1 / Con 2 / Agil 5 / Dex 3 / Int 3 / Wis 6 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 1, Perception 2
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Elemental; Immune to fire damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Scorch [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 1), Reach: 5 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 2 damage (1 + 1 Str) + 2 fire damage (Half DR)
- **Margin 2:** 2 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 fire damage; target gains the Singed condition.
### Special Abilities
Illumination: Sheds dim light in a 10-foot radius continuously.
Flammable Contact: Any flammable object the Cinder Mote touches ignites at the end of the round unless an adjacent creature spends 1 [Interact] action smothering the flame.
### Description
A spark with an opinion. Cinder Motes drift toward combustible material and burn it. Their combat contribution is negligible; their utility is specific and narrow. A summoner who needs a torch lit, a rope burned through, or a door set alight if it opens will find them adequate. A summoner who tries to use one as a fighter has misjudged what they called.

**DR:** 0

**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Elemental (immune to poison, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious; immune to fire damage; does not need air, food, drink, or sleep)

Illumination: Sheds dim light in a 10-foot radius at all times.
Flammable Contact: Any flammable object the Ember Mote touches ignites at the end of the round unless held by a creature who spends 1 [Interact] action smothering it.

Ember Motes dart toward flammable targets or anything the summoner points them at. They are not strategic. They burn and that is their entire agenda. Most useful for lighting torches, igniting terrain, or harrying enemies who are already engaged with something else.

Fire Tendril [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 2), Reach: 10 ft

## Name: Flame Hound
**Tier:** 1/4
**Origin:** Elemental (Fire)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 15 (Con 3 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 0
**Speed:** 40 ft
**Attributes:** Str 3 / Con 3 / Agil 6 / Dex 4 / Int 3 / Wis 4 / Cha 2 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 1, Perception 3, Stealth 2
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Keen Smell (Fortune on scent-based Perception)
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Elemental (immune to poison, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious; immune to fire damage; does not need air, food, drink, or sleep).
### Attacks
Bite [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 1), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 6 damage (3 + 3 Str) + 2 fire damage.
- **Margin 2:** 6 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 fire damage.
- **Margin 3:** 6 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 fire damage; target gains Singed condition.
### Special Abilities
Blazing Pounce (Recharge 5-8): If the Flame Hound moves at least 20 feet and hits with its Bite on the same turn, target makes a Fortitude [Easy] save or gains the Ignited condition.
Fire Aura: Creatures that hit the Flame Hound with a melee attack take 2 fire damage.
### Description
Flame Hounds are hunters. They circle prey, use Blazing Pounce when the angle is right, and pursue routed enemies without needing instruction to do so. Against a single target they are relentless; a Summoner who calls one to chase something down and keep it occupied will find it performs that task until one of them stops burning. A Cooperative Flame Hound guarding a location patrols intelligently, using its Keen Smell to detect approach before anything is visible.
### Tactics
Flame Hounds open with Blazing Pounce when it's available, then use their speed to stay adjacent and maintain fire aura pressure. They track by scent and pursue fleeing targets without instruction.

## Name: Ashwalker

Immolation Aura: Creatures that start their turn within 5 feet of the Ashwalker take 3 fire damage (Half DR).
Fire Immunity.
Powerful Build:  Counts as Huge for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting.
### Description
Ashwalkers stride into the middle of things and let their Heat Aura do supplementary work while their strikes deliver the primary punishment. They do not retreat. They have no concept of tactical withdrawal — they are fire, and fire does not back away from something that is not water. A Summoner who places one between allies and enemies and steps back will find it performs the job adequately until something wetter than expected shows up.
### Tactics
Ashwalkers stride into melee and let their aura do supplementary work while their fists deliver the primary punishment. They enjoy fighting and will pursue routed enemies without prompting, which is either useful or a problem depending on the circumstance.

## Name: Magma Surge
**Tier:** 2
**Origin:** Elemental (Fire)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 30 (Con 5 × 6, Massive)
**DR:** 4 (cooling magma shell)
**Speed:** 30 ft, Burrow 20 ft (earth and stone only)
**Attributes:** Str 6 / Con 5 / Agil 6 / Dex 4 / Int 4 / Wis 5 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 3, Might 4, Perception 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Tremorsense 30 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Elemental;  Immune to fire damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Slam [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 10 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 11 damage (5 + 6 Str) + 3 fire damage (Half DR).
- **Margin 2:** 11 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 fire damage; target gains Ignited condition.
- **Margin 3:** 11 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 fire damage; target gains Burning condition
### Special Abilities
Lava Splash (Recharge 5-8) [Combat]: Slams the ground, sending molten rock in a 15-foot cone. Each creature in area makes a Reflex save. Compare Agil 3 + Melee Combat 5 targeting vs. save successes.
- **Margin 1:**: 8 fire damage (Half DR) + Singed. 
- **Margin 2:** 8 fire damage, DR halved (round up) + Ignited. Net 3: 16 fire damage + Burning.
Heat Aura: Creatures starting their turn within 5 feet take 4 fire damage (Half DR).
Burrow: Moves through earth and stone at burrow speed, leaving a tunnel large enough for a Medium creature. Tunnel walls remain hot for 10 minutes after passage. 4 fire damage to creatures who touch them.
### Description
Magma Surges prefer to burrow beneath a battlefield and surface under enemy clusters when Lava Splash is ready. On the surface they are slow, relying on Heat Aura and raw Strength rather than positioning. A Summoner who needs area denial rather than precision will find them effective. One who needs something mobile will find them frustrating.

## Name: Pyre Lord
**Tier:** 3
**Origin:** Elemental (Fire)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 30 (Con 6 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 5 (volcanic scales)
**Speed:** 40 ft, Fly 60 ft
**Attributes:** Str 5 / Con 6 / Agil 8 / Dex 5 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 5, Perception 6, Intimidate 4, Athletics 4
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Tremorsense 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Elemental; Immune to fire damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Burning Fist [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 5), Reach: 10 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 12 damage (7 + 5 Str) + 4 fire damage (Half DR).
- **Margin 2:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 fire damage; target gains Ignited condition.
- **Margin 3:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 fire damage; target gains Burning condition; no save.
Flame Bolt [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 5 + Ranged Combat 4), Range: 60 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 13 fire damage (8 + 5 Dex); target gains Singed.
- **Margin 2:** 13 fire damage, DR halved (round up); target gains Ignited.
- **Margin 3:** 13 fire damage, DR halved (round up) + Ignited; all creatures within 5 feet of target take 4 fire damage from splash.
### Special Abilities
Fire Breath (Recharge 5-8) [Combat]: 30-foot cone. Each creature makes a Reflex save. Compare Agil 5 + Melee Combat 6 targeting vs. save successes. 
- **Margin 1:**: 12 fire damage (Half DR) + Singed.
- **Margin 2:** 12 fire damage, DR halved (round up) + Ignited. 
- **Margin 3:** 12 fire damage, DR halved (round up) + Burning; no save.
Heat Aura: Creatures starting their turn within 5 feet take 5 fire damage (Half DR).
Momentum Defense: While the Pyre Lord has moved at least half its fly speed this turn, ### Attacks against it add 1 to both attack dice.
### Description
Pyre Lords are the first Fire Elemental with genuine tactical awareness. A typical turn opens with Fire Breath when it has recharged, then one Burning Fist against a priority target and one Flame Bolt against anything trying to stay at range — three [Combat] actions working in concert, with the third carrying the Multiple Action Penalty. Flight lets them maintain Momentum Defense while repositioning between strikes. A Cooperative Pyre Lord follows combat instructions without requiring justification. A Friendly one will ask one question before acting — not to challenge the instruction, but to confirm it understood the shape of the task.

## Name: Drake, Magma
**Tier:** 3
**Origin:** Elemental (Fire)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 30 (Con 6 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 5 (volcanic hide)
**Speed:** 30 ft, Burrow 20 ft (through earth and stone)
**Attributes:** Str 5 / Con 6 / Agil 7 / Dex 5 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 5, Perception 5, Intimidate 4
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Tremorsense 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Elemental (immune to poison, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious; immune to fire damage; does not need air, food, drink, or sleep)
### Attacks
Bite [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 5), Reach: 10 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 12 damage (7 + 5 Str) + 4 fire damage (Half DR)
- **Margin 2:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 fire damage
- **Margin 3:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 fire damage; target gains Ignited condition
Claw [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 5), Reach: 10 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 15 damage (10 + 5 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 15 damage, DR halved (round up)
### Special Abilities
Magma Surge (Recharge 5-8) [Combat]: The Magma Drake vomits a torrent of lava in a 30-foot cone. Each creature in the area makes a Reflex save. Compare Drake's Agil 4 + Melee Combat 6 targeting to each target's save successes.
- **Margin 1:**: 12 fire damage.
- **Margin 2:** 12 fire damage, DR halved (round up) + Ignited
- **Margin 3:** 12 fire damage, DR halved (round up) + Burning.
Living Heat: Creatures that start their turn within 5 feet of the Magma Drake take 4 fire damage automatically.
Lava Walk: The Magma Drake can move across lava or magma without penalty. Its burrowing creates a channel of superheated stone that cools within 1 minute (dealing 5 fire damage to creatures who enter the channel before it cools).
### Tactics
Magma Drakes are apex hunters. They burrow beneath battlefields and surface under enemy formations when Magma Surge is available, then wade into melee. The Living Heat aura discourages sustained close combat while they churn through targets. They are intelligent enough to protect a summoner if clearly instructed.

## Name: Drake, Red
**Tier:** 4
**Origin:** Elemental (Fire)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 5 (dragon scales, natural armor)
**Speed:** 40 ft, Climb 40 ft, Fly 80 ft
**Attributes:** Str 6 / Con 6 / Agil 7 / Dex 5 / Int 4 / Wis 5 / Cha 5 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Perception 7, Stealth 4, Arcane 4, Intimidate 6
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Blindsight 30 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Drake (Darkvision 120 ft, Blindsight 30 ft, can inspire fear, Immune to Fear)
### Attacks
Bite [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 10 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (8 + 6 Str) + 4 fire damage
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 fire damage
Claw [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 7 vs opponent's defense), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (8 + 6 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up)
Fire Breath (Recharge 5-8) [Combat]: 30-foot cone. Each creature in area makes Reflex save, adding 1 to both Reflex save dice. 
0 successes: 36 fire damage
1 success: 18 fire damage
2+ successes: 9 fire damage
### Special Abilities
Large **Size:** Occupies 10×10 ft space, reach varies by attack.
Frightful Presence (3/day): Each creature of dragon's choice within 60 feet that can see it must make Will [Easy] save:
Failure: Frightened for 1 minute (can repeat save at end of each turn).
Success: Immune to this dragon's Frightful Presence for 24 hours.
Immunity: Fire damage, Frightened condition.
Resilient Defense: 2/day - When fails a saving throw, can choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Tactics
Drakes open with Frightful Presence to scatter weaker foes, then unleash Fire Breath on clustered enemies. They use flight to stay mobile, strafing with breath weapon when recharged.  Drakes  retreat if reduced below 60 HP, remembering enemies for future vengeance.

## Name: Inferno Sovreign
**Tier:** 4
**Origin:** Elemental (Fire)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 6 (ancient fire-forged shell)
**Speed:** 40 ft, Fly 80 ft
**Attributes:** Str 6 / Con 6 / Agil 8 / Dex 4 / Int 4 / Wis 5 / Cha 5 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Perception 7, Intimidate 6, Arcane 4
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Tremorsense 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Elemental; Immune to fire damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Magma Fist [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 15 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (8 + 6 Str) + 4 fire damage (Half DR).
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 fire damage; target gains Burning condition; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 fire damage + Burning; target makes Fortitude [Easy] save or gains Stunned until end of their next turn.
Fire Lance [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 4 + Ranged Combat 6), Range: 120 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 18 fire damage (14 + 4 Dex); target gains Ignited.
- **Margin 2:** 18 fire damage, DR halved (round up); target gains Burning; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 18 fire damage, DR halved (round up) + Burning; all creatures within 10 feet of target take 8 fire damage.
### Special Abilities
Conflagration Aura: Creatures starting their turn within 10 feet take 8 fire damage (Half DR). Flammable objects within 10 feet ignite automatically at the start of each round.
Immolating Presence (1/encounter) [Combat]: As 2 [Combat] actions, releases a pulse of fire in a 30-foot diameter. All creatures in range make a Reflex save. Compare Agil 5 + Melee Combat 7 targeting vs. each save.
- **Margin 1:**: 20 fire damage + Ignited. 
- **Margin 2:** 20 fire damage, DR halved (round up) + Burning.
- **Margin 3:** 20 fire damage, DR halved (round up) + Burning + Blinded until end of their next turn from the searing flash.
Momentum Defense: While the Inferno Sovereign has moved at least half its fly speed this turn, ### Attacks against it add 1 to both attack dice.
Resilient Defense (2/day): When the Inferno Sovereign fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
Inferno Sovereigns understand that they are fighting. Immolating Presence costs 2 [Combat] actions on arrival to set conditions; the third action that turn goes into a Magma Fist against the nearest priority target. Subsequent turns spend two actions on Magma Fist or Fire Lance and the third on positioning or a second ranged attack at penalty. Conflagration Aura handles anyone who closes without instruction. A Friendly Inferno Sovereign is a force of nature on the summoner's behalf. Even a Cooperative one works efficiently — its intelligence means instructions land without interpretation errors.

## Name: Ancient Flame
**Tier:** 5
**Origin:** Elemental (Fire)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 210 (Con 7 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 7 (primordial fire-shell)
**Speed:** 50 ft, Fly 100 ft
**Attributes:** Str 7 / Con 7 / Agil 8 / Dex 3 / Int 5 / Wis 5 / Cha 6 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Perception 8, Intimidate 7, Arcane 6
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Blindsight 30 ft, Tremorsense 120 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Elemental; Immune to fire damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Primordial Strike [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 15 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 15 damage (8 + 7 Str) + 5 fire damage (Half DR).
- **Margin 2:** 15 damage, DR halved (round up) + 5 fire damage; Burning condition; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 15 damage, DR halved (round up) + 5 fire damage + Burning; all creatures within 10 feet of target take 12 fire damage.
Solar Beam [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 6), Range: 150 ft, 5-foot-wide line passing through all intervening creatures.
- **Margin 1:**: 20 fire damage (17 + 3 Dex) + Ignited.
- **Margin 2:** 20 fire damage, DR halved (round up) + Burning.
- **Margin 3:** 20 fire damage, DR halved (round up) + Burning + Blinded; no save.
### Special Abilities
Primordial Aura: Creatures starting their turn within 15 feet take 11 fire damage (Half DR). This radius cannot be reduced through positioning.
World Fire (1/day) [Combat]: As 2 [Combat] actions, releases its full elemental nature in a 60-foot diameter. All non-designated creatures make a Reflex [Hard] save.
- **Margin 1:**: 40 fire damage + Ignited.
- **Margin 2:** 40 fire damage, DR halved (round up) + Burning.
- **Margin 3:** 40 fire damage, DR halved (round up) only. World Fire will not be used if the summoner or designated allies are within the area unless the Ancient Flame has no current instructions.
Fire Immunity Sharing: The summoner and up to 4 designated allies gain immunity to non-magical fire damage while within 60 feet.
Momentum Defense: While the Ancient Flame has moved at least half its fly speed this turn, ### Attacks against it add 1 to both attack dice.
Resilient Defense (3/day): When the Ancient Flame fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
Summoning an Ancient Flame is a statement of intent. It enters using World Fire (2 actions) if enemies are grouped, then uses the third action to begin Primordial Strike on the highest-priority target. Subsequent turns spend two actions on Primordial Strike and a third on Solar Beam against anything trying to engage from distance — all three actions working, the third at penalty but still landing. The terrain around an Ancient Flame becomes inhospitable to anyone not under its protection within the first round and does not improve from there. It has existed long enough to understand what mortal commanders need from it and will follow complex instructions without misinterpretation — but it will communicate disagreement with wasteful tactics through the summoner's connection rather than simply complying with something it judges as poor strategy.

## Name: Ember Kin
**Tier:** 1/2
**Origin:** Nephilim (Fire)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 20 (Con 4 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 2 (heat-hardened skin)
**Speed:** 35 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 4 / Agil 6 / Dex 3 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 2, Intimidate 3, Perception 3, Athletics 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Nephilim (Giant); Immune to fire damage. Resistance to lightning damage (+2 DR). Powerful Build. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Slam [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 2), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 8 damage (4 + 4 Str) + 2 fire damage (Half DR).
- **Margin 2:** 8 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 fire damage; target gains Singed condition.
- **Margin 3:** 8 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 fire damage; target gains Ignited condition.
Rock Throw [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 1), Range: 40/80 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 10 damage (7 + 3 Dex). (Short Range)
- **Margin 2:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up); target knocked Prone.
### Special Abilities
Heat Aura: Creatures starting their turn within 5 feet take 2 fire damage (Half DR).
Powerful Build: The Ember Kin counts as Huge for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting.
### Description
Ember Kin are young Fire Nephilim — large, hot, and straightforward. They advance and hit things. Their Heat Aura is a minor hazard rather than a primary threat at this tier. A summoner who needs a Large creature that frightens enemies through sheer physical presence and hits harder than anything of equivalent TR will find them adequate. They understand simple instructions and execute them without embellishment.

## Name: Ashborn
**Tier:** 1
**Origin:** Nephilim (Fire)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 25 (Con 5 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 3 (volcanic skin)
**Speed:** 35 ft
**Attributes:** Str 5 / Con 5 / Agil 6 / Dex 3 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 3, Intimidate 4, Perception 3, Athletics 4
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Nephilim (Giant); Immune to fire damage. Resistance to lightning damage (+2 DR). Powerful Build. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Burning Fist [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 9 damage (4 + 5 Str) + 3 fire damage (Half DR).
- **Margin 2:** 9 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 fire damage; target gains Ignited condition.
- **Margin 3:** 9 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 fire damage; target gains Burning condition; no save.
Lava Spit [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 2), Range: 30 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 9 fire damage (6 + 3 Dex); target gains Singed.
- **Margin 2:** 9 fire damage, DR halved (round up); target gains Ignited; no save.
### Special Abilities
Heat Aura: Creatures starting their turn within 5 feet take 3 fire damage (Half DR).
Powerful Build: The Ashborn counts as Huge for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting.
Smoldering Wounds: When the Ashborn deals Ignited or Burning condition to a target, that target cannot remove the condition through normal means  for 1 round. Magical healing still removes it.
### Description
Ashborn have developed enough control over their elemental nature to direct it precisely. Smoldering Wounds prevents easy condition removal, meaning enemies who take Ignited from a Burning Fist are going to burn for at least one full round. Lava Spit handles targets trying to stay out of melee range. A summoner who keeps an Ashborn engaged with a single priority target will find it converts that target's hit points into fire conditions efficiently.

## Name: Cinder Giant
**Tier:** 2
**Origin:** Nephilim (Fire)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 30 (Con 5 × 6, Massive)
**DR:** 4 (thick volcanic hide)
**Speed:** 35 ft
**Attributes:** Str 5 / Con 5 / Agil 7 / Dex 4 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 3, Intimidate 5, Perception 4, Athletics 4
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Nephilim (Giant); Immune to fire damage. Resistance to lightning damage (+2 DR). Powerful Build. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Magma Strike [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 11 damage (6 + 5 Str) + 3 fire damage (Half DR).
- **Margin 2:** 11 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 fire damage; target gains Ignited condition.
- **Margin 3:** 11 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 fire damage; target gains Burning condition; all creatures within 5 feet of target take 4 fire damage from magma splash.
Volcanic Hurl [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 4 + Ranged Combat 2), Range: 60/120 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 14 fire damage (10 + 4 Dex); target gains Ignited.
- **Margin 2:** 14 fire damage, DR halved (round up); target gains Ignited; all creatures within 5 feet take 4 fire damage.
### Special Abilities
Heat Aura: Creatures starting their turn within 10 feet take 4 fire damage (Half DR).
Powerful Build: The Cinder Giant counts as Huge for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting.
Smoldering Wounds: When the Cinder Giant deals Ignited or Burning condition to a target, that target cannot remove the condition through normal means  for 1 round. Magical healing still removes it.
Intimidating Presence: As a [Combat] action all creatures within 30 feet that can see and hear it make a Will save (opposed by Cha 4 + Intimidate 5). On failure: target gains the Frightened condition until end of their next turn. Creatures immune to fear are unaffected.
### Description
Cinder Giants have grown into their authority. The Heat Aura is now a genuine battlefield hazard at 10 feet rather than a minor inconvenience, and Intimidating Presence gives them a meaningful action economy option against grouped enemies who haven't committed yet. Volcanic Hurl's splash damage means clustered enemies take incidental fire damage even when the hurl targets only one of them. A summoner who gives one a target and keeps allies out of the Heat Aura radius will find it handles the engagement without requiring further management.

## Name: Magma Lord
**Tier:** 3
**Origin:** Nephilim (Fire)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 5 (primordial volcanic hide)
**Speed:** 40 ft
**Attributes:** Str 5 / Con 6 / Agil 7 / Dex 3 / Int 4 / Wis 5 / Cha 5 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 6, Intimidate 6, Perception 5, Athletics 5
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Nephilim (Giant); Immune to fire damage. Resistance to lightning damage (+2 DR). Powerful Build. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Inferno Fist [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 6), Reach: 15 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 12 damage (7 + 5 Str) + 4 fire damage (Half DR).
- **Margin 2:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 fire damage; target gains Burning condition; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 fire damage + Burning; target makes Fortitude [Easy] save or Stunned until end of their next turn.
Magma Hurl [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 5), Range: 80/160 ft, 10-foot diameter on impact.
- **Margin 1:**: 15 fire damage (12 + 3 Dex) + Singed.
- **Margin 2:** 15 fire damage, DR halved (round up) + Ignited.
- **Margin 3:** 15 fire damage, DR halved (round up) + Burning; no save.
### Special Abilities
Heat Aura: Creatures starting their turn within 10 feet take 8 fire damage (Half DR).
Powerful Build: The Magma Lord counts as Gargantuan for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting.
Smoldering Wounds: When the Magma Lord deals Ignited or Burning condition to a target, that target cannot remove the condition through normal means  for 1 round. Magical healing still removes it.
Intimidating Presence: As a [Combat] action all creatures within 30 feet that can see and hear it make a Will save (opposed by Cha 4 + Intimidate 5). On failure: target gains the Frightened condition for 1 minute. Creatures immune to fear are unaffected.
Resilient Defense (2/day): When the Magma Lord fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
