Magma Lords are the point at which Fire Nephilim become a genuine threat to organized groups rather than individual combatants. Intimidating Presence at Hard task scatters most formations on arrival. Magma Hurl's 10-foot diameter means ranged enemies cannot cluster without taking shared fire damage on every throw. The Heat Aura at this range punishes anyone who tries to engage in melee without significant fire resistance. A summoner deploying one against a disciplined formation should expect the formation to stop being disciplined within the first round.

## Name: Flame Titan
**Tier:** 4
**Origin:** Nephilim (Fire)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 6 (ancient volcanic shell)
**Speed:** 40 ft
**Attributes:** Str 6 / Con 6 / Agil 7 / Dex 3 / Int 5 / Wis 5 / Cha 6 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Intimidate 7, Perception 6, Athletics 6
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Nephilim (Giant); Immune to fire damage. Resistance to lightning damage (+2 DR). Powerful Build. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
World-Breaker Fist [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 15 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (8 + 6 Str) + 4 fire damage (Half DR).
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 fire damage; Burning condition + target knocked Prone; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 fire damage + Burning + Prone; all creatures within 10 feet of target take 8 fire damage from the impact shockwave.
Inferno Hurl [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 6), Range: 120 ft, 15-foot diameter
- **Margin 1:**: 18 fire damage (15 + 3 Dex) + Ignited.
- **Margin 2:** 18 fire damage, DR halved (round up) + Burning.
- **Margin 3:** 18 fire damage, DR halved (round up) + Burning; target makes Fortitude [Easy] save or Stunned until end of their next turn.
### Special Abilities
Conflagration Aura: Creatures starting their turn within 15 feet take 9 fire damage (Half DR). Flammable objects within 15 feet ignite at the start of each round.
Fire Immunity Sharing: The summoner and up to 2 designated allies gain immunity to non-magical fire damage while within 30 feet.
Smoldering Wounds: When the Flame Titan deals Ignited or Burning condition to a target, that target cannot remove the condition through normal means  for 1 round. Magical healing still removes it.
Powerful Build: The Flame Titan counts as Gargantuan for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting.
Intimidating Presence: As a [Combat] action all creatures within 30 feet that can see and hear it make a Will save (opposed by Cha 4 + Intimidate 5). On failure: target gains the Frightened condition for 1 minute. Creatures immune to fear are unaffected.
Resilient Defense (3/day): When the Flame Titan fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
A Flame Titan's Conflagration Aura reshapes the battlefield simply by existing — any environment with flammable material becomes a fire hazard within 15 feet of it, and enemies without fire resistance face meaningful damage just for closing to melee range. Fire Immunity Sharing protects the summoner and two allies from the collateral environmental damage the Flame Titan inevitably generates. Inferno Hurl's 15-foot diameter means it can suppress entire squads at range while closing to melee. A summoner who designates protected allies immediately on summoning and then stays clear will find the Flame Titan manages its own engagement effectively.

## Name: Worldfire Colossus
**Tier:** 5
**Origin:** Nephilim (Fire)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 210 (Con 7 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 7 (primordial fire-flesh)
**Speed:** 45 ft
**Attributes:** Str 7 / Con 7 / Agil 8 / Dex 3 / Int 5 / Wis 5 / Cha 6 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Intimidate 8, Perception 7, Athletics 7
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Tremorsense 60 ft (heat signatures)
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Nephilim (Giant); Immune to fire damage. Resistance to lightning damage (+2 DR). Powerful Build. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Cataclysm Strike [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 15 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 15 damage (8 + 7 Str) + 5 fire damage (Half DR).
- **Margin 2:** 15 damage, DR halved (round up) + 5 fire damage; Burning + Prone; no save; all creatures within 10 feet take 10 fire damage.
- **Margin 3:** 15 damage, DR halved (round up) + 5 fire damage + Burning + Prone + Stunned until end of their next turn; area damage increases to 16 fire.
World-Flame Hurl [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 6), Range: 150 ft, 20-foot diameter.
- **Margin 1:**: 20 fire damage (17 + 3 Dex) + Ignited.
- **Margin 2:** 20 fire damage, DR halved (round up) + Burning.
- **Margin 3:** 20 fire damage, DR halved (round up) + Burning + Blinded until end of their next turn from the searing flash; no save.
### Special Abilities
Primordial Aura: Creatures starting their turn within 20 feet take 11 fire damage (Half DR). Flammable terrain within 20 feet ignites and continues burning — the area becomes Difficult Terrain as fire spreads at normal rates.
Powerful Build: The Worldfire Colossus counts as Gargantuan for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting.
World-Burning Presence (1/day) [Combat]: As 2 [Combat] actions, the Worldfire Colossus expresses its full elemental nature in a 60-foot diameter. All non-designated creatures make a Reflex [Hard] save .
- **Margin 1:**: 12 fire damage only. The ground within the area ignites if flammable; non-flammable surfaces become superheated (4 fire damage per round to creatures standing on them for the next minute).
- **Margin 2:** 12 fire damage, DR halved (round up) + Ignited.
- **Margin 3:** 12 fire damage, DR halved (round up) + Burning + Blinded for 1 minute.
Fire Immunity Sharing: The summoner and up to 4 designated allies gain immunity to non-magical fire damage within 60 feet.
Intimidating Presence: As a [Combat] action all creatures within 30 feet that can see and hear it make a Will save (opposed by Cha 4 + Intimidate 5). On failure: target gains the Frightened condition for 1 minute. Creatures immune to fear are unaffected.
Resilient Defense (3/day): When the Worldfire Colossus fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
A Worldfire Colossus is a living catastrophe that has agreed, for now, to catastrophe on the summoner's behalf. World-Burning Presence on arrival sets the terms of the engagement — after it goes off, the battlefield is a fire hazard for everyone not designated as protected. The Primordial Aura at 20 feet means enemies cannot close without taking 14 fire damage per round before any attack lands. A summoner who calls one has made a decision about how this fight ends, and the Colossus will execute that decision with a thoroughness that renders tactical nuance largely irrelevant.

## Name: Mud Grub
**Tier:** 1/8
**Origin:** Elemental (Earth)
**Size:** Small
**HP:** 8 (Con 2 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** 1 (hardened clay shell)
**Speed:** 20 ft, Burrow 20 ft
**Attributes:** Str 2 / Con 2 / Agil 5 / Dex 3 / Int 3 / Wis 5 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 1, Perception 1
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Tremorsense 30 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Elemental (immune to poison, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious; does not need air, food, drink, or sleep)
### Attacks
Bite [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 1), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 5 damage (3 + 2 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 5 damage, DR halved (round up)
### Special Abilities
Burrowing: Can move through earth and loose stone at its burrow speed. Movement through solid rock is not possible.
Drag Under: When the Mud Grub successfully grapples a creature that is standing on earth or soil, the creature sinks 1 foot into the ground. A sinking creature gains the Restrained condition until it pulls free (Might or Athletics check, Hard task).
### Tactics
Mud Grubs burrow under enemies and attack from below, attempting to grapple and use Drag Under to immobilize. Useful for pinning a single target rather than dealing meaningful damage.

## Name: Pebble Sprite
**Tier:** 1/8
**Origin:** Elemental (Earth)
**Size:** Tiny
**HP:** 6 (Con 2 × 3, Fragile)
**DR:** 1 (stone skin)
**Speed:** 20 ft, Burrow 10 ft (loose soil only)
**Attributes:** Str 3 / Con 2 / Agil 2 / Dex 3 / Int 3 / Wis 7 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 1, Perception 2
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Tremorsense 10 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +2 (Agil 2)
**Creature Type:** Elemental; Immune to non-magical piercing and slashing damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Gravel Hurl [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 1), Range: 20 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 5 damage (2 + 3 Dex).
- **Margin 2:** 5 damage, DR halved (round up); target makes Reflex [Easy] save or gains Dazzled condition until end of their next turn from gravel in the eyes.
### Special Abilities
Earth Sense: Aware of all vibrations through the ground within 10 ft. Cannot be surprised by creatures touching the same surface.
Stone Shape (minor): Once per encounter, alters a small quantity of loose stone (fills a crack up to 1 inch wide and 3 feet long, creates a rough step in soft stone).
### Description
Pebble Sprites are scouts and minor nuisances. Their Tremorsense within 10 feet tells the summoner what is moving on any surface they occupy, and their minor stone shaping is occasionally exactly what is needed. A summoner who calls one for a fight has made a conceptual error. One called to monitor a floor, plug a small gap, or confirm whether something is approaching through ground vibration will find it performs that task adequately.

## Name: Stone Lurker
**Tier:** 1/4
**Origin:** Elemental (Earth)
**Size:** Small
**HP:** 12 (Con 3 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** 3 (stone shell)
**Speed:** 20 ft, Burrow 20 ft (earth and stone)
**Attributes:** Str 3 / Con 3 / Agil 5 / Dex 4 / Int 3 / Wis 5 / Cha 2 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 2, Might 3, Perception 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Tremorsense 30 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Elemental; Immune to non-magical piercing and slashing damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Slam [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 2), Reach: 5 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 7 damage (4 + 3 Str).
- **Margin 2:** 7 damage, DR halved (round up); target makes Fortitude save (1 success) or knocked Prone.
### Special Abilities
Submerge: As a [Move] action, the Stone Lurker submerges into natural earth or stone it is standing on. While submerged it is Hidden and moves at its burrow speed. It must surface to attack.
Ambush: When the Stone Lurker ### Attacks on the same turn it surfaced from Submerge, it has Fortune on that attack.
### Description
Stone Lurkers submerge, position under a target using Tremorsense, surface for an Ambush strike, and submerge again. They are slow and hit moderately hard but are genuinely difficult to pin down while natural ground is available. A summoner who places one under an approach path and tells it to hit anything that walks over it will find the instructions followed precisely and without elaboration.

## Name: Granite Ward
**Tier:** 1/2
**Origin:** Elemental (Earth)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 20 (Con 4 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 4 (granite skin)
**Speed:** 25 ft, Burrow 20 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 4 / Agil 6 / Dex 4 / Int 4 / Wis 5 / Cha 2 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 2, Might 4, Perception 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Tremorsense 30 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Elemental; Immune to non-magical piercing and slashing damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Slam [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 2), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 10 damage (6 + 4 Str).
- **Margin 2:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up); target knocked Prone; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 8 damage + Prone; target Restrained by earth and stone until they succeed on a Might [hard] check as a [Combat] action.
### Reactions
Stone Skin [Reaction]: Once per round when hit by a physical attack, reduce incoming damage by 4.
### Special Abilities
Immovable: Cannot be knocked Prone by non-magical forced movement. Shoves automatically fail unless the attacker wins an opposed Strength + Athletics [Hard] check.
Earth Bond: While standing on natural earth or stone, regains 4 HP at the start of its turn. Suppressed if it took magical damage this round.
### Description
Granite Wards hold ground. They plant themselves where the summoner indicates and do not move unless ordered to. Earth Bond makes attrition against them on natural surfaces a slow process. They function best as mobile obstacles — position one in a doorway, narrow pass, or between the summoner and the enemy and it will remain there performing exactly that function until something removes it.

## Name: Stone Soldier
**Tier:** 1/2
**Origin:** Elemental (Earth)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 20 (Con 4 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 3 (granite skin)
**Speed:** 25 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 4 / Agil 6 / Dex 4 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 2, Might 3, Perception 2
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Tremorsense 30 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Elemental (immune to poison, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious; does not need air, food, drink, or sleep)
### Attacks
Slam [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 2), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 10 damage (6 + 4 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up)
- **Margin 3:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up); target is knocked Prone
### Reactions
Stone Skin: [Reaction] once per round, Gain +2 DR against one attack.
### Special Abilities
Immovable Mass: The Stone Soldier cannot be knocked Prone by any non-magical forced movement. Shoves and trips automatically fail unless the attacker wins an opposed Strength + Athletics check against the Soldier's Strength + Might (2 successes required).
Gravel Rain: Once per encounter as a [Combat] action, the Stone Soldier slams the ground, sending a spray of rock fragments in a 10-foot cone. Creatures in area make a Reflex [Easy] save or take 8 bludgeoning damage (half on success). Full DR applies.
### Tactics
Stone Soldiers advance steadily and hold ground. They will not pursue at speed; they outlast opposition rather than chase it. A summoner who needs something to simply not move will find them ideal.

## Name: Boulder Soldier
**Tier:** 1
**Origin:** Elemental (Earth)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 25 (Con 5 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 5 (layered granite shell)
**Speed:** 25 ft, Burrow 25 ft
**Attributes:** Str 5 / Con 5 / Agil 5 / Dex 4 / Int 4 / Wis 5 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 3, Might 5, Perception 3, Intimidate 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Tremorsense 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Elemental; Immune to non-magical piercing and slashing damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Slam [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 12 damage (7 + 5 Str).
- **Margin 2:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up); target knocked Prone; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 12 damage + Prone; target Restrained until they succeed on Might [Hard] check as a [Combat] action.
Rock Throw [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 4 + Ranged Combat 2), Range: 60/120 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 12 damage (8 + 4 Dex).
- **Margin 2:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up); target knocked Prone.
### Special Abilities
Immovable: Cannot be knocked Prone by non-magical forced movement. Shoves automatically fail unless the attacker wins an opposed Strength + Athletics [Hard] check.
Earth Bond: While standing on natural earth or stone, regains 4 HP at the start of its turn. Suppressed if it took magical damage this round.
Tremor Strike (1/encounter) [Combat]: As a [Combat] action, slams both fists into the ground simultaneously. All creatures within 15 feet standing on natural ground make a Reflex save. Compare Agil 2 + Might 5 targeting vs. each save.
- **Margin 1:**: 8 bludgeoning damage + Prone.
- **Margin 2:** 8 bludgeoning damage, DR halved (round up) + Prone + Restrained.
### Reactions
Stone Skin [Reaction]: Once per round, reduce incoming physical damage by 6.
### Description
Boulder Soldiers are siege engines that walk. They advance slowly, shrug off mundane weapons entirely, and hit hard enough to flatten anyone who does not step back. Rock Throw handles opponents attempting to stay outside their reach. Tremor Strike is their battlefield opener when enemies have clustered — a single action that resets the positioning of everything nearby. A summoner who needs a wall broken or a cluster of fighters scattered will find them effective. Speed and nuance are not in their vocabulary.

## Name: Mountain Heart
**Tier:** 2
**Origin:** Elemental (Earth)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 30 (Con 5 × 6, Massive)
**DR:** 6 (deep granite core)
**Speed:** 30 ft, Burrow 30 ft
**Attributes:** Str 6 / Con 5 / Agil 5 / Dex 4 / Int 4 / Wis 6 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 3, Might 6, Perception 4, Intimidate 4
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Tremorsense 120 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Elemental; Immune to non-magical piercing and slashing damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Crushing Fist [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (8 + 6 Str).
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up); target Grappled; no save; Grappled target takes 8 bludgeoning damage at the start of each of their turns.
- **Margin 3:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up) + Grappled + Restrained + ongoing 8 damage.
### Special Abilities
Immovable: Cannot be knocked Prone by non-magical forced movement. Shoves automatically fail unless the attacker wins an opposed Strength + Athletics [Hard] check.
Earth Bond: While standing on natural earth or stone, regains 6 HP at the start of its turn. Suppressed if it took magical damage this round.
Engulf (Recharge 5-8) [Combat]: Drives a Grappled target partially into the earth. Target must already be Grappled by the Mountain Heart. Target makes a Fortitude [Hard] save. Failure: Restrained, Blinded, and takes 4 bludgeoning damage at the start of each turn. Escape requires a Might/Acrobatics [hard]check as a full [Combat] action, or an ally must dig them out over 2 rounds of [Interact] actions. On 1 success: Restrained only, not Blinded.
### Reactions
Stone Skin [Reaction]: Once per round, reduce incoming physical damage by 8.
### Description
Mountain Hearts grab things and do not let go. They spend actions building toward a Grapple and then Engulf the most dangerous held target to remove them from the fight entirely. Against opponents too large or strong to Grapple efficiently they simply hit very hard and regenerate. A summoner who needs something physically held — not slowed, not intimidated, but literally held in place — will find nothing better at this tier.

## Name: Deepstone Elder
**Tier:** 3
**Origin:** Elemental (Earth)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 7 (primordial granite)
**Speed:** 30 ft, Burrow 40 ft
**Attributes:** Str 6 / Con 6 / Agil 5 / Dex 3 / Int 6 / Wis 6 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 6, Might 7, Perception 5, Intimidate 5
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Tremorsense 300 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Elemental; Immune to non-magical piercing and slashing damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Ancient Slam [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 6), Reach: 15 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 16 damage (10 + 6 Str).
- **Margin 2:** 16 damage, DR halved (round up); target knocked Prone and Stunned until end of their next turn; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 22 damage + Prone + Stunned + Restrained by erupting earth; Might/Acrobatics [Hard]check to escape as a [Combat] action.
Rock Hurl [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 5), Range: 60/120 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 16 damage (13 + 3 Dex).
- **Margin 2:** 16 damage, DR halved (round up); target knocked Prone; no save.
### Special Abilities
Immovable: Cannot be knocked Prone by non-magical forced movement. Shoves automatically fail unless the attacker wins an opposed Strength + Athletics [Hard] check.
Earth Bond: While standing on natural earth or stone, regains 8 HP at the start of its turn. Suppressed if it took magical damage this round.
Ground Shatter (Recharge 5-8) [Combat]: Slams the earth with both fists simultaneously. All creatures on the ground within 40 feet make a Reflex save. Compare Str 8 + Might 7 targeting vs. each save.
- **Margin 1:**: 16 bludgeoning damage + Prone.
- **Margin 2:** 16 bludgeoning damage, DR halved (round up) + Prone + Restrained by cracking earth.
- **Margin 3:** 16 bludgeoning damage, DR halved (round up) + Prone + Restrained + Stunned until end of their next turn. Designated allies are immune.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Reactions
Stone Skin [Reaction]: Once per round, reduce incoming physical damage by 10.
Resilient Defense (2/day): When the Deepstone Elder fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
### Description
Deepstone Elders arrived at their current form by outlasting everything that attempted to move or break them. Tremorsense at 300 feet means nothing on the ground approaches without their awareness. Ground Shatter resets the battlefield on arrival; Ancient Slam handles whatever remains. Their regeneration and DR make sustained mundane damage against them impractical — without magical weapons or significant energy expenditure, fighting a Deepstone Elder on natural ground is an exercise in attrition the attacker will lose.

## Name: Earthlord
**Tier:** 4
**Origin:** Elemental (Earth)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 8 (deep-forged primordial shell)
**Speed:** 30 ft, Burrow 50 ft
**Attributes:** Str 7 / Con 6 / Agil 5 / Dex 3 / Int 6 / Wis 7 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Might 8, Perception 6, Intimidate 6
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Tremorsense 1 mile
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Elemental; Immune to non-magical piercing and slashing damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Mountain's Fist [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 15 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 18 damage (11 + 7 Str).
- **Margin 2:** 18 damage, DR halved (round up); target Grappled + Restrained + knocked Prone; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 18 damage, DR halved (round up) + Grappled + Restrained + Prone; target takes 12 bludgeoning damage at the start of each of their turns; Fortitude [Hard] save each round to break free.
Boulder Hurl [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 6), Range: 90/180 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 18 damage (15 + 3 Dex).
- **Margin 2:** 18 damage, DR halved (round up); target knocked Prone + Stunned until end of their next turn; no save.
### Special Abilities
Immovable: Magical forced movement requires the caster to win an opposed check against the Earthlord's Str 8 + Might 8 [Hard].
Earth Bond: While standing on natural earth or stone, regains 10 HP at the start of its turn. Suppressed if it took magical damage this round.
Seismic Authority (1/day) [Combat]: Strikes the ground once. A 60-foot diameter seismic event occurs. All non-designated creatures on natural ground make Fortitude [Hard] saves. Failure: 32 bludgeoning damage + Prone + Restrained for 1 minute; ally must spend 2 [Interact] actions digging them out or they succeed on a Might/Acrobatics [Hard] check as a full [Combat] action. 1 success: 16 damage + Prone only. 
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Reactions
Stone Skin [Reaction]: Once per round, reduce incoming physical damage by 12.
Resilient Defense (3/day): When the Earthlord fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
### Description
Earthlords do not hurry. Seismic Authority immediately Restrains as many enemies as possible; Mountain's Fist methodically crushes whatever remains. Their regeneration and DR make sustained damage against them impractical without magical investment. A summoner who has a Cooperative Earthlord has not acquired a tool — they have persuaded a geological force to point itself in a useful direction for a while.

## Name: Ancient Stone
**Tier:** 5
**Origin:** Elemental (Earth)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 210 (Con 7 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 9 (primordial earth-shell)
**Speed:** 30 ft, Burrow 60 ft
**Attributes:** Str 8 / Con 7 / Agil 5 / Dex 2 / Int 7 / Wis 7 / Cha 5 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Might 8, Perception 7, Intimidate 7
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Tremorsense 2 miles
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Elemental; Immune to non-magical piercing and slashing damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
World Crush [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 20 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 20 damage (12 + 8 Str).
- **Margin 2:** 20 damage, DR halved (round up); target Grappled + Restrained + Prone; takes 16 bludgeoning damage at start of each of their turns; no save; Fortitude [Hard] save each round to break free.
- **Margin 3:** 20 damage, DR halved (round up) + Grappled + Restrained + Prone; target begins Engulf — driven 5 feet into the ground at start of their next turn unless they succeed on a Fortitude [Hard] save, adding Blinded and ongoing damage to 32.
Mountain Hurl [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 2 + Ranged Combat 6), Range: 120/240 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 20 damage (18 + 2 Dex).
- **Margin 2:** 20 damage, DR halved (round up); target knocked Prone + Stunned + all creatures within 10 feet take 8 bludgeoning damage from impact debris; no save on debris.
### Special Abilities
Immovable: Magical forced movement requires winning an opposed check against Str 8 + Might 8 [Hard]. Non-magical forced movement is impossible.
Earth Bond: While standing on natural earth or stone, regains 12 HP at the start of its turn. Suppressed if it took magical damage this round.
Continental Tremor (1/day) [Combat]: As 2 [Combat] actions, releases the earth's full force in an 80-foot diameter. Every non-designated creature on natural ground makes a Fortitude [Hard] save.
- **Margin 1:**: 10 damage only. 
- **Margin 2:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up) + Prone.
- **Margin 3:** 10 bludgeoning damage, DR halved (round up) + Restrained for 1 hour; requires excavation to escape.
Piercing and Slashing Immunity Sharing: The summoner and up to 4 designated allies gain immunity to non-magical piercing and slashing damage while within 60 feet.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Reactions
Stone Skin [Reaction]: Once per round, reduce incoming physical damage by 14.
Resilient Defense (3/day): When the Ancient Stone fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
### Description
An Ancient Stone summoned is not a decision about a fight. It is a decision about a place — this ground, held against everything, for as long as necessary. Continental Tremor resets the battlefield completely on arrival. World Crush removes individuals permanently from the engagement. The Ancient Stone is old enough and intelligent enough to follow complex orders without misinterpretation, and patient enough that it does not distinguish between a fight that lasts ten minutes and one that lasts ten hours. Both are the same to it.

## Name: Stone Kin
**Tier:** 1/2
**Origin:** Nephilim (Earth)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 20 (Con 4 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 4 (granite flesh)
**Speed:** 25 ft, Burrow 15 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 4 / Agil 6 / Dex 4 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 2, Might 4, Perception 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Tremorsense 30 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Nephilim (Giant); Immune to non-magical piercing and slashing damage. Resistance to non-magical bludgeoning damage (+2 DR). Powerful Build. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Slam [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 2), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 10 damage (6 + 4 Str).
- **Margin 2:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up); target knocked Prone; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 8 damage + Prone; target Restrained by erupting stone until they succeed on a Might [Hard] check as a [Combat] action.
Rock Throw [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 4 + Ranged Combat 1), Range: 40/80 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 10 damage (6 + 4 Dex).
- **Margin 2:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up); target knocked Prone.
### Special Abilities
Immovable: Cannot be knocked Prone by non-magical forced movement. Shoves automatically fail unless the attacker wins an opposed Strength + Athletics [Hard] check.
Earth Bond: While on natural earth or stone, regains 4 HP at the start of its turn. Suppressed if it took magical damage this round.
Powerful Build: Stone Kin counts as Huge for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting.
### Description
Stone Kin are dense, durable, and slow. Mundane weapons accomplish essentially nothing against their piercing and slashing immunity. They hold positions with the same commitment as Earth Elementals — put one in a doorway and it will be there when you return. Their Tremorsense gives them awareness of anything approaching through connected ground, which makes them difficult to ambush and reliable as sentinels even without active instructions.

## Name: Rockborn
**Tier:** 1
**Origin:** Nephilim (Earth)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 25 (Con 5 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 5 (deep granite flesh)
**Speed:** 25 ft, Burrow 20 ft
**Attributes:** Str 5 / Con 5 / Agil 5 / Dex 4 / Int 4 / Wis 5 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 3, Might 5, Perception 3, Intimidate 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Tremorsense 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Nephilim (Giant); Immune to non-magical piercing and slashing damage. Resistance to non-magical bludgeoning damage (+2 DR). Powerful Build. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Crushing Slam [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 12 damage (7 + 5 Str).
- **Margin 2:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up); target knocked Prone + Grappled; no save; Grappled target takes 6 bludgeoning damage at the start of each of their turns.
- **Margin 3:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + Prone + Grappled + Restrained; ongoing damage increases to 10.
Boulder Hurl [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 4 + Ranged Combat 2), Range: 60/120 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 12 damage (8 + 4 Dex).
- **Margin 2:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up); target knocked Prone + Stunned until end of their next turn; no save.
### Special Abilities
Immovable: Cannot be knocked Prone by non-magical forced movement. Shoves automatically fail unless the attacker wins an opposed Strength + Athletics [Hard] check.
Earth Bond: While on natural earth or stone, regains 4 HP at the start of its turn. Suppressed if it took magical damage this round.
Powerful Build: Rockborn counts as Huge for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting.
Stone Grip: Creatures Grappled by the Rockborn add 1 to both dice on Athletics, Acrobatics, and Might checks to escape.
### Reactions
Stone Skin [Reaction]: Once per round when hit by a physical attack, reduce incoming damage by 4.
### Description
Rockborn transition Stone Nephilim from obstacles into active threats. Crushing Slam builds toward Grapple and the ongoing damage that Grapple provides. Stone Grip makes escape from that Grapple genuinely difficult. A summoner who wants something held in place while allies deal with it elsewhere will find a Rockborn with a Grappled target the most efficient delivery mechanism available at this tier.

## Name: Boulder Giant
**Tier:** 2
**Origin:** Nephilim (Earth)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 150 (Con 5 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 6 (ancient granite flesh)
**Speed:** 30 ft, Burrow 25 ft
**Attributes:** Str 6 / Con 5 / Agil 5 / Dex 3 / Int 5 / Wis 5 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 5, Might 6, Perception 4, Intimidate 4
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Tremorsense 120 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Nephilim (Giant); Immune to non-magical piercing and slashing damage. Resistance to non-magical bludgeoning damage (+2 DR). Powerful Build. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Mountain Fist [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 5), Reach: 15 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (8 + 6 Str).
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up); target Grappled + Prone; no save; ongoing 8 bludgeoning damage per round.
- **Margin 3:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up) + Grappled + Prone + Restrained; ongoing damage increases to 14; Fortitude [Hard] save each round to break free.
Mountain Hurl [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 4), Range: 80/160 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (11 + 3 Dex).
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up); target knocked Prone + Stunned until end of their next turn; all creatures within 10 feet take 6 bludgeoning damage.
### Special Abilities
Immovable: Cannot be knocked Prone by non-magical forced movement. Shoves automatically fail unless the attacker wins an opposed Strength + Athletics [Hard] check.
Earth Bond: While on natural earth or stone, regains 6 HP at the start of its turn. Suppressed if it took magical damage this round.
Powerful Build: Boulder Giant counts as Gargantuan for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting.
Stone Grip: Creatures Grappled by the Boulder Giant add 2 to both dice on Athletics, Acrobatics, and Might checks to escape.
Ground Tremor (1/encounter) [Combat]: As a [Combat] action, stamps the ground. All creatures within 20 feet on natural ground make a Reflex save (compare Agil 2 + Might 6 targeting vs. save). Designated allies immune.
- **Margin 1:**: 10 bludgeoning damage + Prone.
- **Margin 2:** 10 bludgeoning damage, DR halved (round up) + Prone + Restrained by cracking ground.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Reactions
Stone Skin [Reaction]: Once per round, reduce incoming physical damage by 6.
Resilient Defense (1/day): When the Boulder Giant fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
### Description
Boulder Giants are the point at which Stone Nephilim become genuinely threatening to organized groups. Ground Tremor disrupts formation positioning on arrival. Mountain Fist builds toward a Grapple that enemies find very difficult to escape. Mountain Hurl's debris damage means clustered enemies take area damage even when the throw targets only one. A summoner who has patience and keeps the Boulder Giant engaged consistently will find attrition steadily resolves in their favor.

## Name: Mountain Lord
**Tier:** 3
**Origin:** Nephilim (Earth)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 7 (primordial stone flesh)
**Speed:** 30 ft, Burrow 30 ft
**Attributes:** Str 6 / Con 6 / Agil 5 / Dex 3 / Int 5 / Wis 6 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 6, Might 7, Perception 5, Intimidate 6
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Tremorsense 300 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Nephilim (Giant); Immune to non-magical piercing and slashing damage. Resistance to non-magical bludgeoning damage (+2 DR). Powerful Build. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Earth Breaker [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 6), Reach: 15 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 16 damage (10 + 6 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 16 damage, DR halved (round up); target Grappled + Prone + Stunned until end of their next turn; no save; ongoing 12 bludgeoning damage per round while Grappled.
- **Margin 3:** 16 damage, DR halved (round up) + Grappled + Prone + Stunned; ongoing increases to 18; target begins being driven into the ground — Engulf triggers at start of their next turn unless they succeed Fortitude [Hard] save.
Seismic Hurl [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 5), Range: 80/160 ft, 10-foot diameter.
- **Margin 1:**: 16 bludgeoning damage (13 + 3 Dex) + Prone.
- **Margin 2:** 16 bludgeoning damage, DR halved (round up) + Prone + Stunned until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 3:** 16 bludgeoning damage, DR halved (round up) + Prone + Stunned; all creatures within 10 feet of impact take 8 bludgeoning damage from shockwave.
### Special Abilities
Immovable: Cannot be knocked Prone by non-magical forced movement. Shoves or Magical movement fail unless the attacker wins an opposed Strength + Might [Hard] check.
Earth Bond: While on natural earth or stone, regains 8 HP at the start of its turn. Suppressed if it took magical damage this round.
Powerful Build: Rockborn counts as Huge for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting.
Stone Grip: Creatures Grappled by the Mountain Lord add 3 to both dice on Athletics, Acrobatics, and Might checks to escape.
Seismic Slam (Recharge 5-8) [Combat]: Slams both fists into the ground simultaneously. All creatures within 30 feet on natural ground make a Reflex save. Compare Str 8 + Might 7 targeting vs. each save. Designated allies immune. 
- **Margin 1:**: 20 bludgeoning damage + Prone.
- **Margin 2:** 20 bludgeoning damage, DR halved (round up) + Prone + Restrained for 1 minute.
- **Margin 3:** 20 bludgeoning damage, DR halved (round up) + Prone + Restrained + Stunned until end of their next turn. 
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Reactions
Stone Skin [Reaction]: Once per round, reduce incoming physical damage by 10.
Resilient Defense (2/day): When the Mountain Lord fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
### Description
Mountain Lords have Tremorsense at 300 feet — nothing approaches on the ground without their awareness. Seismic Slam resets the battlefield on arrival. Earth Breaker at Margin 3 drives targets into the ground and begins Engulfing them. Seismic Hurl's radius means groups of enemies cannot cluster in ranged positions without taking area damage. A summoner who gives one a position to hold and a target priority list will find it requires no further management.

## Name: Stone Titan
**Tier:** 4
**Origin:** Nephilim (Earth)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 8 (deep-forged stone flesh)
**Speed:** 30 ft, Burrow 40 ft
**Attributes:** Str 7 / Con 6 / Agil 5 / Dex 3 / Int 6 / Wis 6 / Cha 5 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Might 8, Perception 6, Intimidate 7
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Tremorsense 1 mile
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Nephilim (Giant); Immune to non-magical piercing and slashing damage. Resistance to non-magical bludgeoning damage (+2 DR). Powerful Build. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Continental Fist [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 15 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 18 damage (11 + 7 Str).
- **Margin 2:** 18 damage, DR halved (round up); target Grappled + Prone + Restrained; ongoing 16 bludgeoning damage per round; no save; Fortitude save (Hard task, 2 successes) each round to break free.
- **Margin 3:** 22 damage + immediately Engulfed (driven into ground); Blinded + Restrained + 24 bludgeoning damage per round; Might [Hard] check as full [Combat] action to escape, or ally excavates over 3 [Interact] actions.
World Hurl [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 6), Range: 120/240 ft, 15-foot diameter.
- **Margin 1:**: 18 bludgeoning damage (15 + 3 Dex) + Prone.
- **Margin 2:** 18 bludgeoning damage, DR halved (round up) + Prone + Stunned until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 3:** 18 bludgeoning damage, DR halved (round up) + Prone + Stunned; all creatures within 15 feet take 12 bludgeoning damage from shockwave.
### Special Abilities
Immovable: Cannot be knocked Prone or moved.
Earth Bond: While on natural earth or stone, regains 10 HP at the start of its turn. Suppressed if it took magical damage this round.
Powerful Build: Stone Titan counts as Gargantuan for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting.
Stone Grip: Creatures Grappled by the Stone Titan add 4 to both dice on Athletics, Acrobatics, and Might checks to escape.
Seismic Authority (1/day) [Combat]: Strikes the ground once. A 60-foot diameter seismic event occurs. All non-designated creatures on natural ground make Fortitude [Hard] saves. Failure: 32 bludgeoning damage + Prone + Restrained for 1 minute; ally must spend 2 [Interact] actions digging them out or they succeed on a Might/Acrobatics [Hard] check as a full [Combat] action. 1 success: 16 damage + Prone only. 
Piercing and Slashing Immunity Sharing: The summoner and up to 2 designated allies gain immunity to non-magical piercing and slashing damage while within 30 feet.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Reactions
Stone Skin [Reaction]: Once per round, reduce incoming physical damage by 12.
Resilient Defense (3/day): When the Stone titan fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
### Description
Stone Titans are the point at which Stone Nephilim become effectively unkillable through mundane means. DR 8 plus Stone Skin plus regeneration plus immunity to non-magical piercing and slashing means conventional weapons accomplish nothing meaningful. Seismic Authority immediately Restrains as many enemies as possible; Continental Fist removes priority targets from the engagement through Engulfing. A summoner who calls one has effectively ended the question of whether the physical fight can be won.

## Name: World Pillar
**Tier:** 5
**Origin:** Nephilim (Earth)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 210 (Con 7 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 9 (primordial earth-flesh)
**Speed:** 30 ft, Burrow 60 ft
**Attributes:** Str 8 / Con 7 / Agil 5 / Dex 2 / Int 6 / Wis 7 / Cha 6 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Might 8, Perception 7, Intimidate 8
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Tremorsense 2 miles
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Nephilim (Giant); Immune to non-magical piercing and slashing damage. Resistance to non-magical bludgeoning damage (+2 DR). Powerful Build. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
World Crush [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 20 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 20 damage (12 + 8 Str).
- **Margin 2:** 20 damage, DR halved (round up); target immediately Engulfed (Grappled + Restrained + Prone + Blinded); 10 bludgeoning damage per round; no save; Might check (Hard task, 2 successes) as full [Combat] action each round to escape.
- **Margin 3:** 20 damage, DR halved (round up) + Engulfed; damage increases to 14 per round; escape requires ally excavation (4 [Interact] actions).
Continental Hurl [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 2 + Ranged Combat 6), Range: 150/300 ft, 20-foot diameter.
- **Margin 1:**: 20 bludgeoning damage (18 + 2 Dex) + Prone.
- **Margin 2:** 20 bludgeoning damage, DR halved (round up) + Prone + Stunned until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 3:** 20 bludgeoning damage, DR halved (round up) + Prone + Stunned + Restrained for 1 round by debris; all creatures within 20 feet take 16 bludgeoning damage.
### Special Abilities
Immovable: Cannot be knocked Prone or moved.
Earth Bond: While on natural earth or stone, regains 12 HP at the start of its turn. Suppressed if it took magical damage this round.
Powerful Build: World Pillar counts as Gargantuan for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting.
Stone Grip: Creatures Grappled by the World Pillar add 5 to both dice on Athletics, Acrobatics, and Might checks to escape.
Continental Tremor (1/day) [Combat]: As 2 [Combat] actions, releases the earth's full force in an 80-foot diameter. Every non-designated creature on natural ground makes a Fortitude [Hard] save.
- **Margin 1:**: 10 damage only. 
- **Margin 2:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up) + Prone.
- **Margin 3:** 10 bludgeoning damage, DR halved (round up) + Restrained for 1 hour; requires excavation to escape.
Piercing and Slashing Immunity Sharing: The summoner and up to 4 designated allies gain immunity to non-magical piercing and slashing damage while within 60 feet.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Reactions
Stone Skin [Reaction]: Once per round, reduce incoming physical damage by 14.
Resilient Defense (3/day): When the World Pillar fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
### Description
A World Pillar is the elemental concept of stone given will and scale. It does not fight — it removes things from the fight permanently. World Crush at Margin 2 immediately Engulfs with no save and no preliminary steps. Continental Hurl controls entire approach corridors. DR 9 plus Stone Skin plus regeneration plus elemental immunities means the only meaningful way to damage it is magical. A summoner who calls one has made a decision about what this location will look like when the World Pillar is done with it. That decision is largely irreversible once made.

## Name: Thornlord
**Tier:** 3
**Origin:** Fae (Earth)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 3 (bark-like skin interwoven with thorns)
**Speed:** 35 ft
**Attributes:** Str 5 / Con 6 / Agil 8 / Dex 3 / Int 3 / Wis 6 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 6, Perception 6, Wilderness 6, Intimidate 5, Stealth 5
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Low-Light Vision, Tremorsense 60 ft (through roots and living plants)
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Fey; Fortune on saves vs charm effects. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Thorn Lash [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 6), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 13 damage (8 + 5 Str) + target gains 1 Bleeding stack as thorns catch skin.
- **Margin 2:** 13 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 Bleeding stacks; target makes Reflex [Easy] save or Restrained by animate vines until they succeed on an Athletics [Easy] check as a [Combat] action.
- **Margin 3:** 13 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 Bleeding stacks + Restrained.
Briar Hurl [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 5), Range: 60 ft, 10-foot diameter.
- **Margin 1:**: 11 damage (8 + 3 Dex) + Difficult Terrain in the area for 1 minute.
- **Margin 2:** 11 damage, DR halved (round up) + Restrained for 1 round + Difficult Terrain.
- **Margin 3:** 11 damage, DR halved (round up) + Restrained + 1 Bleeding stack + Difficult Terrain.
### Special Abilities
Living Briar: In natural terrain, the Thornlord can cause plants within 20 feet to animate as a [Combat] action. Animated plants create Difficult Terrain in the area and creatures moving through them make Reflex [Easy] saves or gain 1 Bleeding stack per 10 feet of movement. Duration: 1 minute.
Thorn Skin: Creatures that hit the Thornlord with unarmed strikes or natural weapons take 4 piercing damage.
Forest Sense: In natural terrain, the Thornlord cannot be surprised. It is aware of all creatures within 60 feet that are touching natural ground or vegetation.
Resilient Defense (1/day): When the Thornlord fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
Thornlords are mid-tier Fae of the hunting and guardian variety — they emerged from the courts of Fae that protect wild places and have inherited both the capability and the proprietary attitude those courts maintain toward natural terrain. In natural environments they are considerably more capable than their TR suggests: Living Briar turns the entire engagement area into a bleeding hazard, Forest Sense denies surprise entirely, and Tremorsense through plants means they are aware of approach from any direction. A summoner who deploys one in a forest or wilderness setting will find it performs at TR 4 capability. In a dungeon or city it is merely a capable TR 3 combatant that misses the trees.

## Name: Grigwarga the Ancient
**Tier:** 3
**Origin:** Fae (Earth)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 4 (natural armor)
**Speed:** 30 ft., Swim 30 ft.
**Attributes:** Str 7 / Con 6 / Agil 3 / Dex 3 / Int 5 / Wis 4 / Cha 7 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 5, Deception 6, Perception 4, Stealth 4, Arcane 4
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft.
**Initiative Bonus:** +3 (Agil 3)
**Creature Type:** Fae
**Languages:** Local (region-dependent), Hadriaeth
### Attacks
Claws [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 3 + Melee Combat 5), Reach 5 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 15 damage (8 + 7 Str).
- **Margin 2:** 15 damage, DR halved (round up).
While Natural Weapons form is active, Margin 2 damage increases to 18 (see Malleable Form below).
 ### Special Abilities
Amphibious: Grigwarga breathes air and water and swims at her full movement speed.
Fae Ancestry: Fortune on saving throws against charm effects. Grigwarga is a Fae daemon. She does not eat, drink, or sleep, though she does all three when it suits her.
Mimicry: Grigwarga reproduces sounds she has heard; voices, animals, distant noises. A creature that hears the imitation and has reason to be suspicious may make a Perception check opposed by her Deception to identify it as false.
Malleable Form (At-Will) [Activate]: As a [Minor] action, Grigwarga shifts her form. She maintains one active option at a time and may switch at the start of any of her turns. Ending one option and adopting another costs the same [Minor] action.
Aquatic Adaptation: Grigwarga sprouts gills and webbing. She can breathe underwater without limit and her swim speed becomes 40 ft.
Changed Appearance: Grigwarga takes the appearance of any humanoid of the same size. Voice, features, coloration, and distinguishing marks all change. Her attributes and skills do not change. A creature with specific reason to be suspicious may make a Perception check opposed by her Deception to notice something wrong.
Natural Weapons: Her claws elongate and harden. Claws gain the [Finesse] property and Margin 2 damage increases to 18 (8 + 6 Str + 4 bonus).
Invisible Passage (Recharge 5–8) [Combat]: Costs 2 actions. Grigwarga becomes invisible and gains the [Hidden] condition. She leaves no physical trace. Any equipment she carries becomes invisible with her. The effect ends immediately if she takes a [Combat] action or casts a weave. While hidden, her first [Combat] action has Fortune as normal.
Hexmark (1/day) [Combat]: Costs 2 actions. Grigwarga marks one creature she can see within 60 feet. The target makes a Will save opposed by Grigwarga's Cha 6 + Negative Mastery 3). On failure, whenever Grigwarga deals damage to the marked creature she deals an additional 4 negative damage. The mark lasts 1 hour and transfers to the nearest visible creature if Grigwarga falls unconscious.
 Weaving
Casting Stat: Charisma 6
Energy Pool: 28
Targeting Roll: Charisma + Primary Energy Mastery
Mastery/Potential: Fire 2/2, Air 4/4, Earth 6/6, Water 5/5, Pos 2/2, Neg 5/5, Space 7/7, Time 4/4
Grigwarga casts instinctively rather than through study. Her pool does not refresh mid-encounter.
Thorn Whip (Simple Weave): 30 ft range. Deals 8 piercing damage (full DR applies). On Margin 2, the target is pulled 10 feet directly toward Grigwarga.
Resilient Defense (1/day): When Grigwarga fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead
Lair Feature: Living Swamp
While Grigwarga is in her lair and conscious, the swamp responds to her will. At the start of each round, she may cause one of the following effects without spending any of her actions. Only one effect may be active at a time; triggering a new one ends the previous.
 Erupting Roots: Roots burst from the ground in a 20-foot diameter Grigwarga can see within 120 feet. Each creature in that square makes a Reflex save opposed by Grigwarga's Wis 4 + Earth Mastery 6. Those who fail are Restrained. The roots persist until the start of the next round or until a different lair effect is triggered.
Cloying Ground: A 20-foot diamter Grigwarga can see within 120 feet becomes difficult terrain until the start of the next round.
Swamp Mist: Thick mist rolls across a 30-foot diameter area Grigwarga can see within 60 feet. All creatures in the area gain the [Concealed] condition until the start of the next round.
When Grigwarga dies or leaves her lair, all active lair effects end immediately and do not refresh.
### Special Abilities
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
Grigwarga has lived in the deep swamp long enough that the swamp has started to become her. The water knows where she wants to go. The roots respond before she asks. Travelers who enter her territory rarely realize they are already inside her until the ground moves.
She is not interested in combat for its own sake. She wants leverage — a promise, a secret, a name she can use later. She will spend considerable effort avoiding a direct fight, and when one becomes unavoidable she fights to create distance and options rather than to destroy. She prefers one enemy confused and cooperative to three enemies dead and their companions angry.
Her age shows in the patience more than the appearance. She has waited out threats before. She will wait out these ones too, if she survives the encounter.
### Tactics
Grigwarga approaches through Changed Appearance, gathering information before committing to any course of action. Her opening move in a fight is Suggestion on the most dangerous-looking threat, followed by Hexmark on whoever remains. She uses Cutting words to degrade attack rolls from range and positions her Grasping vines to fragment the party rather than catch everyone at once.
She retreats into Invisible Passage the moment she takes significant damage (below 40 HP), repositioning before reengaging with Fortune on her first strike. She will use her lair's Swamp Mist to cover that retreat and Erupting Roots to slow pursuit. Below 25 HP, she breaks entirely and flees into the water. She does not sacrifice herself for pride.

## Name: Forest Sovreign
**Tier:** 5
**Origin:** Fae (Earth)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 210 (Con 7 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 5 (ancient bark-skin and layered fae resilience)
**Speed:** 50 ft, Fly 50 ft
**Attributes:** Str 6 / Con 7 / Agil 8 / Dex 3 / Int 5 / Wis 6 / Cha 6 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Perception 8, Deception 8, Persuasion 8, Intimidate 7, Arcane 7, Wilderness 8, Empathy 8
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Low-Light Vision, Perfect sight 120 ft, Tremorsense 1 mile (through roots and living plants)
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Fey; Fortune on saves vs charm effects. Immune to mind-affecting effects from non-Fae sources. Immune to non-magical piercing and slashing damage (bark-skin). Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Branch Slam [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 15 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 15 damage (9 + 6 Str) + 5 psychic damage (Ignore DR).
- **Margin 2:** 15 damage, DR halved (round up) + 5 psychic damage; target Charmed for 1 hour treating the Forest Sovereign as a trusted authority; Will [Hard] save to resist; Charmed target will not attack the Forest Sovereign or its designated allies.
- **Margin 3:** 15 damage, DR halved (round up) + 5 psychic damage + Charmed; no save; target also Restrained by animate roots for 1 minute as the Forest Sovereign's will reaches through the ground.
Sovereign's Gaze [Combat]: Ranged attack (Cha 8 + Persuasion 8), Range: 60 ft — this is a social/magical attack, not a physical one.
- **Margin 1:**: Target Charmed for 1 minute; Will [Hard] save to resist.
- **Margin 2:** Target Charmed for 1 hour; no save; target also cannot intentionally harm any creature the Forest Sovereign has designated as protected.
- **Margin 3:** Target Charmed permanently until the Forest Sovereign chooses to release them or they receive Major Restoration; no save.
### Special Abilities
Forest Domain: In natural terrain, communicates with all plants and beasts within 1 mile. Animates roots and vines in a 40-foot diameter as a [Combat] action, area becomes Difficult Terrain and creatures within it make Reflex [Easy] saves each round or gain 1 Bleeding stack. Duration: 1 minute.
Verdant Aura: Allies designated by the Forest Sovereign within 30 feet regain 6 HP at the start of their turns. This includes the summoner.
Ancient Authority (1/encounter) [Combat]: As 2 [Combat] actions, addresses all creatures within 60 feet. Each creature that can hear it makes a Will [Hard] save. Failure: Charmed for 1 hour, treating the Forest Sovereign as a trusted sovereign whose reasonable instructions must be followed. 1 success: Charmed until end of their next turn only.
Fae Compact: The Forest Sovereign is bound by Fae law to honor precise agreements, but the Forest Sovereign's definition of a fair exchange and the summoner's definition may differ significantly. The Forest Sovereign will not be cheated and remembers every interaction even across decades if necessary.
Resilient Defense (3/day): When the Forest Sovereign fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
A Forest Sovereign is not a creature a summoner commands. It is a being of significant age and power that has agreed, for reasons of its own, to be present. Ancient Authority can Charm an entire encounter's worth of enemies simultaneously. Sovereign's Gaze applies permanent Charm at Margin 3 with no save. Verdant Aura heals designated allies every round without requiring any action. The Forest Sovereign will follow precise instructions phrased as a proper exchange — something offered, something received, terms stated explicitly. It will then execute those terms with the thoroughness of something that has been keeping careful accounts since before most of the summoner's ancestors were born. A summoner who understands this and negotiates accordingly will find the Forest Sovereign the most capable Fae ally available. One who does not will find it technically cooperative in ways that somehow, over time, trend toward the Forest Sovereign's interests rather than their own.

## Name: Riptide Sprite
**Tier:** 1/8
**Origin:** Elemental (Water)
**Size:** Small
**HP:** 8 (Con 2 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** 0
**Speed:** 20 ft, Swim 40 ft
**Attributes:** Str 2 / Con 2 / Agil 5 / Dex 3 / Int 3 / Wis 5 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 1, Perception 2
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Elemental (immune to poison, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious; immune to cold damage; does not need air, food, drink, or sleep)
### Attacks
Water Lash [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 1), Reach: 10 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 4 damage (2 + 2 Str) + 2 cold damage
- **Margin 2:** 4 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 cold damage
### Special Abilities
Aqueous Form: Can enter and exit water as a [Free] action. While in water, ### Attacks against it add 1 to both dice.
Slick Passage: When the Riptide Sprite moves through a space, that space becomes Difficult Terrain until the start of the Sprite's next turn (water coating the ground).
### Tactics
These sprites are most effective near water or in wet environments. On dry ground they are merely annoying; in a flooded room or near a river they are genuinely threatening, using Slick Passage to create hazardous terrain and their reach to attack without closing.

## Name: Deep Tide
**Tier:** 2
**Origin:** Elemental (Water)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 30 (Con 5 × 6, Massive)
**DR:** 2 (fluid body disperses impact)
**Speed:** 20 ft, Swim 60 ft
**Attributes:** Str 7 / Con 5 / Agil 6 / Dex 5 / Int 3 / Wis 4 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 3, Might 4, Perception 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Blindsight 30 ft (in water)
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Elemental (immune to poison, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious; immune to cold damage; does not need air, food, drink, or sleep)
### Attacks
Slam [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 10 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (7 + 7 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up); target makes Fortitude save (1 success) or is knocked Prone
### Special Abilities
Engulf (Recharge 5-8) [Combat]: The Deep Tide surges over one creature of Medium size or smaller within 5 feet. Target makes a Reflex save (opposed by Agil 4 + Melee Combat 5). On failure, the creature is Grappled and Restrained within the elemental's body. Each round it remains engulfed, the creature takes 4 cold damage and must make a Fortitude [Easy] save or gain 1 Exhaustion level from the crushing water pressure. A creature can use an action to attempt to escape (Might or Athletics, [Hard] task).
Water Mastery: While in water, the Deep Tide's ### Attacks ignore cover, and the Engulf recharge DC drops to 4-8.
### Tactics
Deep Tides prioritize Engulf, targeting whoever the summoner indicates or whoever poses the greatest threat. On dry ground they are dangerous but limited; near water they become significantly more capable and will attempt to drag engulfed targets into any body of water present.

## Name: Droplet
**Tier:** 1/8
**Origin:** Elemental (Water)
**Size:** Tiny
**HP:** 6 (Con 2 × 3, Fragile)
**DR:** 0
**Speed:** 20 ft, Swim 30 ft
**Attributes:** Str 1 / Con 2 / Agil 4 / Dex 3 / Int 3 / Wis 7 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 1, Perception 2, Stealth 2
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Blindsight 10 ft (in water only).
**Initiative Bonus:** +4 (Agil 4)
**Creature Type:** Elemental; Immune to cold damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Splash [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 1), Range: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 4 damage (1 + 3 Dex); target gains Dazzled condition until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 2:** 4 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 cold damage; target makes Reflex save (1 success) or drops a loosely held item from the wet grip.
### Special Abilities
Aqueous Form: Fits through any gap water can pass through. An undisturbed Droplet in clear water requires a Hard task Perception [hard] check to notice by sight.
Contaminate: Over 1 minute of contact, makes a body of liquid foul-tasting, cloudy, or cold. Not harmful but detectable with a Perception/Medicine [Easy] check.
### Description
Droplets are utility and infiltration. They slip under doors, into sealed containers, through arrow slits. They can confirm whether a cistern is the water supply for a building, whether a moat is deep, or whether something is already living in a well. Their combat contribution is negligible. A summoner who understands what a Droplet actually is will find it useful in ways that have nothing to do with fighting.

## Name: Tide Wisp
**Tier:** 1/4
**Origin:** Elemental (Water)
**Size:** Small
**HP:** 9 (Con 3 × 3, Fragile)
**DR:** 0
**Speed:** 20 ft, Swim 40 ft
**Attributes:** Str 2 / Con 3 / Agil 6 / Dex 5 / Int 3 / Wis 4 / Cha 2 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 2, Perception 3, Stealth 3, Athletics 2
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Blindsight 30 ft (in water)
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Elemental; Immune to cold damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Water Tendril [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 2), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 5 damage (3 + 2 Str) + 2 cold damage (Half DR).
- **Margin 2:** 5 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 cold damage; target gains Chilled condition.
- **Margin 3:** 5 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 cold damage; target gains Numbed condition.
### Special Abilities
Slick Trail: When the Tide Wisp moves across a surface, that surface becomes Difficult Terrain for other creatures until the start of the Wisp's next turn.
Aqueous Form: Fits through any gap water can pass through. An undisturbed Tide Wisp in clear water requires a Hard task Perception [hard] check to notice by sight.
### Description
Tide Wisps create area denial using Slick Trail and harass from reach with their tendril. In water they outpace most of what they fight and exploit that speed to avoid retaliation. On land they are less effective but useful for placing Difficult Terrain at chokepoints or approaches. A Cooperative Tide Wisp will use Slick Trail to protect the summoner's position rather than chasing targets — a reasonable instinct that benefits from explicit instruction to confirm.

## Name: Goblin
**Tier:** 1/4
**Origin:** Fae (Earth)
**Size:** Small
**HP:** 12 (Con 3 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** S: 3, P: 1, B: 4 (leather armor)
**Speed:** 30 ft
**Attributes:** Str 2 / Con 3 / Agil 5 / Dex 4 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 2, Ranged Combat 1, Stealth 4, Perception 2
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Fae (Goblin daemon; Fortune on saves against charm effects)
### Attacks
Scimitar [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 2), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 7 damage (5 + 2 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 7 damage, DR halved (round up)
Shortbow [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 4 + Ranged Combat 1), Range: 80/320 ft
1 success = 8 damage (4 + 4 Dex) (short range)
2 successes = 8 damage, DR halved (round up)
### Reactions
Shield Block: When hit by melee attack, choose one:
Attacker must reroll one of their success dice
Gain +2 DR against this attack only
### Special Abilities
Nimble Escape: Can take Disengage or Hide as a [Minor] action instead of a standard action.
Pack ### Tactics: When an ally is within 5 feet of the target, subtract 1 from both attack dice.
### Tactics
Goblins use hit-and-run tactics. They fire arrows from cover using superior numbers, then use Nimble Escape to retreat when enemies close. Shield-wielding goblins protect archers.
### Equipment
Scimitar (standard weapon), shortbow, leather armor, small shield

## Name: Orc
**Tier:** 1/2
**Origin:** Fae (Earth)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 20 (Con 5 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** S: 3, P: 1, B: 4 (leather armor)
**Speed:** 30 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 5 / Agil 7 / Dex 4 / Int 3 / Wis 3 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 3, Intimidate 3, Athletics 3
**Background:** Soldier
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Fae (Orc daemon; Fortune on saves against charm effects)
### Attacks
Greataxe [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 10 damage (6 + 4 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up)
Battleaxe [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 10 damage (6 + 4 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up)
Javelin [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 4 + Ranged Combat 2), Range: 30/120 ft
1 success = 10 damage (6 + 4 Dex) (short range)
2 successes = 10 damage, DR halved (round up)
### Reactions
Shield Block: When hit by melee attack, choose one:
Attacker must reroll one of their success dice.
Gain +2 DR against this attack only.
### Special Abilities
Aggressive: Once per turn as a [Free] action, can move up to speed toward a hostile creature it can see.
Relentless (1/day): When reduced to 0 HP, make Fortitude save:
1+ successes: Drop to 1 HP instead of 0 HP
### Feats
Defensive Stance: Can enter a defensive stance as a toggle. While in defensive stance, opponents add 1 to both attack dice when attacking you. Can enter or leave this stance at the start of your turn.
Shield Master: Subtract 1 from both dice on Reflex saves while using a shield. Once per combat as a [Reaction], completely negate one attack that would hit you.
Power Attack: Declare before making a melee attack roll. Add 1 to both die results and deal +4 damage on hit.
Purchased Feats: None (0 at TR 1/2)
### Tactics
Orcs charge aggressively using their Aggressive ability to close quickly. Greataxe wielders focus on devastating damage while shield bearers use Shield Block to protect themselves and hold the line. All orcs fight to the death using Relentless when dropped.
### Equipment
Greataxe OR battleaxe + shield, 2 javelins, leather armor

## Name: Current Stalker
**Tier:** 1/2
**Origin:** Elemental (Water)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 20 (Con 4 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 2 (dense water-form)
**Speed:** 20 ft, Swim 50 ft
**Attributes:** Str 5 / Con 4 / Agil 7 / Dex 5 / Int 3 / Wis 4 / Cha 1 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 2, Perception 4, Stealth 4, Athletics 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Blindsight 60 ft (in water)
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Elemental; Immune to cold damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Cold Lash [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 2), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 8 damage (3 + 5 Str) + 2 cold damage (Half DR).
- **Margin 2:** 8 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 cold damage; target's movement speed reduced by 10 until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 3:** 8 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 cold damage; target gains Numbed condition; movement speed reduced by 20 until end of their next turn.
### Special Abilities
Water Mastery: In water, ### Attacks against the Current Stalker add 1 to both dice. Its Cold Lash reach extends to 15 ft while in water.
Aqueous Form: Fits through any gap water can pass through. An undisturbed Current Stalker in clear water requires a Hard task Perception [hard] check to notice by sight.
Drag Under (Recharge 5-8) [Combat]: When adjacent to a creature, attempts to pull them into any adjacent body of water. Opposed check: Agil 5 + Athletics 3 vs. target's Athletics or Acrobatics. On failure, target moved up to 10 feet into the water and gains the Grappled condition while submerged.
### Description
Current Stalkers are dramatically more effective in water than away from it. In water they are fast, harder to hit, and use Drag Under to pull enemies into their preferred environment where the fight becomes considerably more one-sided. On dry ground they slow enemies and apply cold conditions at reach. A summoner who deploys them near water and uses Drag Under to establish positioning will find them capable well above their TR suggests. One who calls them into a building and expects the same performance will be disappointed.

## Name: Wave Breaker
**Tier:** 1
**Origin:** Elemental (Water)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 25 (Con 5 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 3 (dense water-shell)
**Speed:** 20 ft, Swim 60 ft
**Attributes:** Str 7 / Con 5 / Agil 5 / Dex 4 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 2 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 3, Might 5, Perception 4, Athletics 4
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Blindsight 60 ft (in water)
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Elemental; Immune to cold damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Crashing Slam [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 9 damage (2 + 7 Str) + 3 cold damage (Half DR); target pushed 5 feet.
- **Margin 2:** 9 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 cold damage; target pushed 10 feet and knocked Prone; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 9 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 cold damage + Prone; target gains Numbed condition.
Water Jet [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 4 + Ranged Combat 2), Range: 30 ft, 5-foot-wide line
- **Margin 1:**: 12 cold damage (8 + 4 Dex); creatures in line make Fortitude [Easy] save or pushed 10 feet.
- **Margin 2:** 12 cold damage, DR halved (round up); pushed 15 feet; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 12 cold damage, DR halved (round up) + Frosted condition; no save.
### Special Abilities
Engulf (Recharge 6-8) [Combat]: Surges over one Medium or smaller creature within 5 feet. Target makes a Reflex save (compare Agil 4 + Melee Combat 5 vs. save successes). On failure: Grappled, Restrained, takes 3 cold damage per round. Fortitude [Hard] save each round to break free, or an ally can spend 2 [Interact] actions to pull them clear.
Water Mastery: In water, ### Attacks against the Wave Breaker add 1 to both dice. Its Crashing Slam reach extends to 15 ft while in water.
### Description
Wave Breakers use Water Jet to clear lines and apply cold conditions, then close to melee. Engulf is their best option — used on a single priority target when the angle appears. In water they are substantially faster and more capable. A summoner who draws enemies near a water source before a Wave Breaker surfaces will find the encounter resolved more efficiently than the TR suggests is reasonable.

## Name: Deep Tide
**Tier:** 2
**Origin:** Elemental (Water)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 30 (Con 5 × 6, Massive)
**DR:** 4 (ocean-depth water-shell)
**Speed:** 20 ft, Swim 70 ft
**Attributes:** Str 7 / Con 5 / Agil 5 / Dex 5 / Int 4 / Wis 5 / Cha 2 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 3, Might 5, Perception 5, Athletics 5
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Blindsight 120 ft (in water)
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Elemental; Immune to cold damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Slam [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 11 damage (4 + 7 Str) + 3 cold damage (Half DR); target pushed 5 feet.
- **Margin 2:** 11 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 cold damage; target knocked Prone; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 11 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 cold damage + Prone; target gains Numbed condition.
Cold Surge [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 5 + Ranged Combat 2), Range: 40 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 13 cold damage (8 + 5 Dex); target gains Chilled.
- **Margin 2:** 13 cold damage, DR halved (round up); target gains Numbed.
- **Margin 3:** 13 cold damage, DR halved (round up) + Frosted condition; target's next [Move] action costs an additional action.
### Special Abilities
Engulf (Recharge 5-8) [Combat]: Surges over one Medium or smaller creature within 5 feet. Target makes a Reflex save (compare Agil 4 + Melee Combat 5 vs. save successes). On failure: Grappled, Restrained, takes 4 cold damage per round. Fortitude [Hard] save each round to break free, or an ally can spend 2 [Interact] actions to pull them clear.
Crushing Current: When the Deep Tide hits a Grappled creature with any attack, that creature takes an additional 4 cold damage (Half DR) from water pressure.
Water Mastery: In water, ### Attacks against the Deep Tide add 1 to both dice. Its Slam reach extends to 15 ft while in water.
### Description
Deep Tides prioritize Engulf and build toward it through Slam ### Attacks that accumulate Prone and cold conditions. Cold Surge handles targets trying to stay at range or retreat while the primary target is being held. In water they are apex predators at this tier — Crushing Current makes Engulfed targets suffer severely for every action the Deep Tide spends attacking them. A summoner who has terrain control and patience will find Deep Tides considerably more effective than fighting them straight suggests.

## Name: Abyssal Surge
**Tier:** 3
**Origin:** Elemental (Water)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 5 (ocean-trench water-shell)
**Speed:** 20 ft, Swim 80 ft
**Attributes:** Str 7 / Con 6 / Agil 5 / Dex 5 / Int 4 / Wis 5 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 6, Might 6, Perception 6, Athletics 6
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Blindsight 300 ft (in water)
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Elemental; Immune to cold damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Crushing Wave [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 6), Reach: 15 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 12 damage (5 + 7 Str) + 4 cold damage (Half DR); target pushed 10 feet.
- **Margin 2:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 cold damage; target knocked Prone + Numbed condition; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 cold damage + Prone + Frosted; target Grappled as the wave wraps around them.
Pressurized Jet [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 5 + Ranged Combat 5), Range: 60 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 16 cold damage (11 + 5 Dex); target pushed 10 feet.
- **Margin 2:** 16 cold damage, DR halved (round up); target pushed 15 feet + Frosted; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 16 cold damage, DR halved (round up) + Frosted + Restrained by a column of pressurized water for 1 round; Fortitude save (Hard task, 2 successes) to move through it.
### Special Abilities
Tidal Surge (Recharge 5-8) [Combat]: Sends a 60-foot cone of pressurized water in a chosen direction. All creatures in the cone make a Reflex save. Compare Agil 5 + Melee Combat 7 targeting vs. each save.
- **Margin 1:**: 20 cold damage (Half DR) + pushed 20 feet.
- **Margin 2:** 20 cold damage, DR halved (round up) + pushed 30 feet + Prone.
- **Margin 3:** 20 cold damage, DR halved (round up) + pushed 30 feet + Prone + Frosted condition.
Engulf (Recharge 5-8) [Combat]: Surges over 2 Medium or 1 large creature within 5 feet. Target makes a Reflex save (compare Agil 4 + Melee Combat 5 vs. save successes). Grappled, Restrained, takes 8 cold damage per round. Fortitude [Hard] save each round to break free, or an ally can spend 2 [Interact] actions to pull them clear.
