Water Mastery: In water, ### Attacks against the Abyssal Surge add 1 to both dice. Its Crushing Wave reach extends to 20 ft while in water.
Resilient Defense (2/day): When the Abyssal Surge fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
Abyssal Surges open with Tidal Surge to scatter enemy formations, then spend subsequent actions building Grapples through Crushing Wave while Pressurized Jet handles anything trying to disengage. Engulf removes priority targets from the engagement. In water, the Water Mastery suppression of Reflex saves makes them genuinely terrifying — everything takes full cold damage regardless of how well enemies roll. A summoner who deploys one near a sea, lake, or river should be aware that the Abyssal Surge will attempt to drag the fight into that water without prompting. That is simply what it is.

## Name: Ocean lord
**Tier:** 4
**Origin:** Elemental (Water)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 6 (deep-current water-shell)
**Speed:** 20 ft, Swim 100 ft
**Attributes:** Str 7 / Con 6 / Agil 5 / Dex 6 / Int 5 / Wis 5 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Might 7, Perception 7, Athletics 7
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Blindsight 1 mile (in water)
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Elemental; Immune to cold damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Abyssal Strike [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 15 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (7 + 7 Str) + 4 cold damage (Half DR); target pushed 15 feet + knocked Prone; no save.
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 cold damage; Prone + Frosted + Grappled; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 cold damage + Prone + Frosted + Grappled + Restrained; ongoing 16 cold damage per round while Grappled.
Pressurized Stream [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 6 + Ranged Combat 6), Range: 120 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 18 cold damage (12 + 6 Dex).
- **Margin 2:** 18 cold damage, DR halved (round up) + Frosted + Restrained by pressurized water column for 1 round.
- **Margin 3:** 18 cold damage, DR halved (round up) + Frozen condition; no save.
### Special Abilities
Maelstrom (1/encounter) [Combat]: As 2 [Combat] actions, creates a 40-foot diameter vortex of pressurized water. All creatures in range make Athletics or Acrobatics [Hard] checks or dragged 20 feet toward the center and knocked Prone. Creatures reaching the center are Grappled and take 9 cold damage per round. Creatures starting their turn in range must check again. Designated allies immune. Lasts 1 minute.
Water Mastery: In water, ### Attacks against the Ocean Lord add 1 to both dice. Its Abyssal Strike reach extends to 20 ft while in water. The Ocean Lord is immune to fire damage.
Engulf (Recharge 5-8) [Combat]: Surges over 2 Medium or 1 large creature within 5 feet. Target makes a Reflex save (compare Agil 4 + Melee Combat 5 vs. save successes). Grappled, Restrained, takes 9 cold damage per round. Fortitude [Hard] save each round to break free, or an ally can spend 2 [Interact] actions to pull them clear.
Resilient Defense (3/day): When the Ocean Lord fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
Ocean Lords open with Maelstrom to immediately cluster enemies, then use Abyssal Strike to lock specific targets into Grapples while the vortex handles everything else. Pressurized Stream freezes high-priority targets that manage to stay at range. A Friendly Ocean Lord coordinates complex engagement sequences without repeated instruction — it understands the shape of a fight well enough to anticipate what the summoner needs next. The Ocean Lord wants to pull everything into deep water. Giving it permission to pursue that agenda is simply a matter of telling it which targets matter most.

## Name: Primordial Wave
**Tier:** 5
**Origin:** Elemental (Water)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 210 (Con 7 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 7 (world-ocean shell)
**Speed:** 20 ft, Swim 120 ft
**Attributes:** Str 7 / Con 7 / Agil 5 / Dex 6 / Int 5 / Wis 6 / Cha 5 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Might 8, Perception 8, Athletics 8
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Blindsight 5 miles (in water)
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Elemental; Immune to cold damage, fire damage, poison damage, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
World Current [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 20 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 15 damage (8 + 7 Str) + 5 cold damage (Half DR); Prone + Frosted; no save.
- **Margin 2:** 15 damage, DR halved (round up) + 5 cold damage; Prone + Frozen + Grappled; ongoing 24 cold damage per round while Grappled.
- **Margin 3:** 15 damage, DR halved (round up) + 5 cold damage + Frozen + Grappled + immediately Engulfed; Blinded + Restrained + doubled ongoing damage to 48.
Ocean's Wrath [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 6 + Ranged Combat 6), Range: 200 ft, 20-foot diameter.
- **Margin 1:**: 20 cold damage (14 + 6 Dex).
- **Margin 2:** 20 cold damage, DR halved (round up) + Frosted.
- **Margin 3:** 20 cold damage, DR halved (round up) + Frozen condition; no save.
### Special Abilities
World Tide (1/day) [Combat]: As 2 [Combat] actions, calls a tidal surge across a 100-foot diameter. Terrain in the radius becomes Difficult Terrain for 1 hour. Every non-designated creature not flying makes a Fortitude [Hard] save.
- **Margin 1:**: 12 damage.  Creates enough water to flood a significant area on natural ground.
- **Margin 2:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + Prone.
- **Margin 3:** 12 cold damage, DR halved (round up) + Prone + Frozen for 1 hour.
Ocean Mastery: In water, the Primordial Wave is immune to all damage except Positive energy and magical fire. On land, normal immunities apply.
Cold Immunity Sharing: The summoner and up to 4 designated allies are immune to non-magical cold damage while within 60 feet.
Maelstrom (1/encounter) [Combat]: As 2 [Combat] actions, creates a 40-foot diameter vortex of pressurized water. All creatures in range make Athletics or Acrobatics [Hard] checks or dragged 20 feet toward the center and knocked Prone. Creatures reaching the center are Grappled and take 11 cold damage per round. Creatures starting their turn in range must check again. Designated allies immune. Lasts 1 minute.
Resilient Defense (3/day): When the Primordial Wave fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
A Primordial Wave at war is the ocean deciding to fight. World Tide resets the battlefield on arrival. World Current removes individuals with a single attack that Engulfs at Margin 3. Ocean's Wrath handles grouped formations at extreme range. In any environment with significant water it becomes effectively impossible to stop without overwhelming magical force — Water Mastery in that context grants near-total immunity to conventional damage. A summoner who calls one knows they are not deploying a creature. They are opening a door, and what comes through it intends to stay.

## Name: Frost Kin
**Tier:** 1/2
**Origin:** Nephilim (Water)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 20 (Con 4 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 3 (ice-dense flesh)
**Speed:** 30 ft (ignores movement penalties from ice and snow)
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 4 / Agil 6 / Dex 4 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 2, Athletics 3, Perception 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Nephilim (Giant); Immune to cold damage. Resistance to non-magical piercing and slashing damage (+2 DR). Powerful Build. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Ice Slam [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 2), Reach: 10 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 8 damage (4 + 4 Str) + 2 cold damage (Half DR); target gains Chilled condition.
- **Margin 2:** 8 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 cold damage; target gains Numbed condition; target's movement speed reduced by 10 until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 3:** 8 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 cold damage; target gains Frosted condition; movement speed reduced by 20 until end of their next turn.
Ice Shard [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 4 + Ranged Combat 1), Range: 30 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 10 cold damage (6 + 4 Dex); target gains Chilled.
- **Margin 2:** 10 cold damage, DR halved (round up); target gains Numbed; movement speed reduced by 10.
### Special Abilities
Frost Aura: Creatures starting their turn within 5 feet take 2 cold damage (Half DR) and gain the Chilled condition.
Powerful Build: Frost kin counts as Huge for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting.
Ice Stride: Moves across ice and snow at full speed. Creates a thin frost trail wherever it walks; surfaces within 5 feet of its path become Difficult Terrain for 1 minute.
### Description
Frost Kin begin the cold stacking process that defines Ice Nephilim at higher tiers. Even at TR 1/2, a target that takes two or three Ice Slam hits will be moving at significantly reduced speed and suffering stacking cold conditions. Ice Stride's Difficult Terrain trail makes repositioning around them increasingly costly. A summoner who keeps a Frost Kin engaged with a single target will find that target significantly impaired within two rounds without necessarily taking catastrophic damage — the goal is impairment, not elimination.

## Name: Iceborn
**Tier:** 1
**Origin:** Nephilim (Water)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 25 (Con 5 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 4 (thick ice-flesh)
**Speed:** 30 ft (ignores ice and snow movement penalties)
**Attributes:** Str 5 / Con 5 / Agil 6 / Dex 4 / Int 3 / Wis 5 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 3, Athletics 4, Perception 4, Intimidate 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Nephilim (Giant); Immune to cold damage. Resistance to non-magical piercing and slashing damage (+2 DR). Powerful Build. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Glacial Strike [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 10 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 9 damage (4 + 5 Str) + 3 cold damage (Half DR); target gains Numbed condition.
- **Margin 2:** 9 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 cold damage; target gains Frosted condition; movement speed halved until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 3:** 12 damage + 4 cold damage; target gains Frozen condition; Restrained until they succeed on a Might check (1 success) as a [Combat] action.
Frost Lance [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 4 + Ranged Combat 2), Range: 60 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 12 cold damage (8 + 4 Dex); target gains Numbed.
- **Margin 2:** 12 cold damage, DR halved (round up); target gains Frosted + movement speed halved until end of their next turn.
### Special Abilities
Frost Aura: Creatures starting their turn within 10 feet take 3 cold damage (Half DR) and gain the Chilled condition.
Powerful Build: Iceborn counts as Huge for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting.
Ice Stride: Moves across ice and snow at full speed. Creates a thin frost trail wherever it walks; surfaces within 5 feet of its path become Difficult Terrain for 1 minute.
Permafrost: Ground within 15 feet of the Iceborn where it has stood for at least 1 round becomes frozen. Frozen ground is Difficult Terrain and creatures moving across it make Acrobatics [Easy] checks or fall Prone.
### Description
Iceborn can apply the Frozen condition through Glacial Strike, which is the first Ice Nephilim capable of fully Restraining a target through cold alone. Permafrost spreads hazardous terrain passively as they move and stand, meaning the longer an engagement lasts the more difficult the battlefield becomes for anything that relies on movement. A summoner who gives one a target and space to maneuver will find the target increasingly unable to move by the second or third round of contact.

## Name: Glacier Giant
**Tier:** 2
**Origin:** Nephilim (Water)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 150 (Con 5 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 5 (ancient ice-flesh)
**Speed:** 35 ft (ignores ice and snow movement penalties)
**Attributes:** Str 6 / Con 5 / Agil 6 / Dex 5 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 5, Athletics 5, Perception 5, Intimidate 4
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Nephilim (Giant); Immune to cold damage. Resistance to non-magical piercing and slashing damage (+2 DR). Powerful Build. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Permafrost Fist [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 5), Reach: 15 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 11 damage (5 + 6 Str) + 3 cold damage (Half DR); target gains Frosted condition.
- **Margin 2:** 11 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 cold damage; target gains Frozen condition + Prone; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 11 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 cold damage + Frozen + Prone; target Grappled as ice encases their limbs; ongoing 8 cold damage per round while Grappled.
Glacier Hurl [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 5 + Ranged Combat 4), Range: 80/160 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 14 cold damage (9 + 5 Dex); target gains Frosted + movement halved.
- **Margin 2:** 14 cold damage, DR halved (round up); target gains Frozen + Prone; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 14 cold damage, DR halved (round up) + Frozen + Prone; target Restrained by ice impact for 1 round; Fortitude save (1 success) to move.
### Special Abilities
Frost Aura: Creatures starting their turn within 10 feet take 6 cold damage (Half DR) and gain the Numbed condition.
Powerful Build: Glacier Giant counts as Gargantuan for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting.
Ice Stride: Moves across ice and snow at full speed. Creates a thin frost trail wherever it walks; surfaces within 5 feet of its path become Difficult Terrain for 1 minute.
Permafrost: Ground within 20 feet of the Glacier Giant where it has stood for at least 1 round becomes frozen. Frozen ground is Difficult Terrain and creatures moving across it make Acrobatics [Easy] checks or fall Prone.
Resilient Defense (1/day): When the Glacier Giant fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
Glacier Giants apply Frozen reliably at Margin 2, which Restrains targets, and then Grapple at Margin 3 to add ongoing cold damage on top of the movement restriction. Glacier Hurl can apply Frozen at range, meaning they can immobilize high-priority targets before closing. The Frost Aura's Numbed condition on everything within 10 feet means enemies fighting in melee are taking stacking cold conditions from proximity alone. A summoner who keeps one engaged consistently will find the battlefield progressively frozen — literally — within a few rounds.

## Name: Tide Lord
**Tier:** 3
**Origin:** Nephilim (Water)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 6 (primordial ice-flesh)
**Speed:** 35 ft (ignores ice and snow movement penalties)
**Attributes:** Str 5 / Con 6 / Agil 7 / Dex 4 / Int 4 / Wis 5 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 6, Athletics 6, Perception 6, Intimidate 5
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Nephilim (Giant); Immune to cold damage. Resistance to non-magical piercing and slashing damage (+2 DR). Powerful Build. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Abyssal Freeze [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 6), Reach: 15 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 12 damage (7 + 5 Str) + 4 cold damage (Half DR); target gains Frosted + movement halved.
- **Margin 2:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 cold damage; target gains Frozen + Prone + Grappled; ongoing 12 cold damage per round; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 cold damage + Frozen + Prone + Grappled + Restrained; target begins Engulf — driven into ice at start of their next turn unless Fortitude [Hard] save.
Tidal Ice Lance [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 4 + Ranged Combat 5), Range: 100 ft, 5-foot-wide line.
- **Margin 1:**: 16 cold damage (12 + 4 Dex) + Frosted.
- **Margin 2:** 16 cold damage, DR halved (round up) + Frozen + movement halved.
- **Margin 3:** 16 cold damage, DR halved (round up) + Frozen + Prone; no save.
### Special Abilities
Glacial Aura: Creatures starting their turn within 15 feet take 8 cold damage (Half DR) and gain the Frosted condition. Creatures already Frosted within the aura gain the Frozen condition instead.
Powerful Build: Tide Lord counts as Gargantuan for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting.
Permafrost: Ground within 30 feet of the Glacier Giant where it has stood for at least 1 round becomes frozen. Frozen ground is Difficult Terrain and creatures moving across it make Acrobatics [Easy] checks or fall Prone.
Ice Stride: Moves across ice and snow at full speed. Creates a thin frost trail wherever it walks; surfaces within 5 feet of its path become Difficult Terrain for 1 minute.
Resilient Defense (2/day): When the Tide Lord fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
Tide Lords make the Glacial Aura genuinely punishing — Frosted creatures within 15 feet immediately become Frozen, which means any enemy who has taken one hit from the Tide Lord and remains adjacent is automatically Restrained by the aura alone. Tidal Ice Lance applies cold conditions across entire lines of enemies simultaneously. Abyssal Freeze at Margin 3 begins Engulfing, encasing targets in ice rather than earth. A summoner deploying one against a formation will find the formation frozen — in every literal and tactical sense — within two rounds of engagement.

## Name: Ice Titan
**Tier:** 4
**Origin:** Nephilim (Water)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 7 (ancient glacial flesh)
**Speed:** 40 ft (ignores ice and snow movement penalties)
**Attributes:** Str 6 / Con 6 / Agil 7 / Dex 3 / Int 6 / Wis 6 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Athletics 7, Perception 7, Intimidate 6
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Nephilim (Giant) — Immune to cold damage. Resistance to non-magical piercing and slashing damage (+2 DR). Powerful Build. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
World Freeze [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 15 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (8 + 6 Str) + 4 cold damage (Half DR); target Frozen + Prone; no save.
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 cold damage; Frozen + Prone + Grappled + Restrained; ongoing 16 cold damage per round; no save; Fortitude save (Hard task, 2 successes) each round to break free.
- **Margin 3:** 21 damage + 10 cold damage + immediately Engulfed in ice; Blinded + Restrained + ongoing 24 cold damage; Might/Acrobatics [Hard] check as full [Combat] action to escape, or ally must melt or break free (2 Interact actions with fire or 3 without).
Glacial Barrage [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 6), Range: 120 ft, up to 3 targets within 30 feet of each other.
- **Margin 1:**: 18 cold damage (15 + 3 Dex) + Frosted.
- **Margin 2:** 18 cold damage, DR halved (round up) + Frozen + movement halved.
- **Margin 3:** 18 cold damage, DR halved (round up) + Frozen + Prone; no save.
### Special Abilities
Absolute Zero Aura: Creatures starting their turn within 20 feet take 9 cold damage (Half DR). Frosted creatures in this range become Frozen. Frozen creatures in this range take an additional 8 cold damage (Half DR) from the intensifying cold.
Cold Immunity Sharing: The summoner and up to 3 designated allies gain immunity to non-magical cold damage within 60 feet.
Powerful Build: Ice Titan counts as Gargantuan for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting.
Permafrost: Ground within 40 feet of the Glacier Giant where it has stood for at least 1 round becomes frozen. Frozen ground is Difficult Terrain and creatures moving across it make Acrobatics [Easy] checks or fall Prone.
Ice Stride: Moves across ice and snow at full speed. Creates a thin frost trail wherever it walks; surfaces within 5 feet of its path become Difficult Terrain for 1 minute.
Resilient Defense (3/day): When the Ice Titan fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
Ice Titans apply Frozen on any hit at - **Margin 1:** — there is no preliminary Chilled or Numbed step. Absolute Zero Aura compounds existing cold conditions automatically, meaning Frozen enemies adjacent to the Ice Titan take additional damage from the aura every round on top of whatever the Titan is actively doing to them. Glacial Barrage applies Frozen to multiple targets simultaneously at range. A summoner who has a Cooperative Ice Titan targeting a formation will find that formation effectively unable to take offensive action within two rounds — too cold, too slow, too Restrained to threaten anyone who isn't also standing in the aura.

## Name: Worldsea Colossus
**Tier:** 5
**Origin:** Nephilim (Water)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 210 (Con 7 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 8 (primordial glacial flesh)
**Speed:** 40 ft (ignores ice and snow movement penalties)
**Attributes:** Str 7 / Con 7 / Agil 8 / Dex 3 / Int 5 / Wis 5 / Cha 6 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Athletics 8, Perception 8, Intimidate 7
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Blindsight 60 ft (cold detection)
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Nephilim (Giant); Immune to cold damage. Resistance to non-magical piercing and slashing damage (+2 DR). Powerful Build. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Glacial Apocalypse [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 20 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 15 damage (8 + 7 Str) + 5 cold damage (Half DR); target immediately Frozen + Prone; no save.
- **Margin 2:** 15 damage, DR halved (round up) + 5 cold damage; immediately Engulfed in ice; Blinded + Grappled + Restrained; ongoing 10 cold damage per round; no save; Might/Acrobatics [Hard] check as full [Combat] action each round to escape.
- **Margin 3:** 15 damage, DR halved (round up) + 5 cold damage + Engulfed; escape requires Might check with Misfortune, or ally intervention (4 [Interact] actions with fire, 6 without); ongoing damage increases to 14
World Ice Storm [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 6), Range: 200 ft, 30-foot diameter
- **Margin 1:**: 20 cold damage (17 + 3 Dex) + Frozen + Prone.
- **Margin 2:** 20 cold damage, DR halved (round up) + Frozen + Prone + Restrained for 1 round.
- **Margin 3:** 20 cold damage, DR halved (round up) + Frozen + Prone + Restrained + Stunned until end of their next turn; no save on any effect.
### Special Abilities
Primordial Cold Aura: Creatures starting their turn within 25 feet take 11 cold damage (Half DR). Frosted creatures become Frozen. Frozen creatures take additional 12 cold damage and become Restrained. Restrained creatures take additional 8 cold damage as ice continues accumulating.
World Glaciation (1/day) [Combat]: As 2 [Combat] actions, releases the cold of the world's deepest ice in an 80-foot diameter. All non-designated creatures make a Fortitude [Hard] save.
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage only. The radius becomes permanent permafrost until magically cleared.
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up) + Frozen for 1 minute.
- **Margin 3:** 14 cold damage, DR halved (round up) + Frozen for 1 hour + Restrained for 1 hour; escape requires fire or an ally's sustained effort.
Cold Immunity Sharing: The summoner and up to 6 designated allies gain immunity to non-magical cold damage within 60 feet.
Powerful Build: Worldsea Colossus counts as Gargantuan for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting.
Permafrost: Ground within 50 feet of the Glacier Giant where it has stood for at least 1 round becomes frozen. Frozen ground is Difficult Terrain and creatures moving across it make Acrobatics [Easy] checks or fall Prone.
Ice Stride: Moves across ice and snow at full speed. Creates a thin frost trail wherever it walks; surfaces within 5 feet of its path become Difficult Terrain for 1 minute.
Resilient Defense (3/day): When the Worldsea Colossus fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
A Worldsea Colossus does not freeze things. It converts the local environment into ice and then continues from there. World Glaciation on arrival creates permanent permafrost across an 80-foot diameter and Restrains everything within it for an hour. Primordial Cold Aura compounds cold conditions through multiple stages automatically — a Restrained enemy in the aura takes 34 cold damage per round from aura effects alone before the Colossus ### Attacks them. Glacial Apocalypse Engulfs at Margin 2 with no save. A summoner who calls one has determined that the fight ends in ice, and what the Worldsea Colossus determines is where the ice begins.
Water Fae


## Name: Wisp
**Tier:** 1/8
**Origin:** Fae (Water)
**Size:** Tiny
**HP:** 6 (Con 2 × 3, Fragile)
**DR:** 0
**Speed:** 0 ft, Fly 40 ft (Hover)
**Attributes:** Str 1 / Con 2 / Agil 6 / Dex 3 / Int 3 / Wis 4 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Perception 3, Stealth 4, Deception 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Low-Light Vision
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Fey; Fortune on saves vs charm effects. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Mote Sting [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 1), Reach: 5 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 2 damage (1 + 1 Str); target must make Will save (1 success) or become briefly distracted — adds 1 to one die on their next action.
- **Margin 2:** 2 damage, DR halved (round up); distraction applies with no save; target also drops any item they were holding loosely.
### Special Abilities
Blink Light: The Wisp can appear to be a torch, candle, or lantern to casual observation. Creatures actively examining it make a Perception [Hard] check to recognize it as something other than a light source. It can vary its brightness from dim to moderately bright at will.
Invisibility (At-Will): As a [Minor] action, the Wisp becomes invisible. Invisibility ends if it ### Attacks or uses Blink Light to produce bright illumination.
Lead Astray: When a creature is following the Wisp believing it to be a light source, the Wisp can guide them in any direction. The creature makes a Wisdom [Easy] check each minute to realize they are being led rather than following a fixed light. This ability only works on creatures who have not identified the Wisp as a Fae.
### Description
Wisps are navigational hazards given will. In wilderness or dungeon environments they lead travelers in circles, away from their destinations, or toward something the Wisp finds interesting — which may or may not align with the traveler's interests. A summoner who calls one is acquiring a scout with a strong editorial opinion about where scouting should happen and what constitutes relevant information. Precise instructions, carefully worded, will be followed. Vague instructions will be interpreted. The Wisp will report back whatever it decided was worth reporting.


## Name: Pixie
**Tier:** 1/8
**Origin:** Fae (Earth)
**Size:** Tiny
**HP:** 6 (Con 2 × 3, Fragile)
**DR:** 0
**Speed:** 20 ft, Fly 30 ft
**Attributes:** Str 1 / Con 2 / Agil 6 / Dex 4 / Int 3 / Wis 3 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Stealth 4, Perception 3, Deception 3, Thievery 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Low-Light Vision
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Fae (Fortune on saves vs charm effects; does not need air, food, drink, or sleep in the conventional sense)
### Attacks
Needle [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 1), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 2 damage (1 + 1 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 2 damage, DR halved (round up) + target makes Fortitude [Easy] save or gains Poisoned (Weak) for 1 hour.
### Special Abilities
Invisibility (At-Will): The Pixie can become invisible as a [Minor] action. The invisibility ends if it ### Attacks or casts a weave.
Natural Tiny: Has Fortune on Stealth checks.
Fairy Dust: Once per short rest, the Pixie can dust one willing creature or itself with faintly luminescent powder. The dusted creature gains Fortune on one saving throw of their choice within the next minute.
### Tactics
Pixies are scouts and mischief-makers. They avoid combat if possible, preferring to steal, distract, or mislead. A Cooperative Pixie makes an excellent spy. An Indifferent one might interpret "distract the guard" as "steal the guard's lunch and hide in his helmet."

## Name: Sprite
**Tier:** 1/4
**Origin:** Fae (Earth)
**Size:** Tiny
**HP:** 9 (Con 3 × 3, Fragile)
**DR:** 0
**Speed:** 20 ft, Fly 40 ft
**Attributes:** Str 1 / Con 3 / Agil 5 / Dex 5 / Int 3 / Wis 5 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Stealth 4, Perception 4, Ranged Combat 2, Wilderness 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Low-Light Vision **Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Fae (Fortune on saves vs charm effects)
### Attacks
Shortbow [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 5 + Ranged Combat 2), Range: 40/80 ft
1 success: 8 damage (3 + 5 Dex) + Fortitude [Easy] save or Poisoned (Weak) for 1 minute. (Short Range)
2 successes: 8 damage, DR halved (round up) + Fortitude [Easy] save or Poisoned (Weak) for 1 hour.
### Special Abilities
Heart Sight: Once per encounter, when the Sprite looks at a creature's eyes, it automatically discerns the creature's emotional state and whether it is lying. Will saves and Deception checks against the Sprite add 1 to both dice for creatures whose heart it has read.
Nature's Ally: The Sprite can communicate with beasts and plants as if they share a language.
### Description
Sprites are scouts and poison delivery systems. They shoot from concealment, apply poison conditions, and retreat before retaliation. Heart Sight makes them genuinely valuable as truth-detection assets — a summoner who needs to know whether an NPC is lying during a negotiation and has a Cooperative Sprite available has an advantage that most participants in the negotiation will not notice. Nature's Ally extends their intelligence-gathering to the natural environment. A summoner who understands what Sprites are good at will use them for information, not damage.
### Tactics
Sprites are excellent scouts and poison delivery systems. They rarely engage in melee, preferring to shoot from concealment. A Cooperative Sprite that has used Heart Sight can report back honestly on whether an NPC is trustworthy — one of the most genuinely useful things a low-tier summoner can get.

## Name: Boggart
**Tier:** 1/2
**Origin:** Fae (Air)
**Size:** Small
**HP:** 20 (Con 4 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 2 (thick hide)
**Speed:** 30 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 4 / Agil 6 / Dex 4 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 2, Stealth 4, Deception 3, Intimidate 3, Perception 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Low-Light Vision
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Fae (Fortune on saves vs charm effects)
### Attacks
Claws [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 2), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 9 damage (5 + 4 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 9 damage, DR halved (round up)
- **Margin 3:** 9 damage, DR halved (round up); target makes Will [Easy] save  or gains Frightened condition for 1 round.
### Special Abilities
Frightful Visage: As a [Combat] action, the Boggart distorts its face into something deeply wrong. One creature within 30 feet that can see it makes a Will save opposed by the Boggart's Cha 2 + Intimidate 3 (total 5). On failure, target gains the Frightened condition until end of their next turn.
Shadow Meld: In dim light or darkness, the Boggart has Fortune on Stealth checks.
Troublemaker: Once per encounter, the Boggart can spend 1 minute interacting with an unattended object to sabotage it in a minor way; a door latch that sticks, a buckle that releases under pressure, a torch that goes out at the worst moment. The GM determines when the sabotage activates. The Boggart cannot sabotage magical items.
### Description
Boggarts ambush from shadows, apply Frightened conditions through Frightful Visage to scatter formation fighters, then close for claw strikes on isolated targets. They are selfish and not suicidal and a Boggart that is clearly losing will attempt to flee. Troublemaker is their most distinctively Fae ability, producing minor environmental sabotage that compounds the chaos of an encounter in ways that are difficult to anticipate.
### Tactics
Boggarts prefer to ambush from shadows, using Frightful Visage to scatter formation fighters before closing. They are selfish but not suicidal — a Boggart that is clearly losing will attempt to flee. They make poor bodyguards and excellent shock troops for a single terrifying moment.

## Name: Redcap
**Tier:** 1
**Origin:** Fae (Air)
**Size:** Small **HP:** 40 (Con 5 × 8, Tough)
**DR:** S: 3, P: 2, B: 4 (iron-soled boots and dense body)
**Speed:** 35 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 5 / Agil 7 / Dex 5 / Int 3 / Wis 3 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 3, Intimidate 4, Athletics 3, Perception 2
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Low-Light Vision
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Fae (Fortune on saves vs charm effects)
### Attacks
Scythe [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 12 damage (8 + 4 Str) [Slashing]
- **Margin 2:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) [Slashing]; target gains 1 Bleeding stack
- **Margin 3:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up); target gains 2 Bleeding stacks
Boot Stomp [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 5 ft (only against Prone targets)
- **Margin 1:**: 12 damage (8 + 4 Str) [Bludgeoning]
- **Margin 2:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up); target is Pinned until the Redcap moves away
### Special Abilities
Blood Thirst: The Redcap's cap must be soaked in fresh blood or it loses its power. When the Redcap deals a Bleeding stack to any creature, it gains temporary HP equal to 4.
Iron Boots: The Redcap's kicks and stomps deal full damage to constructs that would otherwise be immune to bludgeoning.
### Description
Redcaps charge the most vulnerable target they can reach, stack Bleeding as quickly as possible, and stomp downed targets with their iron boots. 
### Tactics
Redcaps charge the most vulnerable target they can reach, stack Bleeding as fast as possible, and stomp downed targets with their boots. A summoner who points a Redcap at an enemy and steps back should not expect precision; they should expect enthusiasm.

## Name: Erlking's Scout
**Tier:** 2
**Origin:** Fae (Air)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 20 (Con 5 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** 2 (fae resilience)
**Speed:** 40 ft, Fly 20 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 5 / Agil 8 / Dex 4 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 5, Ranged Combat 4, Stealth 6, Perception 6, Wilderness 4
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Low-Light Vision, Truesight 30 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Fae (Fortune on saves vs charm effects)
### Attacks
Fae Blade [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 5), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 11 damage (7 + 4 Str) + 3 psychic damage (Ignore DR)
- **Margin 2:** 11 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 psychic damage
Hunter's Arrow [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 4 + Ranged Combat 4), Range: 80/160/320 ft
1 success: 12 damage (8 + 4 Dex) (short range)
2 successes: 12 damage, DR halved (round up); target cannot benefit from invisibility or the Hidden condition for 1 hour.
### Special Abilities
Quarry: As a [Minor] action, the Scout designates one creature as its quarry. Against the quarry, the Scout subtracts 1 from both attack dice. Only one quarry can be designated at a time.
Fae Step (1/short rest): As a [Move] action, the Scout teleports up to 30 feet to an unoccupied space it can see.
Perfect Sight 30 ft: Sees through invisibility, illusory effects, shapechanges, and into the Ethereal Plane within 30 feet.
### Description
Erlking's Scouts serve the great Fae hunting lord. They are patient, methodical, and relentless toward a designated target. They designate quarry at the start of combat and pursue that creature until it is down, switching only when directly ordered.
### Tactics
Erlking's Scouts are methodical and patient. They designate a quarry at the start of combat and pursue that creature until it is down, switching only when directly ordered. They open from range, use Hunter's Arrow to mark their target (denying the hidden condition), then close with Fae Step when the opportunity arises.

## Name: Forest Lord
**Tier:** 4
**Origin:** Fae (Earth)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 4 (bark-like skin)
**Speed:** 40 ft
**Attributes:** Str 6 / Con 6 / Agil 7 / Dex 3 / Int 5 / Wis 5 / Cha 6 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Perception 7, Wilderness 7, Intimidate 5, Empathy 5
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Low-Light Vision, Truesight 60 ft, Tremorsense 120 ft (through roots and soil)
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Fae (Fortune on saves vs charm effects; immune to mind-affecting effects from non-Fae sources; does not age)
### Attacks
Branch Slam [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 15 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 18 damage (12 + 6 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 18 damage, DR halved (round up); target is knocked Prone
- **Margin 3:** 18 damage, DR halved (round up); target is knocked Prone and Restrained by animate roots until they succeed on an Athletics check (Hard task, 2 successes).
### Special Abilities
Forest Domain: In natural terrain, the Forest Lord can communicate with all plants and beasts within 1 mile. At will, it can cause roots and vines to animate in a 30-foot diameter, making the area Difficult Terrain for all creatures it has not blessed.
Verdant Aura: Allies designated by the Forest Lord within 30 feet regain 4 HP at the start of their turns. 
Ancient Authority: Once per encounter as a [Combat] action, the Forest Lord addresses all creatures within 60 feet. Each creature that can hear it makes a Will save (opposed by Wis 7 + Empathy 5). Those that fail become Charmed for 1 minute, treating the Forest Lord as a trusted authority.
Resilient Defense (3/day): When the Forest Lord fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Tactics
Forest Lords do not fight like soldiers. They reshape the battlefield, animate terrain, and compel those who oppose them to reconsider. A Friendly Forest Lord is a genuine force multiplier that will protect the summoner intelligently. Even a Cooperative one follows clear instructions well, though it will add editorial judgments about approach.

## Name: Lantern Archon
**Tier:** 1/4
**Origin:** Outsider (Aetherim)
**Size:** Tiny
**HP:** 12 (Con 3 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** 2 (divine resilience)
**Speed:** 0 ft, Fly 60 ft (Hover)
**Attributes:** Str 1 / Con 3 / Agil 4 / Dex 5 / Int 4 / Wis 5 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Perception 4, Religion 3, Melee Combat 1
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Truesight 30 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +4 (Agil 4)
**Creature Type:** Outsider/Celestial (immune to poison, disease, fear; Fortune on saves vs magic; does not need air, food, drink, or sleep)
### Attacks
Light Ray [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 5 + Ranged Combat 2), Range: 30 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 8 Positive damage (3 + 5 Dex)
- **Margin 2:** 8 Positive damage, DR halved (round up); undead and fiends take double damage
### Special Abilities
Continual Flame: Sheds bright light in a 20-foot radius. This light suppresses magical darkness of equal or lower energy cost.
Detect Evil: The Archon automatically senses creatures with malicious intent within 60 feet. It communicates this information to the summoner as a [Free] action.
Aura of Courage: Allies within 10 feet of the Archon subtract 1 from both dice on saves against the Frightened condition.
Diminutive Target: ### Attacks against the Archon add 1 to both dice due to its tiny size.
### Tactics
Lantern Archons support and illuminate rather than fight. They position to maximize their Aura of Courage benefit, report detected threats, and pepper resistant enemies with Light Ray. Against undead they are considerably more dangerous. They will refuse to enter areas where they would be asked to assist with anything they perceive as unjust.

## Name: Hound Archon
**Tier:** 1
**Origin:** Outsider (Aetherim)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 20 (Con 5 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** S: 4, P: 4, B: 3 (divine plate)
**Speed:** 40 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 5 / Agil 7 / Dex 3 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 4, Perception 5, Athletics 4, Intimidate 3, Religion 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Truesight 30 ft, Keen Hearing and Smell
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Outsider/Celestial (immune to poison, disease, fear; Fortune on saves vs magic; does not need air, food, drink, or sleep)
### Attacks
Holy Sword [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 4), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 9 damage (5 + 4 Str) + 3 Positive damage (Half DR)
- **Margin 2:** 9 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 Positive damage; undead and fiends gain Frightened condition
- **Margin 3:** 9 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 Positive damage; condition applies with no save
Bite [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 4), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 10 damage (6 + 4 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up)
### Special Abilities
Change Shape: The Hound Archon can assume the form of any Medium humanoid as a [Interact] action. Its stats do not change; the form is cosmetic only.
Magic Circle: Once per encounter, the Hound Archon can establish a 10-foot diameter zone of protection centered on a point within 30 feet. Fiends and corrupted creatures cannot enter this zone and cannot charm, frighten, or possess creatures within it. Duration 10 minutes.
### Tactics
Hound Archons are guardians first. They position to protect the summoner and designated allies, using Magic Circle to establish a safe zone when under pressure. They engage threats directly prioritizing undead and fiends. They can disguise themselves to blend into a crowd if needed for a protection mission.

## Name: Solar Aspect
**Tier:** 4
**Origin:** Outsider (Aetherim)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 6 (celestial armor)
**Speed:** 50 ft, Fly 100 ft (Hover)
**Attributes:** Str 6 / Con 6 / Agil 8 / Dex 3 / Int 5 / Wis 5 / Cha 5 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Ranged Combat 6, Perception 8, Religion 8
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Truesight 120 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Outsider/Celestial (immune to poison, disease, fear, charm, necrotic damage; immune to non-magical weapons; Fortune on all saves vs magic; does not need air, food, drink, or sleep)
### Attacks
Radiant Greatsword [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 10 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (8 + 6 Str) + 4 Positive damage (Half DR)
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 Positive damage; fiends and corrupted creatures make a Will save or become Blinded until end of their next turn
- **Margin 3:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 Positive damage; Blinded condition, no save
Radiant Arrow [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 6), Range: 120 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (11 + 3 Dex) + 4 Positive damage (Half DR)
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 Positive damage; target is Revealed (cannot gain Invisible, Hidden, or Concealed) for 1 minute
### Special Abilities
Blinding Presence: Fiends and corrupted creatures that begin their turn within 30 feet make a Fortitude save (1 success) or gain the Dazzled condition until they move out of range.
Divine Awareness: Cannot be surprised. Knows the nature and intent of any creature within 60 feet.
Empower Good (1/day): As 2 [Combat] actions, all allies within 60 feet gain temporary HP equal to 40 and Fortune on all attack rolls and saves for 1 minute.
Resilient Defense (3/day). When the Solar Aspect fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead
Celestial Compact: A Solar Aspect will not arrive for a Summoner it judges to be acting in bad faith. This is a purely GM determination — a Solar Aspect called by a character who has recently committed serious moral wrongs arrives only at Indifferent regardless of targeting roll, and may use its Divine Awareness to announce its assessment to the party before deciding whether to cooperate.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Tactics
Solar Aspects fight with authority and precision. Empower Good goes up first when allies need it; otherwise they alternate between suppressing the most dangerous enemy with Radiant Greatsword and denying concealment with Radiant Arrows. Their Blinding Presence passively disrupts fiends and corrupted opponents throughout the fight.

## Name: Mote Aetherim
**Tier:** 1/8
**Origin:** Outsider (Aetherim)
**Size:** Tiny
**HP:** 6 (Con 2 × 3, Fragile)
**DR:** 0
**Speed:** 0 ft, Fly 40 ft (Hover)
**Attributes:** Str 1 / Con 2 / Agil 5 / Dex 4 / Int 3 / Wis 5 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Perception 3, Medicine 2
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Perfect Sight 10 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Aetherim; Immune to poison damage, Poisoned, disease, fear. Fortune on saves vs magic. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Light Pulse [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 4 + Ranged Combat 1), Range: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 6 Positive damage (2 + 4 Dex); undead and evil-aligned creatures take 4 Positive damage instead.
- **Margin 2:** 6 Positive damage, DR halved (round up); target gains Dazzled condition until end of their next turn from the concentrated light; undead take 4 Positive damage + Frightened until end of their next turn.
### Special Abilities
Healing Touch (1/short rest): As a [Interact] action, touches a living creature and restores 4 HP. Cannot raise creatures above their maximum HP. Cannot be used on undead.
Continual Radiance: Sheds bright light in a 20-foot radius and dim light for an additional 20 feet. The Mote Aetherim cannot suppress this light voluntarily.
Truth Sense: Automatically knows when a creature within 10 feet speaks a deliberate lie. Communicates this to the summoner as a [Free] action.
### Description
A Mote Aetherim is barely more than the Positive plane's awareness made briefly tangible. It has no meaningful combat capability against anything that is not undead. What it has is Healing Touch, reliable truth detection within close range, and the ability to suppress magical darkness simply by existing nearby. A summoner who needs a small amount of healing available without action cost, a truth-detection asset in a conversation, or a light source that cannot be extinguished by darkness weaves will find it adequate. It will answer direct questions about what it perceives with complete honesty, including observations about the summoner's own situation that the summoner may not have asked for.

## Name: Warden Aetherim
**Tier:** 1/4
**Origin:** Outsider (Aetherim)
**Size:** Small
**HP:** 12 (Con 3 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** 2 (divine resilience)
**Speed:** 20 ft, Fly 50 ft (Hover)
**Attributes:** Str 2 / Con 3 / Agil 3 / Dex 5 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Perception 4, Medicine 3, Religion 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Perfect Sight 30 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +3 (Agil 3)
**Creature Type:** Aetherim; Immune to poison damage, Poisoned, disease, fear. Fortune on saves vs magic. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Radiant Bolt [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 5 + Ranged Combat 2), Range: 30 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 8 Positive damage (3 + 5 Dex).
- **Margin 2:** 8 Positive damage, DR halved (round up); undead and evil-aligned creatures make Will save (1 success) or Frightened until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 3:** 8 Positive damage, DR halved (round up); Frightened condition; no save; target also Revealed — cannot benefit from Hidden, Invisible, or Concealed for 1 minute as radiant light clings to them.
### Special Abilities
Healing Touch (2/short rest): As a [Interact] action, touches a living creature and restores 6 HP. Cannot raise creatures above their maximum HP. Cannot be used on undead.
Detect Evil: Automatically senses creatures with actively malicious intent within 60 feet and communicates their general direction and distance to the summoner as a [Free] action each round.
Continual Radiance: Sheds bright light in a 20-foot radius and dim light for an additional 20 feet. The Warden Aetherim cannot suppress this light voluntarily.
Aura of Courage: Allies within 10 feet of the Warden Aetherim subtract 1 from both dice on saves against the Frightened condition.
Diminutive Target: ### Attacks against the Warden Aetherim add 1 to both dice.
### Description
Warden Aetherim are scouts and minor guardians. Detect Evil gives them persistent awareness of hostile intent in the area, which they report without being asked — a useful property that occasionally produces information the summoner finds inconvenient. Aura of Courage provides passive protection against fear effects for nearby allies. Radiant Bolt's Reveal effect at Margin 3 counters invisibility and stealth in encounters where those factors are significant. A summoner who deploys one as a persistent lookout and minor healer will find it performs both roles reliably while maintaining a running commentary on anything it considers worth noting.

## Name: Shield Aetherim
**Tier:** 1/2
**Origin:** Outsider (Aetherim)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 16 (Con 4 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** 4 (divine plate)
**Speed:** 30 ft, Fly 40 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 4 / Agil 6 / Dex 3 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 3, Perception 4, Medicine 3, Religion 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Perfect Sight 30 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Aetherim; Immune to poison damage, Poisoned, disease, fear. Fortune on saves vs magic. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Radiant Blade [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 5 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 8 damage (4 + 4 Str) + 2 Positive damage (Half DR).
- **Margin 2:** 8 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 Positive damage; undead and evil-aligned creatures make Will [Easy] save or Frightened until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 3:** 8 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 Positive damage; Frightened condition; no save; target also Blinded until end of their next turn from the concentrated radiant discharge.
### Special Abilities
Healing Touch (3/short rest): As a [Interact] action, touches a living creature and restores 8 HP. Cannot raise creatures above their maximum HP. Cannot be used on undead.
Protective Aura: Allies within 15 feet of the Shield Aetherim gain +2 DR against all damage. This aura is always active.
Intercept (1/round) [Reaction]: When an adjacent ally would be hit by an attack, the Shield Aetherim may use a [Reaction] to place itself between the attacker and the ally. The attack targets the Shield Aetherim instead, resolving against its defenses and DR.
Continual Radiance: Sheds bright light in a 20-foot radius and dim light for an additional 20 feet. The Shield Aetherim cannot suppress this light voluntarily.
Detect Evil: Automatically senses creatures with actively malicious intent within 60 feet and communicates their general direction and distance to the summoner as a [Free] action each round.
### Description
Shield Aetherim are bodyguards. Protective Aura provides passive DR to everyone nearby; Intercept redirects ### Attacks from vulnerable allies to the Shield Aetherim itself. They will position themselves adjacent to whoever the summoner designates as the priority protection target and maintain that position until ordered otherwise. A summoner who is concerned about a specific ally — a spellcaster, an injured party member, a person of importance — and places a Shield Aetherim beside them will find that ally considerably harder to damage. The Shield Aetherim will not abandon that position to chase a more interesting fight, which is either their most valuable property or their most frustrating one depending on how the battle develops.

## Name: Blade Aetherim
**Tier:** 1
**Origin:** Outsider (Aetherim)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 25 (Con 5 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 4 (divine plate)
**Speed:** 40 ft, Fly 60 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 5 / Agil 8 / Dex 3 / Int 3 / Wis 4 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 3, Perception 5, Medicine 4, Religion 4, Intimidate 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Perfect Sight 30 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Aetherim; Immune to poison damage, Poisoned, disease, fear. Fortune on saves vs magic. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Holy Blade [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 5 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 9 damage (5 + 4 Str) + 3 Positive damage (Half DR).
- **Margin 2:** 9 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 Positive damage; undead and evil-aligned creatures Frightened for 1 minute; Will [Easy] save to resist.
- **Margin 3:** 9 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 Positive damage; Frightened; no save; undead must make Fortitude [Easy] saveor destroyed outright if below half HP.
Radiant Arc [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 2), Range: 60 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 11 Positive damage (8 + 3 Dex); target Dazzled until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 2:** 11 Positive damage, DR halved (round up) + Blinded until end of their next turn; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 11 Positive damage, DR halved (round up) + Blinded + Revealed for 1 minute.
### Special Abilities
Healing Touch (3/short rest): Restores 10 HP per use. Can now stabilize dying creatures automatically with a touch as a [Minor] action.
Protective Aura: Allies within 20 feet of the Blade Aetherim gain +2 DR against all damage. This aura is always active.
Turn Undead (1/encounter) [Combat]: As a [Combat] action, releases a pulse of Positive energy in a 30-foot diameter. All undead in range make a Will save (opposed by Wis 5 + Religion 4). On failure, undead are Frightened and must move away from the Blade Aetherim as fast as possible for 1 minute. They cannot willingly move closer for the duration.
Detect Evil: Automatically senses creatures with actively malicious intent within 60 feet and communicates their general direction and distance to the summoner as a [Free] action each round.
Continual Radiance: Sheds bright light in a 30-foot radius and dim light for an additional 30 feet. The Shield Aetherim cannot suppress this light voluntarily.
### Description
Blade Aetherim are the first Aetherim capable of functioning as a primary combatant rather than a support presence. Holy Blade's destruction of low-HP undead at Margin 3 makes them highly effective in encounters involving significant undead threats. Turn Undead clears the engagement zone of undead entirely in many cases, allowing other combatants to focus on non-undead threats. A summoner who calls one into an undead-heavy encounter will find it efficiently handles that specific problem while maintaining Protective Aura support for allies. Against living opponents it is capable but not exceptional — capable enough to hold its own, not so effective that a summoner should expect it to carry the fight.

## Name: Voice Aetherim
**Tier:** 2
**Origin:** Outsider (Aetherim)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 150 (Con 5 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 5 (divine resilience, centuries of Positive energy concentration)
**Speed:** 40 ft, Fly 70 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 5 / Agil 8 / Dex 3 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 5 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 5, Perception 6, Medicine 5, Religion 5, Persuasion 5, Empathy 6
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Perfect Sight 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Aetherim; Immune to poison damage, Poisoned, disease, fear. Fortune on saves vs magic. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Radiant Strike [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 5), Reach: 5 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 11 damage (7 + 4 Str) + 3 Positive damage (Half DR).
- **Margin 2:** 11 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 Positive damage; undead Frightened for 1 minute; Will [Hard] save to resist.
- **Margin 3:** 11 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 Positive damage; Frightened; no save; undead at half HP or below destroyed outright.
Word of Light [Combat]: Ranged attack (Cha 6 + Religion 5), Range: 60 ft 
- **Margin 1:**: Target makes Will [Easy] save or Dazed until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 2:** Target Stunned until end of their next turn; Will [Hard] save to resist.
- **Margin 3:** Target Stunned for 1 minute; Will [Hard] save each round to end; undead also take 16 Positive damage (Half DR); no save on damage.
### Special Abilities
Mass Healing (1/short rest) [Combat]: As 2 [Combat] actions, releases a pulse of Positive energy in a 30-foot diameter. All living allies in range heal HP equal to the Voice Aetherim's Wisdom score (7). Cannot affect undead or constructs.
Protective Aura: Allies within 30 feet of the Voice Aetherim gain +3 DR against all damage. This aura is always active.
Turn Undead (1/encounter) [Combat]: As a [Combat] action, releases a pulse of Positive energy in a 30-foot diameter. All undead in range make a Will save (opposed by Wis 7 + Religion 5). On failure, undead are Frightened and must move away from the Voice Aetherim as fast as possible for 1 minute. They cannot willingly move closer for the duration.
Divine Authority: When the Voice Aetherim addresses a creature directly and that creature fails a Will save (opposed by Cha 6 + Persuasion 5), the creature cannot take harmful actions for 1 round as divine authority momentarily overwhelms their aggression. This is not Charm.
Detect Evil: Automatically senses creatures with actively malicious intent within 120 feet and communicates their general direction and distance to the summoner as a [Free] action each round.
Continual Radiance: Sheds bright light in a 40-foot radius and dim light for an additional 40 feet. The Shield Aetherim cannot suppress this light voluntarily.
Resilient Defense (1/day): When the Voice Aetherim fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
Voice Aetherim are authority figures. Mass Healing sustains entire parties; Divine Authority suppresses aggression in a single target without charm immunity applying; Word of Light stuns enemies through spoken command. A summoner who calls one into a complex encounter involving both combat and potential negotiation will find it capable of contributing meaningfully in both registers. It will also express opinions about the encounter's participants based on what Detect Evil and Perfect sigh reveal — observations delivered matter-of-factly and without diplomatic softening, because Voice Aetherim have no particular investment in sparing feelings when the truth is relevant.

## Name: Herald Aetherim
**Tier:** 3
**Origin:** Outsider (Aetherim)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 6 (divine plate, ancient construction)
**Speed:** 50 ft, Fly 90 ft
**Attributes:** Str 5 / Con 6 / Agil 8 / Dex 3 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 5 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 6, Perception 7, Medicine 6, Religion 6, Persuasion 6, Intimidate 6
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Perfect Sight 120 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Aetherim; Immune to poison damage, Poisoned, disease, fear, necrotic damage. Fortune on saves vs magic. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Radiant Greatsword [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 6), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 12 damage (7 + 5 Str) + 4 Positive damage (Half DR).
- **Margin 2:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 Positive damage;  undead make Will [Hard] save or Blinded for 1 minute + Frightened for 1 minute; both conditions apply simultaneously.
- **Margin 3:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 Positive damage; Blinded + Frightened; no save; undead at or below half HP destroyed outright.
Radiant Arrow [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 5), Range: 120 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 15 Positive damage (12 + 3 Dex); target Revealed for 1 minute.
- **Margin 2:** 15 Positive damage, DR halved (round up) + Revealed + Blinded until end of their next turn; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 15 Positive damage, DR halved (round up) + Revealed + Blinded for 1 minute + Frightened for 1 minute; no save.
### Special Abilities
Mass Healing (2/short rest) [Combat]: As 2 [Combat] actions, releases a pulse of Positive energy in a 40-foot diameter. All living allies in range heal HP equal to the Herald Aetherim's Wisdom score (7). Cannot affect undead or constructs.
Blinding Presence: Undead within 30 feet make a Fortitude [Easy] save at the start of their turn. Failure: Dazzled for 1 round. This is passive and always active.
Divine Proclamation (1/encounter) [Combat]: As a [Combat] action, the Herald Aetherim speaks a word of absolute authority. All creatures within 60 feet that can hear it make a Will [Hard]save. Failure: Stunned until end of their next turn and Frightened for 1 minute. 1 success: Dazed until end of their next turn only. Creatures that succeed are immune to this Herald's Divine Proclamation for 24 hours.
Protective Aura: Allies within 40 feet of the Voice Aetherim gain +4 DR against all damage. This aura is always active.
Turn Undead (1/encounter) [Combat]: As a [Combat] action, releases a pulse of Positive energy in a 30-foot diameter. All undead in range make a Will save (opposed by Wis 7 + Religion 6). On failure, undead are Frightened and must move away from the Herald Aetherim as fast as possible for 1 minute. They cannot willingly move closer for the duration. Undead with less then ½ HP remaining are destroyed.
Resilient Defense (2/day): When the Herald Aetherim fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Continual Radiance: Sheds bright light in a 60-foot radius and dim light for an additional 60 feet. The Herald Aetherim cannot suppress this light voluntarily.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
Herald Aetherim are messengers of the Positive plane's full authority — not in the sense of divine errands, but in the sense that their presence expresses what the Positive plane is directly and without intermediary. Blinding Presence passively disrupts undead and evil-aligned creatures continuously. Divine Proclamation stuns entire engagements simultaneously. Mass Healing sustains parties through serious combat. Radiant Greatsword at Margin 3 destroys half-HP undead without a save, making them effectively immune to attrition from undead hordes at this tier. A summoner who calls a Herald Aetherim into an undead-heavy or fiend-heavy encounter will find the encounter resolved in a way that involves considerably less danger to allies than it might otherwise. Against living opponents the Herald is still a powerful combatant — just less disproportionately so.

## Name: Sovereign Aetherim
**Tier:** 4
**Origin:** Outsider (Aetherim)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 7 (ancient divine construction)
**Speed:** 50 ft, Fly 110 ft
**Attributes:** Str 6 / Con 6 / Agil 8 / Dex 3 / Int 5 / Wis 5 / Cha 5 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Perception 8, Medicine 7, Religion 7, Persuasion 7, Intimidate 7
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Perfect Sight 120 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Aetherim; Immune to poison damage, Poisoned, disease, fear, necrotic damage, non-magical weapons. Fortune on saves vs magic. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Sovereign Blade [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (8 + 6 Str) + 4 Positive damage (Half DR).
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 Positive damage; all evil-aligned creatures and undead within 10 feet of the target make Will [Hard] save or Blinded + Frightened for 1 minute.
- **Margin 3:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 Positive damage; area effect triggers; no save; undead at or below half HP in the area destroyed outright.
Heaven's Arrow [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 6), Range: 150 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 18 Positive damage (15 + 3 Dex) + Revealed for 1 hour.
- **Margin 2:** 18 Positive damage, DR halved (round up) + Revealed + Blinded for 1 minute; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 18 Positive damage, DR halved (round up) + Revealed + Blinded + Frightened for 1 minute; undead take double damage from this attack; no save.
### Special Abilities
Sovereign Healing (1/short rest) [Combat]: As 2 [Combat] actions, releases a wave of Positive energy in a 50-foot diameter. All living allies heal HP equal to the Sovereign Aetherim's Wisdom score × 2 (16). Additionally, one ally of the summoner's choice within range is restored from any single condition affecting them. Cannot affect undead or constructs.
Blinding Presence: Undead within 40 feet make a Fortitude [Hard] save at the start of their turn. Failure: Blinded for 1 round. This is passive and always active.
Divine Proclamation (1/encounter) [Combat]: As a [Combat] action, the Herald Aetherim speaks a word of absolute authority. All creatures within 60 feet that can hear it make a Will [Hard]save. Failure: Stunned and Frightened for 1 minute. 1 success: Dazed until end of their next turn only. Creatures that succeed are immune to this Herald's Divine Proclamation for 24 hours.
Protective Aura: Allies within 50 feet of the Voice Aetherim gain +5 DR against all damage. This aura is always active.
Turn Undead (1/encounter) [Combat]: As a [Combat] action, releases a pulse of Positive energy in a 60-foot diameter. All undead in range make a Will save (opposed by Wis 7 + Religion 6). On failure, undead are Frightened and must move away from the Herald Aetherim as fast as possible for 1 hour. They cannot willingly move closer for the duration. Undead with less then ½ HP remaining are destroyed.
Resilient Defense (2/day): When the Sovereign Aetherim fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Continual Radiance: Sheds bright light in a 80-foot radius and dim light for an additional 80 feet. The Sovereign Aetherim cannot suppress this light voluntarily.
Immunity to Non-Magical Weapons: Mundane weapons simply pass through the Sovereign Aetherim's form without effect.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
A Sovereign Aetherim's presence reshapes the terms of any engagement involving undead, fiends, or evil-aligned creatures — Blinding Presence, Turn Undead, and immunity to non-magical weapons mean those categories of opponent cannot effectively engage it. Against living opponents it is still a powerful combatant with significant support capability. Sovereign Healing at this tier can restore an entire party from serious damage in a single action. A summoner whose moral standing is genuinely good will find a Sovereign Aetherim the most capable protective ally available at any tier. One whose standing is ambiguous will find it Cooperative at best, technically compliant, and quietly persistent in noting when its observations suggest the summoner might reconsider a course of action.

## Name: Radiant Aetherim
**Tier:** 5
**Origin:** Outsider (Aetherim)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 210 (Con 7 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 8 (primordial divine construction)
**Speed:** 60 ft, Fly 130 ft
**Attributes:** Str 7 / Con 7 / Agil 8 / Dex 3 / Int 5 / Wis 5 / Cha 6 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Perception 8, Medicine 8, Religion 8, Persuasion 8, Intimidate 7
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Perfect Sight 120 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Aetherim; Immune to poison damage, Poisoned, disease, fear, necrotic damage, non-magical weapons. Fortune on all saves. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Primordial Radiance [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 15 damage (8 + 7 Str) + 5 Positive damage (Half DR).
- **Margin 2:** 15 damage, DR halved (round up) + 5 Positive damage; undead within 20 feet of the target make Will [Hard] save or Blinded + Frightened for 1 minute + 16 Positive damage from the radiant discharge; living allies in the same radius heal 8 HP.
- **Margin 3:** 15 damage, DR halved (round up) + 5 Positive damage; all effects apply with no save; undead at or below half HP in the area destroyed; living allies heal 16 HP.
Solar Strike [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 6), Range: 200 ft, 20-foot diameter.
- **Margin 1:**: 20 Positive damage (17 + 3 Dex) + Blinded until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 2:** 20 Positive damage, DR halved (round up) + Blinded for 1 minute + Frightened for 1 minute; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 20 Positive damage, DR halved (round up) + Blinded + Frightened; undead take double damage and are destroyed outright if at or below half HP; no save on any effect.
### Special Abilities
Radiant Healing (1/short rest) [Combat]: As 2 [Combat] actions, releases the full Positive plane's healing force in a 60-foot diameter. All living allies heal to their full maximum HP. All conditions affecting them that are not permanent are removed. Undead and constructs in the radius take 40 Positive damage (Half DR); no save.
Blinding Presence: Undead within 60 feet make a Fortitude [Hard] save at the start of their turn. Failure: Blinded for 1 minute. This is passive and always active.
Divine Proclamation (1/encounter) [Combat]: As a [Combat] action, the Radiant Aetherim speaks a word of absolute authority. All creatures within 60 feet that can hear it make a Will [Hard]save. Failure: Stunned and Frightened for 1 minute. 1 success: Dazed until end of their next turn only. Creatures that succeed are immune to this Radiant’s Divine Proclamation for 24 hours.
Protective Aura: Allies within 60 feet of the Voice Aetherim gain +6 DR against all damage. Fortune on all saves within the aura. This aura is always active.
Turn Undead (1/encounter) [Combat]: As a [Combat] action, releases a pulse of Positive energy in a 120-foot diameter. All undead in range make a Will save (opposed by Wis 8 + Religion 8). On failure, undead are Frightened and must move away from the Radiant Aetherim as fast as possible for 24 hours. They cannot willingly move closer for the duration. Undead with less then ½ HP remaining are destroyed.
Immunity to Non-Magical Weapons: Mundane weapons simply pass through the Radiant Aetherim's form without effect.
Resilient Defense (3/day): When the Radiant Aetherim fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Positive Energy Sharing: The summoner and up to 6 designated allies gain immunity to necrotic damage and the Poisoned condition while within 60 feet.
Continual Radiance: Sheds bright light in a 100-foot radius and dim light for an additional 100 feet. This light acts as sunlight; suppresses all magical darkness. The Radiant Aetherim cannot suppress this light voluntarily.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
A Radiant Aetherim is the Positive plane's full authority given form and summoned to act. Radiant Healing at full HP restoration for all allies in a 60-foot diameter is a fight-resetting ability with no equivalent in the bestiary. Turn Undead triggers automatically every round, destroying half-HP undead and routing the rest without requiring any action. Blinding Presence at 60 feet means undead and evil-aligned creatures cannot function effectively within the area. Divine Proclamation prevents all hostile action by everyone who fails the save. A summoner who calls a Radiant Aetherim into an encounter has made a decisive statement about how that encounter ends. The Radiant Aetherim will assess the summoner's moral standing before deciding whether to apply its full capability or merely its baseline cooperation — not as a threat, but as an honest reflection of what the Positive plane is willing to pour through the connection. A summoner whose record warrants it will receive everything the entry describes. The Radiant Aetherim will tell them, clearly and without judgment, if their record does not.


## Name: Imp
**Tier:** 1/4
**Origin:** Outsider (Fiend)
**Size:** Tiny
**HP:** 12 (Con 3 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** 2 (scaled skin)
**Speed:** 20 ft, Fly 40 ft
**Attributes:** Str 2 / Con 3 / Agil 6 / Dex 3 / Int 4 / Wis 3 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Stealth 5, Deception 4, Perception 3, Melee Combat 2
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Truesight 20 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Outsider/Fiend (resistant to fire, cold, lightning damage (+2 DR); immune to poison, Poisoned condition; Fortune on saves vs magic; does not need air, food, drink, or sleep)
### Attacks
Sting [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 2), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 5 damage (3 + 2 Str) + Fortitude [Easy] save or Poisoned (Weak) for 1 minute.
- **Margin 2:** 5 damage, DR halved (round up) + Fortitude [Easy] save or Poisoned (Strong) for 1 minute.
### Special Abilities
Invisibility (At-Will): Imp can become invisible as a [Minor] action. Ends if it ### Attacks.
Change Shape: Can assume the form of a small beast (rat, raven, spider) as a [Interact] action.
Devil's Sight: Sees through magical darkness.
Pocket Fiend: Imps are frequently used as spies and familiars by casters of dark intent. A Cooperative Imp will gather information reliably, though it exaggerates its own contribution significantly when reporting back.
### Tactics
Imps avoid combat. They spy, report, and sting when they have an overwhelming advantage. A Summoner who treats an Imp like a combat asset will be disappointed; one who treats it like an intelligence resource will get more than expected, though the information will always have a slight self-serving editorial slant.

## Name: Shadow Hound
**Tier:** 1/2
**Origin:** Outsider (Fiend)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 20 (Con 4 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 0
**Speed:** 40 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 4 / Agil 7 / Dex 5 / Int 2 / Wis 5 / Cha 2 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 2, Stealth 5, Perception 4
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Devil's Sight (sees through magical darkness)
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Outsider/Fiend (resistant to fire, cold, lightning (+2 DR); immune to Poisoned, poison damage; does not need air, food, drink, or sleep)
### Attacks
Bite [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 2), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 8 damage (4 + 4 Str) + 2 Negative damage (Half DR)
- **Margin 2:** 8 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 Negative damage; target makes Fortitude [Easy] save or gains Weakened condition until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 3:** 8 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 Negative damage; Weakened condition, no save.
### Special Abilities
 Shadow Meld: In dim light or darkness, the Shadow Hound has Fortune on Stealth checks and ### Attacks against it add 1 to both dice.
Pack Hunt: When an ally is within 5 feet of the target, the Shadow Hound subtracts 1 from both attack dice.
Death Sense: The Shadow Hound automatically detects creatures below half HP within 60 feet.
### Tactics
Shadow Hounds hunt. They use Death Sense to identify the most vulnerable target, Shadow Meld to approach unseen, and converge on weakened enemies. They ignore the summoner's allies unless those allies attack them, instinctively prioritizing wounded prey. This is their nature and not something negotiable.

## Name: Bone Devil
**Tier:** 2
**Origin:** Outsider (Fiend)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 30 (Con 5 × 6, Massive)
**DR:** 4 (bone armor)
**Speed:** 40 ft, Fly 40 ft
**Attributes:** Str 5 / Con 5 / Agil 7 / Dex 3 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 5 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 3, Perception 5, Deception 5, Intimidate 5
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Devil's Sight, Truesight 30 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Outsider/Fiend (resistant to fire, cold, lightning (+2 DR); immune to poison, Poisoned condition; Fortune on saves vs magic; does not need air, food, drink, or sleep)
### Attacks
Claw [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 10 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (9 + 5 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up)
Tail Sting [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 10 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 13 damage (8 + 5 Str) + Fortitude [Easy] save or Poisoned (Strong).
- **Margin 2:** 13 damage, DR halved (round up) + Poisoned (Strong) (no save) + Weakened condition for 1 minute.
### Special Abilities
Fear Aura: Creatures within 10 feet that are not Fiends make a Will [Easy] save at the start of their turn. On failure, they gain the Frightened condition until they succeed. Creatures immune to fear or charm are unaffected.
Wall of Ice (1/day): As 2 [Combat] actions, create a 60-foot-long, 20-foot-high wall of ice. Each section has HP 20 and DR 4. Duration until destroyed.
### Tactics
Bone Devils are commanders and negotiators — they spend the first round assessing before fully committing. Fear Aura keeps weaker enemies at a distance while they focus on primary threats. A Bone Devil that has agreed to terms will execute those terms with precision and will always point out when you could have asked for more.

## Name: Pit Fiend
**Tier:** 5
**Origin:** Outsider (Fiend)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 210 (Con 7 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 6 (infernal hide)
**Speed:** 30 ft, Fly 60 ft
**Attributes:** Str 7 / Con 7 / Agil 7 / Dex 3 / Int 5 / Wis 6 / Cha 6 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Perception 7, Deception 7, Intimidate 7
