**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Truesight 120 ft, Devil's Sight
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Outsider/Fiend (immune to fire and Negative damage; resistant to cold, lightning (+4 DR); immune to poison, Poisoned, Charmed, Frightened; Fortune on all saves vs magic; does not need air, food, drink, or sleep)
### Attacks
Crushing Fist [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 10 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 20 damage (13 + 7 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 20 damage, DR halved (round up); target makes Fortitude [Easy] save or is Grappled
- **Margin 3:** 20 damage, DR halved (round up); target Grappled and Restrained (no save).
Hellfire [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 6), Range: 120 ft, 20-foot diameter
- **Margin 1:**: 20 fire damage (17 + 3 Dex).
- **Margin 2:** 20 fire damage, DR halved (round up) + Ignited condition.
### Special Abilities
Infernal Command: The Pit Fiend can issue commands to any Fiend of lower TR within 300 feet as a [Free] action. Those Fiends must succeed on a Will [Hard] save to refuse. If other Fiends are present on the battlefield from any source, they must make this save or follow the Pit Fiend's orders.
Regeneration 10: The Pit Fiend regains 10 HP at the start of its turn unless it took Positive damage this round.
Fear Presence: All non-Fiend creatures within 60 feet make a Will [Easy] save at the start of combat. Failure: Frightened for 1 minute. Success: Immune to this Pit Fiend's Fear Presence for 24 hours.
Resilient Defense (3/day). When the Pit Fiend fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Binding Contract: Any Pit Fiend that agrees to be summoned expects something in return. This is a GM narrative element, not a mechanical one — but the Pit Fiend will not forget.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Tactics
A Pit Fiend enters a battle and immediately establishes hierarchy. Infernal Command suppresses any other Fiend present. Fear Presence scatters weaker opponents. It uses Hellfire on grouped enemies and Crushing Fists to control priority targets. A Summoner who has negotiated clearly will find it an extraordinarily capable ally. A Summoner who assumed good faith will find the agreement technically honored in ways they did not intend.

## Name: Shadow
**Tier:** 1/4
**Origin:** Outsider (Fiend)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 12 (Con 3 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** 0 (but see Shadow Form)
**Speed:** 40 ft
**Attributes:** Str 2 / Con 3 / Agil 6 / Dex 4 / Int 3 / Wis 4 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 2, Stealth 6, Perception 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Undead/Shadow Plane (immune to poison, Poisoned, disease, exhaustion; vulnerable to Positive damage; does not need air, food, drink, or sleep)
### Attacks
Strength Drain [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 2), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 6 Negative damage (4 + 2 Str) + target's Strength reduced by 1 for 1 hour.
- **Margin 2:** 6 Negative damage, DR halved (round up) + Strength reduced by 2 for 1 hour + target makes Fortitude [Easy] save  or gains the Weakened condition.
### Special Abilities
Shadow Form: While in dim light or darkness, the Shadow has DR 8 against all non-magical damage. Positive-energy damage ignores this entirely.
Amorphous: The Shadow can move through a space as narrow as 1 inch.
Sunlight Weakness: In bright light, the Shadow has Misfortune on all rolls.
Shadow Spawn: If a non-undead creature is killed by Strength Drain-induced Strength reduction (Strength reduced to 0), it rises as a Shadow under the controlling summoner's influence at the start of its next turn.
### Tactics
Shadows avoid bright light and exploit dim areas. They stack Strength Drain against a single target, attempting to reduce them to 0 Strength. A Summoner who calls one should be prepared for it to ignore tactical nuance in favor of finding the darkest spot in the room and hunting from there.

## Name: Shadow Walker
**Tier:** 1
**Origin:** Outsider (Fiend)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 20 (Con 5 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** 2 (shadow substance)
**Speed:** 40 ft, Climb 40 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 5 / Agil 8 / Dex 3 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 4, Stealth 6, Perception 4, Deception 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, See Invisibility
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Shadow Plane (immune to poison, Poisoned; resistant to cold damage (+2 DR); does not need air, food, drink, or sleep)
### Attacks
Shadow Strike [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 4), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 9 damage (5 + 4 Str) + 3 Negative damage (Ignore DR)
- **Margin 2:** 9 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 Negative damage; target is Disoriented until start of their next turn.
- **Margin 3:** 9 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 Negative damage; target Disoriented and loses the Hidden condition if they had it.
### Special Abilities
Shadow Meld: In dim light or darkness, the Shade Walker has Fortune on Stealth. ### Attacks against it add 1 to both dice.
Planar Step (1/short rest): As a [Move] action, the Shade Walker steps briefly into the Shadow Plane and re-emerges up to 30 feet away in any dim or dark location it can see. This movement does not provoke opportunity ### Attacks.
Sunlight Sensitivity: In bright light, the Shade Walker adds 1 to both attack dice.
### Tactics
Shade Walkers are assassins. They use Planar Step to close distance, strike from darkness, and retreat before retaliation. A Cooperative Shade Walker follows target priority without complaint. An Indifferent one picks whoever is standing in the most shadow.

## Name: Nightmare
**Tier:** 2
**Origin:** Outsider (Fiend)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 30 (Con 5 × 6, Massive)
**DR:** 2
**Speed:** 60 ft, Fly 90 ft
**Attributes:** Str 5 / Con 5 / Agil 8 / Dex 5 / Int 3 / Wis 3 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 3, Perception 4, Intimidate 4, Athletics 4
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Devil's Sight
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Shadow Plane (immune to poison, Poisoned, Frightened; resistant to fire (+2 DR); does not need air, food, drink, or sleep)
### Attacks
Flaming Hooves [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 11 damage (6 + 5 Str) + 3 fire damage (Half DR).
- **Margin 2:** 11 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 fire damage; target knocked Prone.
- **Margin 3:** 11 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 fire damage; target Prone and Stunned until end of their next turn.
### Special Abilities
Shadow Stride (1/day): The Nightmare can carry up to 3 riders into the Shadow Plane for up to 1 hour, moving through solid objects during transit. It must return to the material plane within the duration or the effect ends suddenly and all passengers are ejected.
Smoke: The Nightmare's flaming hooves generate a thick smoke trail when running at full speed (40+ feet moved in a turn). The trail persists for 1 minute and creates a 10-foot-wide path of Difficult Terrain and heavy obscurement.
Terror Mount: A rider on a Nightmare adds 2 to both dice on Intimidate checks while mounted.
### Tactics
Nightmares are mounts first and combatants second. In combat without a rider, they rear and strike with their hooves, using their speed to break through formations. As a summoner's mount they are exceptional — fast, terrifying, and capable of brief planar transit. A Cooperative Nightmare will carry the summoner wherever they indicate; an Indifferent one runs toward whatever frightens it least and expects to be chased.

## Name: Spite
**Tier:** 1/8
**Origin:** Outsider (Fiend)
**Size:** Tiny
**HP:** 6 (Con 2 × 3, Fragile)
**DR:** 1 (scaled hide)
**Speed:** 20 ft, Fly 30 ft
**Attributes:** Str 1 / Con 2 / Agil 6 / Dex 3 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Stealth 5, Deception 4, Perception 3, Melee Combat 1
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Fiend; Immune to poison damage, Poisoned. Resistance to fire, cold, lightning (+2 DR each). Fortune on saves vs magic. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Sting [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 1), Reach: 5 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 2 damage (1 + 1 Str); target makes Fortitude save (1 success) or gains Poisoned (Weak) for 1 minute.
- **Margin 2:** 2 damage, DR halved (round up); Poisoned (Weak) for 1 hour; no save; target also gains Sickened condition for 1 round.
### Special Abilities
Invisibility (At-Will): As a [Minor] action, becomes invisible. Ends if it ### Attacks.
Devil's Sight: Sees through magical darkness as if dim light.
Whisper Malice: Once per encounter, the Spite whispers something into the ear of a sleeping or distracted creature within 5 feet. The target makes a Will [Easy] save. On failure, they wake with a specific false belief planted by the Spite; a suspicion about an ally, a misremembered detail, a conviction that something they heard was said by someone who did not say it. The belief feels like their own thought. The Spite communicates what it planted to the summoner afterward, accurately.
Diminutive Target: ### Attacks against the Spite add 1 to both dice.
### Description
Spites are barely-manifested malice — small, venomous, and primarily useful for the specific cruelty of Whisper Malice rather than any combat contribution. A summoner who needs poison delivered to a sleeping target, a false belief planted in an NPC's mind, or a persistent invisible scout that sees through magical darkness will find them adequate. Their combat presence is negligible. They are calculating even at this tier — a Spite that has been summoned is already evaluating what it can learn about the summoner and their situation that might be useful later. Most summoners do not consider this relevant. Whether they are right depends on whether they ever summon another Spite.

## Name: Gnasher
**Tier:** 1/4
**Origin:** Outsider (Fiend)
**Size:** Small
**HP:** 15 (Con 3 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 2 (thick hide)
**Speed:** 35 ft
**Attributes:** Str 3 / Con 3 / Agil 6 / Dex 4 / Int 3 / Wis 3 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 1, Perception 2, Athletics 2, Stealth 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Fiend; Immune to poison damage, Poisoned. Resistance to fire, cold, lightning (+2 DR each). Fortune on saves vs magic. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Bite [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 1), Reach: 5 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 6 damage (3 + 3 Str) + 2 Negative damage (Half DR).
- **Margin 2:** 6 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 Negative damage; target makes Fortitude [easy] save or Weakened until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 3:** 6 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 Negative damage; Weakened; no save; target also gains Sickened condition until end of their next turn.
### Special Abilities
Pack Menace: When two or more Gnashers are within 5 feet of the same target, both subtract 1 from both attack dice against that target.
Death Frenzy: When any creature within 30 feet drops to 0 HP, the Gnasher may immediately move up to half its speed toward that creature as a [Free] action. This triggers even if the fallen creature is an ally.
Devil's Sight: Sees through magical darkness as if dim light.
### Description
Gnashers are small fiend predators, aggressive and tactically crude. They charge the nearest target, use Pack Menace when another Gnasher is nearby, and go into Death Frenzy when anything falls — including other Gnashers, which they will move toward and potentially attack if no other target is available. A summoner who calls one needs to understand that Gnashers are not discriminating about what they bite when the frenzy takes them. Clear target instructions and physical separation from the Gnasher when allies drop are both advisable. They follow target priority adequately when Cooperative. When Indifferent, they follow their instincts, which tend toward the nearest warm thing that is bleeding.


## Name: Tormentor
**Tier:** 1
**Origin:** Outsider (Fiend)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 20 (Con 5 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** 3 (fiend hide)
**Speed:** 40 ft, Fly 30 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 5 / Agil 8 / Dex 3 / Int 3 / Wis 4 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 4, Intimidate 5, Deception 4, Perception 4, Stealth 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Devil's Sight, Perfect sight 15 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Fiend; Immune to poison damage, Poisoned. Resistance to fire, cold, lightning (+2 DR each). Fortune on saves vs magic. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Barbed Claws [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 4), Reach: 5 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 12 damage (8 + 4 Str) + 1 Bleeding stack.
- **Margin 2:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 Bleeding stacks; target makes Will [Easy] save or Frightened until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 3:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 Bleeding stacks; Frightened; no save; target also Disoriented until end of their next turn.
Agony Lance [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 3), Range: 30 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 9 Negative damage (6 + 3 Dex); target Sickened until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 2:** 9 Negative damage, DR halved (round up) + Sickened for 1 minute; Will [Easy] save each round to end.
- **Margin 3:** 9 Negative damage, DR halved (round up) + Sickened for 1 minute + Weakened for 1 minute; no save.
### Special Abilities
Fear Aura: Creatures within 10 feet that are not Fiends make a Will [Easy] save at the start of their turn. Failure: Frightened until they move outside the aura. Creatures immune to fear are unaffected.
Pain Sense: The Tormentor automatically knows the current HP of every creature within 30 feet. It prioritizes wounded targets instinctively and communicates target HP to the summoner as a [Free] action each round if instructed.
Torment: When the Tormentor hits a target that is already suffering any [Hindered] condition, deal an additional 4 Negative damage (Half DR) as the pain of their existing wound compounds.
Devil's Sight: Sees through magical darkness as if dim light.
### Description
Tormentors are intelligent fiends — the first in the category with enough sophistication to evaluate the summoner's intentions rather than simply responding to instructions. They understand that they are being used and they are deciding how much that serves their interests. A Cooperative Tormentor executes instructions cleanly. A Friendly one — which requires exceptional targeting roll results — will proactively share observations from Pain Sense and suggest tactical adjustments. Fear Aura creates a perimeter of psychological pressure around them; Torment rewards them for targeting already-injured opponents, which they will do naturally without instruction. A summoner who calls one should be aware that it remembers the encounter afterward, including everything it learned about the summoner's capabilities, weaknesses, and allies.

## Name: Bone Fiend
**Tier:** 1
**Origin:** Outsider (Fiend)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 150 (Con 5 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 5 (bone armor, fiend resilience)
**Speed:** 40 ft, Fly 40 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 5 / Agil 7 / Dex 3 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 4, Perception 5, Deception 5, Intimidate 5
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Devil's Sight, Truesight 30 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Fiend; Immune to poison damage, Poisoned. Resistance to fire, cold, lightning (+2 DR each). Fortune on saves vs magic. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Claw [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 4), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 12 damage (8 + 4 Str).
- **Margin 2:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up); target Grappled; no save; Grappled target takes 6 Negative damage at start of each of their turns.
- **Margin 3:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + Grappled + Restrained; ongoing damage increases to 10 Negative.
Tail Sting [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 4), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 12 damage (8 + 4 Str); target makes Fortitude [Easy] save or Poisoned (Strong) for 1 hour.
- **Margin 2:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up); Poisoned (Strong); no save; target also Weakened for 1 minute.
- **Margin 3:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up); Poisoned (Strong) + Weakened; target loses 3 from Current Luck pool as the venom disrupts vital energy.
### Special Abilities
Fear Aura: 10-foot diameter; Will [Easy] save; failure: Frightened until outside aura.
Wall of Bone (1/day) [Combat]: As 2 [Combat] actions, raises a 40-foot-long, 10-foot-high wall of bone and calcified matter from any available surface within 60 feet. Each 10-foot section has HP 20 and DR 4 against non-magical damage. Duration: until destroyed. The wall cannot be raised through living creatures.
Tactical Assessment: At the start of combat, the Bone Fiend evaluates the battlefield and communicates its assessment to the summoner — primary threats, potential flanking angles, weakest targets. This assessment is accurate and delivered as a [Free] action on its first turn. Whether the summoner uses it is their decision.
Resilient Defense (1/day): When the Bone Fiend fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
Bone Fiends are commanders. They assess before committing, use Wall of Bone to reshape the engagement geometry, Claw to hold high-priority targets, and Tail Sting to degrade the most dangerous opponent's combat effectiveness. Tactical Assessment makes them the first Fiend that actively contributes to the summoner's strategic planning rather than simply executing instructions. A summoner who engages with that assessment will find the Bone Fiend performs considerably above its TR. One who ignores it will find it still performs adequately — it will simply note, for its own satisfaction, that the summoner made an error. Bone Fiends remember everything and find incompetence irritating in a way that, over multiple summonings, tends to express itself in subtle ways that are technically within the terms of their agreement.

## Name: Wrath Fiend
**Tier:** 3
**Origin:** Outsider (Fiend)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 5 (rage-hardened hide)
**Speed:** 50 ft, Fly 50 ft
**Attributes:** Str 5 / Con 6 / Agil 8 / Dex 4 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 6, Intimidate 6, Perception 5, Athletics 5
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Devil's Sight
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Fiend; Immune to poison damage, Poisoned. Resistance to fire, cold, lightning (+2 DR each). Fortune on saves vs magic. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Devastation Strike [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 6), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 12 damage (7 + 5 Str) + 4 Negative damage (Half DR).
- **Margin 2:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 Negative damage; target knocked Prone + Stunned until end of their next turn; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 Negative damage + Prone + Stunned; target makes Fortitude [Easy] save or loses 1 point of Strength permanently until they receive Major Restoration.
Infernal Roar [Combat]: As a [Combat] action, releases a roar of pure destructive fury in a 30-foot cone. All creatures in range make a Will save (compare Agil 5 + Intimidate 6 targeting vs. save).
- **Margin 1:**: Frightened for 1 round.
- **Margin 2:** Frightened for 1 minute; Will [Easy] save each round to end.
Net 3: Cowering until end of their next turn, then Frightened for 1 minute.
### Special Abilities
Unstoppable Fury: The Wrath Fiend cannot be Stunned, Dazed, or Charmed. 
Devastating Charge: If the Wrath Fiend moves at least 20 feet straight toward a target before attacking, subtract 1 from both attack dice on that attack. On Margin 2+, the target is also pushed 15 feet and knocked Prone regardless of size.
Wrath Aura: Creatures within 15 feet that are not Fiends make a Fortitude [Easy] save at the start of their turn or gain the Frightened condition until they move outside the aura.
Blood Frenzy: When the Wrath Fiend reduces a creature to 0 HP, it regains 10 HP and may immediately move up to half its speed as a [Free] action.
Resilient Defense (1/day): When the Wrath Fiend fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
Wrath Fiends are the Negative plane's embodiment of destructive fury given form and scale. They do not have tactical plans. They have targets. A summoner who gives one a target designation and stands back will find it converges on that target with single-minded effectiveness, using Devastating Charge to open, Devastation Strike to deal structural damage, and Infernal Roar to scatter anything trying to assist. Unstoppable Fury means control effects that would neutralize other summoned creatures simply do not work on them. The permanent Strength reduction at Margin 3 is the detail summoners should brief their allies about before the engagement — a target who has been hit at Margin 3 by a Wrath Fiend is permanently diminished until they can access Major Restoration. A summoner who deploys one against an enemy that matters should expect that enemy to have a lasting problem regardless of how the broader fight resolves.

## Name: Pit Caller
**Tier:** 4
**Origin:** Outsider (Fiend)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 6 (ancient fiend resilience)
**Speed:** 40 ft, Fly 60 ft
**Attributes:** Str 5 / Con 6 / Agil 8 / Dex 3 / Int 5 / Wis 5 / Cha 6 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Perception 6, Deception 7, Intimidate 7, Arcane 6
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Devil's Sight, Perfect Sight 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Fiend; Immune to poison damage, Poisoned. Resistance to fire, cold, lightning (+2 DR each). Fortune on saves vs magic. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Fiend Blade [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (9 + 5 Str) + 4 Negative damage (Half DR).
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 Negative damage; target Weakened + Sickened for 1 minute; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 Negative damage + Weakened + Sickened; target also loses 4 from Current Luck pool; target makes Fortitude [Easy] save or gains 1 Exhaustion level.
Negative Bolt [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 6), Range: 120 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 18 Negative damage (15 + 3 Dex); target loses 2 from Current Luck.
- **Margin 2:** 18 Negative damage, DR halved (round up) + Weakened for 1 minute; target loses 4 from Current Luck; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 18 Negative damage, DR halved (round up) + Weakened + Sickened for 1 minute; target loses 6 from Current Luck; no save.
### Special Abilities
Call from the Pit (1/day) [Combat]: As 2 [Combat] actions, tears a rift to the lower Negative plane in a space within 60 feet. From this rift emerge 1d4 Gnashers and 1 Shadow Hound at the start of the Pit Caller's next turn. These creatures are not under the summoner's control — they are under the Pit Caller's control, and the Pit Caller directs them as it chooses. The rift closes after 1 minute. The summoner's Summoning Limit does not apply to these creatures as they are not summoned by the summoner.
Infernal Command: Any Fiend of lower TR within 300 feet that is not already under another creature's control must make a Will [Hard] save at the start of each of their turns or follow the Pit Caller's instructions instead. This includes other summoned Fiends if the summoner's Cooperative or Friendly disposition bond is not actively maintained.
Fear Aura: 20-foot diameter; Will [Hard] save; failure: Frightened for 1 minute.
Bargain: The Pit Caller can offer the summoner a deal at any point during the summoning. The terms it proposes will appear favorable. The terms it proposes will technically be favorable. The way those terms interact with the summoner's existing obligations, future plans, and the Pit Caller's own longer strategy is something the summoner should evaluate very carefully before accepting.
Resilient Defense (3/day): When the Pit Caller fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
Pit Callers are the first Fiend the summoner genuinely needs to approach with comprehensive preparation. Infernal Command means other summoned Fiends in the area may switch allegiance if the summoner's disposition bond weakens. Call from the Pit produces creatures under the Pit Caller's control, not the summoner's — which is either a useful addition to the engagement or a serious problem depending on whether the summoner and Pit Caller's current instructions align. Bargain introduces an active negotiation element that no other summoned creature offers. A summoner who has prepared precise, gap-free terms and maintains those terms throughout the summoning will find a Pit Caller an extraordinarily capable ally. One who has not will find it begins working toward its own interests at the first available opportunity, and its interests tend to involve the summoner being obligated to it in ways the summoner did not intend.

## Name: Void Fiend
**Tier:** 5
**Origin:** Outsider (Fiend)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 210 (Con 7 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 7 (primordial fiend resilience)
**Speed:** 40 ft, Fly 80 ft
**Attributes:** Str 7 / Con 7 / Agil 8 / Dex 3 / Int 5 / Wis 5 / Cha 6 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Perception 7, Deception 8, Intimidate 8, Arcane 7
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Devil's Sight, True Sight 120 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Fiend; Immune to poison damage, Poisoned, non-magical weapons, fire damage. Resistance to cold, lightning (+4 DR each). Fortune on all saves. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Entropic Strike [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 15 damage (8 + 7 Str) + 5 Negative damage (Half DR).
- **Margin 2:** 15 damage, DR halved (round up) + 5 Negative damage; target Weakened + Sickened + loses 6 from Current Luck; no save; target makes Fortitude [Easy] save or gains 1 Exhaustion level.
- **Margin 3:** 15 damage, DR halved (round up) + 5 Negative damage + all conditions; Exhaustion level; no save; target also loses 1 point of Constitution permanently until Major Restoration.
Void Lance [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 6), Range: 150 ft, 10-foot diameter.
- **Margin 1:**: 20 Negative damage (17 + 3 Dex) + Weakened for 1 minute.
- **Margin 2:** 20 Negative damage, DR halved (round up) + Weakened + Sickened for 1 minute + loses 4 from Current Luck; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 20 Negative damage, DR halved (round up) + all conditions + 1 Exhaustion level; no save.
### Special Abilities
Entropic Presence: Creatures within 20 feet that are not Fiends make a Fortitude [Hard] save at the start of their turn. Failure: lose 2 from Current Luck and gain the Weakened condition until end of their next turn.
Infernal Command: Any Fiend of equal or lower TR within 1 mile that is not already under another creature's control must make a Will [Hard] save at the start of each of their turns or follow the Pit Caller's instructions instead. This includes other summoned Fiends if the summoner's Cooperative or Friendly disposition bond is not actively maintained.
Call from the Pit (1/day) [Combat]: As 2 [Combat] actions, tears a rift to the lower Negative plane in a space within 60 feet. From this rift emerge 4 Gnashers and 2 Shadow Hounds, and 1 Tormentor at the start of the Pit Caller's next turn. These creatures are not under the summoner's control — they are under the Void Fiend's control, and the Void Fiend directs them as it chooses. The rift closes after 1 minute. The summoner's Summoning Limit does not apply to these creatures as they are not summoned by the summoner.
Void Compulsion (1/encounter) [Combat]: As 2 [Combat] actions, the Void Fiend exerts its full will on all creatures within 60 feet that are not Fiends. Each creature makes a Will [Hard] save. Failure: Charmed by the Void Fiend for 1 minute; treats it as a trusted authority whose instructions must be followed. 1 success: Frightened for 1 minute. Creatures immune to charm still suffer the Frightened result on 1 success.
Bargain: The Void Fiend can offer the summoner a deal at any point during the summoning. The terms it proposes will appear favorable. The terms it proposes will technically be favorable. The way those terms interact with the summoner's existing obligations, future plans, and the Void Fiend’s own longer strategy is something the summoner should evaluate very carefully before accepting.
Immunity to Non-Magical Weapons.
Immunity to Fire Damage
Resilient Defense (3/day): When the Void Fiend fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Negative Energy Sharing: The summoner and up to 4 designated allies gain immunity to the Weakened and Sickened conditions while within 60 feet.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.


## Name: Gibbering Mouther
**Tier:** 1/2
**Origin:** Aberration (Space)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 20 (Con 4 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 0
**Speed:** 10 ft, Swim 10 ft
**Attributes:** Str 3 / Con 4 / Agil 6 / Dex 5 / Int 3 / Wis 5 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 2, Perception 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Blindsight 30 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Aberration (immune to Frightened, Prone; immune to mind-affecting effects; does not need air, food, drink, or sleep)
### Attacks
Bite (Multiple) [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 2), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 10 damage (7 + 3 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up); target makes Fortitude [Easy] save or is Grappled by one of the mouths. While Grappled this way, target takes 4 damage at the start of each of their turns.
- **Margin 3:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up); Grappled (no save)
### Special Abilities
Gibbering: All creatures within 20 feet that can hear the Gibbering Mouther and are not Aberrations must make a Will [Easy] save at the start of their turn. On failure, they cannot use reactions until the start of their next turn and must move in a random direction before acting (rolled by GM).
Blinding Spittle (Recharge 5-8): Ranged attack against one creature within 30 feet. Target makes Reflex [Easy] save or gains Blinded condition for 1 minute (Fortitude [Easy] save each turn to remove).
Ground Destabilization: The area within 5 feet of the Gibbering Mouther becomes Difficult Terrain.
### Tactics
Gibbering Mouthers do not have tactics. They surge toward creatures and gibber. A summoner who calls one needs to accept that the Gibbering affect their allies too unless those allies are out of range. They are useful for area denial or as a terrifying distraction. Their cooperation is genuine; their execution is chaotic.

## Name: Star Spawn Grue
**Tier:** 1
**Origin:** Aberration (Space)
**Size:** Small
**HP:** 20 (Con 5 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** 2
**Speed:** 30 ft, Fly 30 ft (Hover)
**Attributes:** Str 3 / Con 5 / Agil 7 / Dex 4 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 4, Perception 5, Stealth 4
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Perfect Sight 30 ft **Initiative Bonus:** +4 (Agil 4) **Creature Type:** Aberration (immune to Frightened; Fortune on saves vs magic; does not need air, food, drink, or sleep)
### Attacks
Reality Pinch [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 4), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 9 damage (6 + 3 Str) + 3 psychic damage (Ignore DR)
- **Margin 2:** 9 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 psychic damage; target makes Will [Easy] save or Disoriented until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 3:** 9 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 psychic damage; Disoriented and Dazed until end of their next turn (no save).
### Special Abilities
Aura of Unreality: Creatures within 10 feet that are not Aberrations add 1 to both dice on attack rolls and skill checks.
Spatial Slip (1/turn): When the Grue is targeted by an attack and misses, it may teleport up to 10 feet as a [Free] action.
### Tactics
Star Spawn Grues are irritants at TR 1 but genuinely disruptive in groups. Their Aura of Unreality affects allies as well as enemies, which a summoner should account for. They use Spatial Slip to avoid being pinned and target spellcasters with Reality Pinch. An Indifferent Grue's concept of the instructions it was given may be subtly distorted in ways it doesn't understand itself.

## Name: Mindflayer
**Tier:** 3
**Origin:** Aberration (Space)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 30 (Con 6 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 2 (aberrant resilience)
**Speed:** 30 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 6 / Agil 7 / Dex 3 / Int 8 / Wis 6 / Cha 1 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 5, Perception 7, Arcane 7, Deception 5, Empathy 6
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Perfect Sight 30 ft, Telepathy 120 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Aberration (immune to Charmed; Fortune on saves vs magic; does not need air, food, drink, or sleep)
### Attacks
Tentacles [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 5), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 10 damage (6 + 4 Str) + 4 psychic damage (Ignore DR); target makes Fortitude [Easy] save or Grappled.
- **Margin 2:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up) + 8 psychic damage; Grappled (no save).
- **Margin 3:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up) + 8 psychic damage; Grappled and target's Intelligence reduced by 2 until end of encounter.
Extract Brain (Grappled targets only) [Combat]: Against one Grappled creature. Target makes Fortitude [Hard] save.
0 Successes: Instantly killed.
1 success: 10 damage and Stunned for 1 minute.
### Special Abilities
Mind Blast (Recharge 5-8) [Combat]: The Mindflayer emits psychic energy in a 60-foot cone. All creatures in the area make Will saves. Compare Int 8 + Arcane 7 targeting to each target's save successes.
- **Margin 1:**: 16 psychic damage + Dazed.
- **Margin 2:** 16 psychic damage, DR halved (round up) + Stunned for 1 minute (Will [Easy] save each turn ends).
Telepathy 120 ft: Communicates telepathically with any creature within range that has a language. It uses this to understand precisely what is being asked of it.
Mind Shield: Fortune on Will saves.
### Tactics
Mindflayers do not fight, they process problems. Mind Blast disrupts enemy formations while the Mindflayer positions for tentacle Grapples on isolated priority targets. They will protect themselves first and the summoner second and will telepathically communicate their assessment of the tactical situation whether asked or not. A Cooperative Mindflayer is a terrifying asset. A Friendly one is as close to a genuine partner as an Aberration gets, though it is curious about everything in an unsettling way.

## Name: Star Spawn Hulk
**Tier:** 4
**Origin:** Aberration (Space)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 4 (alien hide)
**Speed:** 30 ft
**Attributes:** Str 8 / Con 6 / Agil 4 / Dex 4 / Int 6 / Wis 5 / Cha 5 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 6, Might 7, Perception 4, Intimidate 5
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft, Blindsight 10 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +4 (Agil 4)
**Creature Type:** Aberration (immune to Frightened, Charmed; Fortune on saves vs magic; does not need air, food, drink, or sleep)
### Attacks
Slam [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 4 + Melee Combat 6), Reach: 15 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 18 damage (10 + 8 Str).
- **Margin 2:** 18 damage, DR halved (round up); target makes Fortitude [Easy] save or Stunned until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 3:** 18 damage, DR halved (round up); Stunned, no save.
Reality Shockwave (Recharge 5-8) [Combat]: All creatures within 30 feet make Will saves. Compare Hulk's Str 8 against each target's save.
- **Margin 1:**: 20 psychic damage + Disoriented,
- **Margin 2:** 20 psychic damage, DR halved (round up) + Dazed + Disoriented. Psychic damage ignores DR.
### Special Abilities
Psychic Mirror: When the Star Spawn Hulk is hit by a weave that deals psychic damage or imposes a mind-affecting condition, the attacker makes a Will [Easy] save or is also affected by the same condition. This is reflexive and not a choice.
Powerful Build: Counts as Gigantic for carrying, pushing, dragging, and lifting.
Alien Mind: The Hulk is immune to Charmed and to all mind-reading or telepathy effects. It cannot be communicated with through telepathy. A summoner's instructions are received as direct behavioral commands.
Resilient Defense (2/day): When the Star Spawn Hulk Fiend fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Tactics
Star Spawn Hulks advance and hit things. They lack tactical judgment but execute instructions with perfect obedience, which makes them reliable in a way that smarter Aberrations often are not. Reality Shockwave is used instinctively when multiple enemies cluster within range — the Hulk does not distinguish ally from enemy in that zone, which the summoner should communicate clearly before the encounter begins.

## Name: Beholder
**Tier:** 5
**Origin:** Aberration (Space)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 210 (Con 7 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 3 (chitinous hide)
**Speed:** 0 ft, Fly 20 ft (Hover)
**Attributes:** Str 3 / Con 7 / Agil 5 / Dex 6 / Int 8 / Wis 8 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Ranged Combat 6, Perception 8, Arcane 7
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Truesight 120 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Aberration (immune to Prone; Fortune on all saves vs magic due to Eye Wards; does not need air, food, drink, or sleep).
### Attacks
Central Eye (Passive): The central eye projects a cone of antimagic 60 feet long. Creatures and objects in the cone cannot use or benefit from weaves. Magical effects in the cone are suppressed. The cone is directed by the Beholder's will. It cannot be aimed away from all of its eyestalks simultaneously.
Eye Rays [Combat]: The Beholder uses 3 of its 8 eye rays per turn. Roll or select which rays fire. Range: 120 ft, Targeted:
Charm Ray: Will [Easy] save. Failure: Charmed for 1 hour (or until harmed).
Paralyzing Ray: Fortitude [Easy] save. Failure: Paralyzed for 1 minute.
Fear Ray: Will [Easy] save. Failure: Frightened (Fleeing) for 1 minute.
Slowing Ray: Will [Easy] save. Failure: Slowed (Major) for 1 minute.
Enervation Ray: Fortitude [Easy] save. Failure: 28 Negative damage (Half DR)
Telekinetic Ray: Fortitude [Easy] save. Failure: target is moved up to 30 feet in any direction.
Sleep Ray: Will [Easy] save. Failure: Unconscious for 1 minute.
Disintegration Ray: Fortitude [Hard] save. Failure: 40 Negative damage (Half DR). At 0 HP from this ray, target is disintegrated.
Bite [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 9 damage (6 + 3 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 9 damage, DR halved (round up)
### Special Abilities
Eye Wards: The Beholder's many eyes give it Fortune on all saves vs magic. It cannot be surprised while its eyes are open.
Antimagic Cone: As above. This is always active and the Beholder uses it as a weapon.
Resilient Defense (3/day): When the Beholder Fiend fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Alien Paranoia: Beholders distrust other Beholders and have extremely idiosyncratic definitions of "cooperation." A Summoner who achieves a Friendly Beholder result should understand that its Friendly disposition means it has decided the summoner is interesting and worth protecting, not that it necessarily agrees with the summoner's goals. It will pursue its own agenda in parallel unless its instructions are extremely specific.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Tactics
The Beholder positions its Antimagic Cone toward spellcasters and fires Eye Rays at everyone else. It selects rays based on perceived threat: Disintegration for primary threats, Charm for creatures it would rather use than destroy, Slowing for fast-moving opponents, Fear for clustered groups. A Cooperative Beholder follows target priority and is one of the most devastatingly effective summoned allies possible, as long as the summoner does not rely on weaves themselves while the central eye is pointed their way.

## Name: Void Mote
**Tier:** 1/8
**Origin:** Aberration (Space)
**Size:** Tiny
**HP:** 6 (Con 2 × 3, Fragile)
**DR:** 0
**Speed:** 0 ft, Fly 30 ft (hover)
**Attributes:** Str 1 / Con 2 / Agil 5 / Dex 3 / Int 3 / Wis 6 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 1, Stealth 4, Perception 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Tremorsense 30 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Aberration; Darkvision 120 ft. Immune to Frightened, Charm. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Reality Nibble [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 1), Reach: 5 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 3 damage (2 + 1 Str); target's next skill check adds 1 to the Skill Die.
- **Margin 2:** 3 damage, DR halved (round up); target's next skill check adds 1 to the both Dice; target makes Will [Easy] save or gains the Disoriented condition until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 3:** 3 damage, DR halved (round up); target's next skill check has Misfortune. Applies Disoriented condition until end of their next turn.
### Special Abilities
Swarm Cohesion: If 3 or more Void Motes are within 10 feet of the same target, they each subtract 1 from both attack dice.
Phase Flicker: When the Void Mote takes damage, it briefly phases between the prime material and the Space between planes, teleporting 1d8 × 5 feet in a random direction as a reaction.
Incoherence Field: Opportunity ### Attacks made by Void Motes have Fortune.
### Description
Void Motes are the smallest coherent expressions of Space-between-planes existence that can be called through a summoning. They are not intelligent in any useful sense — they have imperatives but not goals, responses but not reasoning. Their value in combat is almost entirely as a nuisance multiplier: individually they deal negligible damage and their skill check penalty is minor. Three of them around a single target simultaneously, applying Swarm Cohesion to their ### Attacks and stacking the skill check penalty, becomes a meaningful debuff. A summoner who calls a Void Mote expecting a combat asset will be disappointed. One who calls several and directs them to swarm a single high-priority caster or skillful opponent will find the cumulative reality disruption degrades that opponent's effectiveness across the encounter.

## Name: Seam Crawler
**Tier:** 1/4
**Origin:** Aberration (Space)
**Size:** Small
**HP:** 12 (Con 3 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** 1
**Speed:** 30 ft, Climb 30 ft, Fly 20 ft
**Attributes:** Str 2 / Con 3 / Agil 5 / Dex 5 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 2 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 2, Stealth 5, Perception 4, Arcane 2
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Tremorsense 30 ft, Seam Sense 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Aberration; Darkvision 120 ft. Immune to Frightened, Charm. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Phase Claw [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 2), Reach: 5 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 6 damage (4 + 2 Str); target's DR reduced by 1 for 1 minute.
- **Margin 2:** 6 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 force damage (Ignore DR); DR reduced by 2 for 1 minute.
- **Margin 3:** 6 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 force damage; DR reduced by 3 for 1 minute.
### Special Abilities
Phase Walk: Once per round, can move through a single solid object up to 5 feet thick as part of its movement. It must end its movement in a legal (unoccupied, solid-floored) space.
DR Erosion: The DR reduction from Phase Claw stacks across hits. A target that has been hit twice has DR reduced by 2 (or more, depending on margins). This reduction applies to all damage types.
### Description
Seam Crawlers are scouts and compromisers — creatures that perceive weak points in physical and planar barriers and erode them. Their Phase Claw's DR reduction is their primary contribution to an encounter: a target that has taken three or four hits from a Seam Crawler is substantially less protected against everything else in the fight. Phase Walk allows them to pass through walls to flank, escape, or reach a target that has retreated behind cover. A summoner who uses one to pre-erode a high-DR target's defenses before committing larger assets will find it pays dividends disproportionate to its tier. The Seam Sense ability is also useful outside of combat — a summoner who wants to know whether a location has a thin boundary to a specific plane can direct the Seam Crawler to survey and report back.

## Name: Fracture Crawler
**Tier:** 1/2
**Origin:** Aberration (Space)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 16 (Con 4 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** 2
**Speed:** 40 ft, Climb 40 ft, Fly 30 ft
**Attributes:** Str 3 / Con 4 / Agil 7 / Dex 4 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 3, Stealth 5, Perception 4, Arcane 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Tremorsense 60 ft, Seam Sense 120 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Aberration; Darkvision 120 ft. Immune to Frightened, Charm. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Reality Fracture [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 5 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 10 damage (7 + 3 Str); target's DR reduced by 2 for 1 minute; target must succeed on a Will [Easy] save or lose 1 action on their next turn.
- **Margin 2:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 force damage (Ignore DR); DR reduced by 3; target must succeed on a Will [Hard] save or lose 1 action on their next turn; target Disoriented for 1 minute.
- **Margin 3:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 force damage; DR reduced by 4; target must succeed on a Will [Hard] save or lose 1 action on their next turn; target Disoriented for 1 minute; target makes Will [Hard] save or loses their [Reaction] until the start of their next turn.
### Special Abilities
Phase Walk: Once per round, can move through a single solid object up to 10 feet thick as part of its movement. It must end its movement in a legal (unoccupied, solid-floored) space.
Fracture Aura: Non Fracture Crawler’s that start their turn within 10 feet of the Fracture Crawler must succeed on a Will [Easy] save or all their ranged ### Attacks this turn add 1 to both attack dice.
Reality Tear (1/short rest) [Combat]: As a [Combat] action, the Fracture Crawler tears a 10-foot diameter hole in the planar fabric at a location within 30 feet. The tear persists for 1 minute. Creatures that pass through it are subjected to 10 force damage (Ignore DR) and must make a Will [Easy] save or be teleported 1d8× 10 feet in a random direction. The tear is visible to Arcane-skilled characters as a faint ripple in the air and appears on any planar detection effect.
### Description
Fracture Crawlers operate at the edge of what is comprehensible about force damage — they do not simply damage things, they damage the relationship between things. The action loss from Reality Fracture is disruptive in ways that go beyond the lost action: a creature that loses one of its three actions has fundamentally changed what it can accomplish on its turn, and the loss stacks badly with Disoriented if both conditions apply. Fracture Aura makes staying near one actively costly. Reality Tear creates a hazard that forces enemies to make choices about movement that they would not otherwise face. A summoner who places a Fracture Crawler in a chokepoint and lets Fracture Aura work on enemies passing through while Reality Tear threatens anyone attempting to advance will find it controls space in a way that belies its tier.

## Name: Void Gnasher
**Tier:** 1
**Origin:** Aberration (Space)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 3
**Speed:** 40 ft, Fly 40 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 6 / Agil 8 / Dex 3 / Int 3 / Wis 4 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 4, Perception 4, Athletics 5, Intimidate 4
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Tremorsense 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Aberration; Darkvision 120 ft. Immune to Frightened, Charm. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Gnashing Maw [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 4), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 9 damage (5 + 4 Str) + 3 force damage (Ignore DR); target is Grappled if they are Medium or smaller.
- **Margin 2:** 9 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 force damage; Grappled; target takes 6 damage at the start of each of their turns while Grappled; target Disoriented while Grappled.
- **Margin 3:** 9 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 force damage; Target is Pinned and suffers 6 damage at the start of each of their turns while Pinned or Grappled; target Disoriented while Pinned or Grappled.
Reality Crush [Combat]: May only target a Grappled or Pinned creature: 
- **Margin 1:**: 14 force damage (Ignore DR); target's Fortitude [Easy] save or Stunned until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 2:** 14 force damage, DR halved (round up); Fortitude [Hard] save or Stunned until end of their next turn.; Fortitude [Easy] save or Weakened for 1 minute.
- **Margin 3:** 14 force damage, DR halved (round up); Fortitude [Hard] save or Stunned until end of their next turn.; Fortitude [Hard] save or Weakened for 1 minute. Fortitude [Hard] save or takes 20 points of damage to their maximum HP.
### Special Abilities
Aberrant Resilience: The Void Gnasher's body does not respond to pain or physical trauma in any predictable way. It does not gain the Hindered condition from standard pain-based effects. Effects that specifically say "creatures immune to pain" function normally against it.
Swallow Whole (1/encounter): If the Void Gnasher has a target Grappled and reduced to below half their maximum HP, it can attempt to Swallow them as a [Combat] action. The target makes a Fortitude [Easy] save. On failure, they are Swallowed: they take 6 force damage (Ignore DR) at the start of each of their turns, are Blinded and Immobilized. Swallowed creatures are expelled if the Gnasher dies.
Resilient Defense (1/day): When the Void Gnasher fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
Void Gnashers are blunt instruments — large, durable, capable of Grappling targets and then applying Reality Crush or Swallow Whole to remove them from the encounter entirely. Their intelligence is below the threshold for complex instruction-following. A summoner who gives one a simple, concrete direction ("grapple that target, do not release it") will find it executes that instruction reliably. One who gives complex conditional instructions will find the Gnasher defaulting to its base imperatives, which is to find something to bite. For the specific function of taking a single high-priority target out of an encounter for several rounds, it is unusually effective at its tier. The force damage on Gnashing Maw ignoring DR means armor provides limited protection.

## Name: Rift Feeder
**Tier:** 2
**Origin:** Aberration (Space)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 150 (Con 5 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 3
**Speed:** 0 ft, Fly 60 ft (hover)
**Attributes:** Str 3 / Con 5 / Agil 3 / Dex 8 / Int 4 / Wis 6 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 4, Ranged Combat 5, Perception 6, Arcane 4, Stealth 4
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Seam Sense 120 ft, See Invisibility
**Initiative Bonus:** +3 (Agil 3)
**Creature Type:** Aberration; Darkvision 120 ft. Immune to Frightened, Charm. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Rift Beam [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 8 + Ranged Combat 5), Range: 80 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 14 force damage (6 + 8 Dex); target's DR reduced by 2 for 1 minute; target makes Will [Easy] save or Disoriented until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 2:** 14 force damage, DR halved (round up); DR reduced by 3; target makes Will [Hard] save or Disoriented until end of their next turn; target makes Will [Easy] save or loses 1 [Reaction] until start of their next turn.
- **Margin 3:** 14 force damage, DR halved (round up); DR reduced by 4; target makes Will [Hard] save or Disoriented until end of their next turn; target makes Will [Hard] save or loses 1 [Reaction] until start of their next turn; target makes Reflex [Easy] save or is pulled 15 feet directly toward the Rift Feeder.
Rift Pulse [Combat — Recharge 6-8]: 30-foot diameter centered on itself. All creatures in radius:
- **Margin 1:**: Will [Easy] save or suffer 8 force damage (Ignore DR), Disoriented for 1 minute.
- **Margin 2:** Will [Easy] save or suffer 16 force damage (Ignore DR), Disoriented for 1 minute, and DR reduced by 3 for 1 minute.
### Special Abilities
Spatial Hunger: The Rift Feeder feeds on planar boundaries. When it deals force damage to a creature, it regains HP equal to half the force damage dealt (rounded down). This includes Rift Pulse.
Rift Anchor: Once per encounter, as a [Combat] action, the Rift Feeder designates a 20-foot diameter area as an anchor zone. Creatures within the anchor zone cannot use teleportation, planar travel, or planar step abilities for 1 minute. The Rift Feeder is not affected by its own anchor.
Hovering Form: Has no ground speed. Cannot be Tripped or Knocked Prone.
Resilient Defense (1/day): When the Rift Feeder fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
Rift Feeders exist at an interface between hunting and feeding that involves consuming the fabric of space around their targets rather than the targets themselves. The regeneration from Spatial Hunger means they sustain themselves through dealing damage — a Rift Feeder at the center of a fight is healing as it contributes, which makes attrition strategies ineffective against one. Rift Anchor is specifically designed to counter summon-heavy opponents or mages who rely on Planar Step — it removes that option from the zone for the duration. A summoner who faces an opponent with significant planar movement capability and calls a Rift Feeder as the counter will find the lockdown cleanly removes that option from the encounter. The healing sustains the Rift Feeder through retaliation while it continues dealing DR-eroding force damage.

## Name: Void Caller
**Tier:** 3
**Origin:** Aberration (Space)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 4
**Speed:** 20 ft, Fly 60 ft
**Attributes:** Str 3 / Con 6 / Agil 4 / Dex 4 / Int 7 / Wis 6 / Cha 5 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 5, Ranged Combat 6, Perception 7, Arcane 7, Deception 5, Intimidate 5
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Seam Sense 300 ft, See Invisibility, Perfect Sight 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +4 (Agil 4)
**Creature Type:** Aberration; Darkvision 120 ft. Immune to Frightened, Charm. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Void Tendril [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 4 + Ranged Combat 6), Range: 60 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 16 force damage (12 + 4 Dex); target loses 3 from Current Luck; target makes Will [Easy] save or Confused until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 2:** 16 force damage, DR halved (round up); Current Luck reduced by 4; target makes Will [Hard] save or Confused until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 3:** 16 force damage, DR halved (round up); Current Luck reduced by 5; target makes Will [Hard] save or Confused for 1 minute; target must succeed on Will [Hard] save or be Dominated for 1 round: they take 1 action this round following the Voidcaller's instruction before the Confusion reasserts.
### Special Abilities
The Signal: Any creature that begins its turn within 30 feet of the Voidcaller must succeed on a Will [Easy] save or take 8 force damage (Ignore DR) and lose 2 from Current Luck. Creatures reduced to 0 Current Luck by this effect fall Unconscious for 1 hour.
Spatial Awareness: The Voidcaller knows the precise location of every creature within 300 feet regardless of concealment, invisibility, or barriers.
Void Gate (1/day) [Combat]: As 2 [Combat] actions, opens a two-way gate to the Space between planes at a point within 60 feet. Creatures that enter the gate voluntarily or are pushed through it are expelled 1d8 × 30 feet in a random direction. The gate persists for 1 minute and is 10 feet in diameter. The Voidcaller can close it early as a free action.
Arcane Disruption: When the Voidcaller is targeted by a weave that requires an attack roll, it may use its [Reaction] to force the attacker to reroll, taking the worse result. Once per encounter.
Resilient Defense (2/day): When the Voidcaller fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
The Voidcaller is the first Aberration in this tier with genuine intelligence — not intelligence in the sense of mortal cognitive capability, but something that can evaluate situations, anticipate responses, and adjust behavior accordingly. The Signal is a passive effect it does not consciously control, which creates a complication for a summoner who needs allies nearby: the Signal damages and drains Current Luck from everyone within 30 feet without discrimination. A summoner who deploys one should plan for allies to maintain distance or accept ongoing Luck drain. Spatial Awareness means it cannot be flanked, ambushed, or approached from an unexpected angle — it always knows exactly where every creature is in a significant radius. Void Gate creates displacement hazards that can remove targets from effective positions. The Confusion from Void Tendril turns targets against their allies for the duration, which can be more disruptive than the damage.

## Name: Rift Warden
**Tier:** 4
**Origin:** Aberration (Space)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 5
**Speed:** 30 ft, Fly 70 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 6 / Agil 5 / Dex 6 / Int 6 / Wis 6 / Cha 5 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 6, Ranged Combat 7, Perception 8, Arcane 8, Deception 6, Intimidate 6
**Senses:** Darkvision unlimited, Seam Sense unlimited, See Invisibility, Perfect Sight 120 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Aberration; Darkvision unlimited. Immune to Frightened, Charm. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Rift Eye [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 6 + Ranged Combat 7), Range: 150 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 18 force damage (12 + 6 Dex); target loses 4 Current Luck; target makes Will [Easy] save or Confused for 1 minute; target's DR reduced by 3 for 1 minute.
- **Margin 2:** 18 force damage, DR halved (round up); Current Luck reduced by 6; target makes Will [Hard] save or Confused for 1 minute; DR reduced by 4; target makes Reflex [Easy] save or loses their [Reaction] until the end of their next turn.
- **Margin 3:** 18 force damage, DR halved (round up); Current Luck reduced by 8; target makes Will [Hard] save or Confused for 1 minute; DR reduced by 5; target makes Reflex [Hard] save or loses their [Reaction] until the end of their next turn.
Eye Rays (Recharge 5-8) [Combat]: The Rift Warden fires rays from multiple eyes in a 60-foot cone. Each creature in the cone is subjected to the effects of one randomly determined eye:
(1) Disorientation: Disoriented for 1 minute, no save.
(2) Spatial Crush:  24 force damage (Ignore DR).
(3) Luck Drain: lose 6 Current Luck.
(4) Ability Severance: lose access to one magical ability for 1 hour, Will save (Hard, 2 successes) to resist.
 (5) Petrification: Fortitude save (1 success) or Slowed for 1 minute (2 actions instead of 3).
(6) Spatial Shunt — teleported 1d6 × 20 feet in a random direction, Fortitude save (1 success) to resist.
### Special Abilities
Partial Discontinuity: The Rift Warden's body exists partially between planes. ### Attacks against it have a 25% chance (roll 6-8 on d8) of passing through a discontinuous portion and dealing no damage. This is determined per hit, after the attack resolves. Area effects are not subject to this.
The Signal: Any creature that begins its turn within 60 feet of the Rift Warden must succeed on a Will [Easy] save or take 12 force damage (Ignore DR) and lose 3 from Current Luck. Creatures reduced to 0 Current Luck by this effect fall Unconscious for 1 hour.
 Spatial Dominion (1/day) [Combat]: As a [Combat] action, the Rift Warden selects a 60-foot diameter for 1 minute. Within this zone: all teleportation is blocked (as Rift Anchor); difficult terrain costs triple movement; all ranged ### Attacks beyond 30 feet add 2 to both attack dice; the Rift Warden's own movement costs are halved. The Rift Warden cannot be moved out of this zone involuntarily while it persists.
Antimagic Gaze (1/encounter) [Combat]: As a [Combat] action, fixes its gaze on a single target within 60 feet. That target's magical abilities are suppressed for 1 round; they cannot use weaves, magical abilities, or magical items. No save. At the end of the round, normal function resumes.
Resilient Defense (2/day): When the Rift Warden fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
The Rift Warden is described in summoning records as something that has existed between planes long enough that the plane it came from has become secondary to what it has become. Its Partial Discontinuity means it absorbs roughly 25% fewer hits than its HP would suggest — ### Attacks that land occasionally strike portions of it that are not, in that moment, present. Spatial Dominion locks down a 60-foot zone in ways that disadvantage every conventional fighting approach simultaneously. Eye Rays on recharge hits every target in a cone with different effects, meaning the outcome of its activation depends on what the specific targets roll. The Enhanced Signal damages and drains every non-Aberration creature within 60 feet per round, which means positioning allies is a significant tactical concern when a Rift Warden is on the field. A summoner who calls one should prepare for a battlefield that has fundamentally changed in spatial terms — the rules that normally govern movement, range, and tactical positioning are operating differently in its presence.

## Name: The Unbound
**Tier:** 5
**Origin:** Aberration (Space)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 210 (Con 7 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 6
**Speed:** 0 ft, Fly 80 ft (hover, ignores difficult terrain and environmental movement penalties)
**Attributes:** Str 5 / Con 7 / Agil 5 / Dex 6 / Int 6 / Wis 6 / Cha 6 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 6, Ranged Combat 7, Perception 8, Arcane 8, Deception 7, Intimidate 7
**Senses:** Darkvision unlimited, Seam Sense unlimited, See Invisibility, Perfect Sight 120 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Aberration; Darkvision unlimited. Immune to Frightened, Charm. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Reality Erasure [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 6 + Ranged Combat 7), Range: 200 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 20 force damage (14 + 6 Dex); target loses 6 Current Luck; DR reduced by 4 for 1 minute; target makes Will [Easy] save or Confused for 1 minute.
- **Margin 2:** 20 force damage, DR halved (round up); Current Luck reduced by 8; DR reduced by 6; target makes Will [Hard] save or Confused for 1 minute; target makes Reflex [Easy] save or loses their [Reaction] until the end of their next turn.
- **Margin 3:** 20 force damage, DR halved (round up); Current Luck reduced by 10; DR reduced by 8; target makes Will [Hard] save or Confused for 1 minute; target makes Reflex [Hard] save or loses their [Reaction] until the end of their next turn.
Void Cascade [Combat — Recharge 6-8]: 40-foot diameter centered on a point within 150 feet. All creatures in radius:
- **Margin 1:**: 20 force damage (14 + 6 Dex); Current Luck reduced by 3; DR reduced by 2 for 1 minute.
- **Margin 2:** 20 force damage, DR halved (round up); Current Luck reduced by 6; DR reduced by 4 for 1 minute; Will [Easy] save or Disoriented + Confused for 1 minute.
### Special Abilities
The Signal: Any creature that begins its turn within 90 feet of the Unbound must succeed on a Will [Hard] save or take 16 force damage (Ignore DR) and lose 4 from Current Luck. Creatures reduced to 0 Current Luck by this effect fall Unconscious for 1 hour.
Spatial Sovereignty: As a [Combat] action, the Unbound selects a 120-foot diameter for 10 minutes. Within this zone: all teleportation is blocked (as Rift Anchor); difficult terrain costs triple movement; all ranged ### Attacks beyond 30 feet add 2 to both attack dice; the Unbound's own movement costs are halved. The Unbound cannot be moved out of this zone involuntarily while it persists.
Partial Discontinuity (Enhanced): The Unbound's body exists partially between planes. ### Attacks against it have a 50% chance (roll 5-8 on d8) of passing through a discontinuous portion and dealing no damage. This is determined per hit, after the attack resolves. Area effects are not subject to this.
- Eye Rays (Enhanced — 1/round, no recharge): As Rift Warden's Eye Rays but 80-foot cone. Activates once per round on the Unbound's turn at no action cost — it is not using an action to fire them, it simply does.
- Reality Anchor Destruction: The Unbound can, as a [Combat] action, target any magical effect, summoning circle, planar boundary marker, or similar spatial construct within 60 feet and attempt to unmake it. The effect ends immediately unless it was created by a TR 5 creature or a weave of cost 12 or higher, in which case the creator makes an opposed Arcane check against the Unbound's Arcane 8 to maintain it.
Resilient Defense (3/day): When the Unbound fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
The Unbound is named for what it no longer is. At some point in the incomprehensibly long period of its existence in the Space between planes, it ceased to be bounded by the rules that govern creatures from specific planes. It does not have a home plane. It does not have a form that is consistently present in any given plane. It has imperatives and it has a perspective that has been developing since before the current configuration of planes existed. When a summoner binds one, they are not binding a creature to their will — they are entering into a temporary agreement with something that has agreed to be bound, for reasons that may relate to the summoner's instructions or may relate to something the Unbound has decided the summoner's instructions will accomplish that the summoner has not yet understood. The agreement holds. The Unbound does not break bindings. But a summoner who has called one should be aware that something with a perspective that old has context they do not have.

Mechanically: Spatial Sovereignty reshapes a 120-foot zone, Enhanced Discontinuity reduces effective damage by 50%, Eye Rays fire every round at no action cost, Void Cascade hits an entire area for 32 force damage with no save on the damage, and the Overwhelming Signal removes creatures from the encounter entirely if their Current Luck reaches 0. A summoner who calls one into a combat encounter has introduced something that will end the encounter. The question of how the encounter ends, and what conclusions the Unbound draws from the experience, is worth considering before the binding is completed.


## Name: Drowned Mariner
**Tier:** 2
**Creature Type:** Undead (Shadow)
All Shadow creatures share: Darkvision 120 ft. Shadow Meld (passive) — in dim light or darkness, Fortune on Stealth checks and ### Attacks against them add 1 to both dice. Positive energy vulnerability — full damage from Positive energy, no DR reduction. Cold Resistance (+2 DR). Do not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
Sunlight Sensitivity is noted per entry where it applies. Not all Shadow creatures suffer equally in bright light — some have adapted through exposure to the prime material, and this is reflected in their individual entries.
**Origin:** Native waters
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 20 (Con 5 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** 2 (waterlogged flesh)
**Speed:** 20 ft, Swim 40 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 5 / Agil 7 / Dex 5 / Int 4 / Wis 5 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 5, Athletics 4, Perception 3, Stealth 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Undead (immune to poison damage, Poisoned condition, disease, exhaustion; does not need air, food, drink, or sleep)
**Languages:** Understands the local regional language it knew in life; cannot speak
### Attacks
Rusting Cutlass [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 5), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 10 damage (6 + 4 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up)
Drowning Grip: When the Drowned Mariner hits with Margin 2 while in water, the target must make a Fortitude save against the mariner's Strength 4 + Athletics 4. On a failure, the target is Grappled and pulled 10 feet toward deeper water.
### Special Abilities
Waterlogged Persistence: The Drowned Mariner has Fortune on checks to resist forced movement while in water. It can remain submerged indefinitely.
Drag Below: A creature Grappled by the Drowned Mariner while submerged gains the Drowning condition. The creature can escape normally, but the mariner always attempts to pull it deeper.
### Tactics
Drowned Mariners attack from below docks, wrecks, and dark water. They grapple the nearest target and drag it away from the vessel before turning on anyone who attempts a rescue. They do not pursue prey onto dry land unless commanded or protecting the site of their death.
### Equipment
Rusting cutlass, torn sailor's coat, barnacle-covered belt, length of chain

## Name: Ghoul
**Tier:** 2
**Origin:** Undead (Shadow)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 20 (Con 5 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** 0
**Speed:** 30 ft, Climb 30 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 5 / Agil 7 / Dex 5 / Int 4 / Wis 5 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 5, Stealth 4, Perception 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Undead (Immune to poison damage, Poisoned condition, disease, exhaustion. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.)
### Attacks
Bite [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 5), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 10 damage (6 + 4 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 10 damage, DR halved (round up)
Claws [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 5), Reach: 5 ft
- **Margin 1:**: 8 damage (4 + 4 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 8 damage, DR halved (round up)
Special: On hit target must make Fortitude [Easy] save or become Paralyzed for 1 minute. Paralyzed creatures can repeat save at end of each turn (2 successes ends early, 1 success adds Fortune to next save)
### Special Abilities
Paralytic Claws: Creatures hit by claw ### Attacks must save vs paralysis (see Claws attack).
Spider Climb: Can climb difficult surfaces including upside-down on ceilings without ability checks.
Paralyze Immunity: Ghouls and other undead are immune to ghoul paralysis.
### Tactics
Ghouls use stealth and climbing to ambush prey from unexpected angles. They use Multiattack to maximize chances of landing paralytic claw strikes, then feast on paralyzed victims. They gang up on dangerous foes to trigger multiple paralysis saves.

## Name: Shade
**Tier:** 1/8
**Origin:** Undead (Shadow)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 6 (Con 2 × 3, Fragile)
**DR:** 0
**Speed:** 40 ft
**Attributes:** Str 1 / Con 2 / Agil 6 / Dex 3 / Int 3 / Wis 5 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 1, Stealth 5, Perception 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Shadow; Darkvision 120 ft. Shadow Meld (passive). Positive energy vulnerability. Cold Resistance (+2 DR). Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
Sunlight Sensitivity: In bright light, adds 1 to both attack dice and skill check dice.
### Attacks
Strength Drain [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 1), Reach: 5 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 5 Negative damage (4 + 1 Str); target's Strength reduced by 1 for 1 hour.
- **Margin 2:** 5 Negative damage, DR halved (round up); Strength reduced by 2 for 1 hour; target gains Weakened condition.
- **Margin 3:** 5 Negative damage, DR halved (round up); Strength reduced by 3 for 1 hour; Weakened; target makes Fortitude [Easy] save or movement speed reduced by 10 until Strength restores.
### Special Abilities
Amorphous: Can move through spaces as narrow as 1 inch without squeezing.
Shadow Form: While in dim light or darkness, DR increases to 6 against all non-magical damage and non-Positive energy damage. Positive energy ignores this entirely.
Shadow Spawn: If a non-undead creature is killed while their Strength has been reduced to 0 by the Shade's ### Attacks, a new Shade rises from their shadow at the start of the next round. This new Shade is not under the summoner's control — it is a free Shadow creature that has just arrived on the prime material through the death. The summoner's Summoning Limit does not apply to it.
### Description
Shades are the most basic expression of the Shadow Plane's predatory nature; a barely-manifested hunger that drains strength through contact. Their combat capability is minimal against anything that fights back effectively, but Strength Drain accumulates across hits in a way that compounds. Shadow Form in darkness makes them genuinely difficult to put down through mundane means. 

## Name: Shadow Lurker
**Tier:** 1/4
**Origin:** Undead (Shadow)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 12 (Con 3 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** 1
**Speed:** 40 ft, Climb 40 ft
**Attributes:** Str 2 / Con 3 / Agil 6 / Dex 5 / Int 3 / Wis 4 / Cha 2 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 2, Stealth 6, Perception 4, Athletics 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Shadow; Darkvision 120 ft. Shadow Meld (passive). Positive energy vulnerability. Cold Resistance (+2 DR). Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
Sunlight Sensitivity: In bright light, adds 1 to both attack dice and skill check dice.
### Attacks
Shadow Claw [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 2), Reach: 5 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 6 damage (4 + 2 Str) + 2 Negative damage (Half DR); target's Current Luck reduced by 1.
- **Margin 2:** 6 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 Negative damage; Current Luck reduced by 2; target gains Disoriented condition until end of their next turn.
- **Margin 3:** 6 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 Negative damage; Current Luck reduced by 3; Disoriented; target makes Will [Easy] save or Frightened until end of their next turn.
### Special Abilities
Amorphous: Can move through spaces as narrow as 1 inch without squeezing.
Shadow Form: While in dim light or darkness, DR increases to 6 against all non-magical damage and non-Positive energy damage. Positive energy ignores this entirely.
Ceiling Stalker: Can move across ceilings and vertical surfaces without Athletics checks. When attacking from above, subtract 1 from both attack dice.
### Description
Shadow Lurkers hunt from above. They use Ceiling Stalker to position on ceilings or high walls, drop onto targets from advantage, and drain Current Luck — which degrades save capability in a way that compounds across the encounter. A target who has taken three hits from a Shadow Lurker is functionally unable to resist saves effectively for the rest of the session. A summoner who calls one to soften a high-priority target before a difficult encounter, or to reduce a spellcaster's save capability during a negotiation that might turn hostile, will find it performs that specific function cleanly. It will not tell the summoner what it has learned about the environment from its ceiling vantage. A summoner who asks will get an accurate answer.

## Name: Shadow Walker
**Tier:** 1/2
**Origin:** Undead (Shadow)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 20 (Con 4 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 2 (shadow substance)
**Speed:** 40 ft, Climb 40 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 4 / Agil 7 / Dex 3 / Int 4 / Wis 4 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 2, Stealth 6, Perception 4, Deception 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, See Invisibility
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Shadow; Darkvision 120 ft. Shadow Meld (passive). Positive energy vulnerability. Cold Resistance (+2 DR). Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
Sunlight Sensitivity: In bright light, adds 1 to both attack dice and skill check dice.
### Attacks
Shadow Strike [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 2), Reach: 5 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 8 damage (4 + 4 Str) + 2 Negative damage (Ignore DR); target loses 2 from Current Luck.
- **Margin 2:** 8 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 Negative damage; Current Luck reduced by 3; target Disoriented until start of their next turn; target loses the Hidden condition if they had it.
- **Margin 3:** 8 damage, DR halved (round up) + 2 Negative damage; Current Luck reduced by 4; Disoriented; target also Dazed until end of their next turn.
### Special Abilities
Amorphous: Can move through spaces as narrow as 1 inch without squeezing.
Shadow Form: While in dim light or darkness, DR increases to 6 against all non-magical damage and non-Positive energy damage. Positive energy ignores this entirely.
Planar Step (1/short rest): As a [Move] action, steps briefly into the Shadow Plane and re-emerges up to 30 feet away in any dim or dark location it can see. Does not provoke opportunity ### Attacks. Cannot be used in bright light.
See Invisibility: Sees invisible creatures and objects within normal sight range.
### Description
Shade Walkers are assassins. They use Planar Step to close distance without exposure, strike from darkness, and retreat before retaliation. See Invisibility means they are not countered by the standard defensive measure of simply turning invisible.
