The Negative damage on Shadow Strike ignores DR entirely at - **Margin 1:** — the shadow energy bypasses physical protection because it is not attacking the body but the life force animating it. A summoner who needs a specific target's Luck depleted quickly, or who needs an invisible or concealed target hit without first locating them, will find Shade Walkers capable of both. Their intelligence is sufficient to follow moderately complex instructions and they do not require simplification.

## Name: Dark Stalker
**Tier:** 1
**Origin:** Undead (Shadow)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 20 (Con 5 × 4, Standard)
**DR:** 3 (dense shadow-substance)
**Speed:** 50 ft, Climb 50 ft
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 5 / Agil 8 / Dex 3 / Int 3 / Wis 4 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 4, Stealth 7, Perception 5, Deception 4, Athletics 4
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Devil's Sight, See Invisibility
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Shadow; Darkvision 120 ft. Shadow Meld (passive). Positive energy vulnerability. Cold Resistance (+2 DR). Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Shadow Blade [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 4), Reach: 5 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 9 damage (5 + 4 Str) + 3 Negative damage (Ignore DR); target loses 3 from Current Luck.
- **Margin 2:** 9 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 Negative damage; Current Luck reduced by 4; target Disoriented + Dazed until end of their next turn; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 9 damage, DR halved (round up) + 3 Negative damage; Current Luck reduced by 5; Disoriented + Dazed; target makes Will save (1 success) or Frightened until end of their next.
Shadow Bolt [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 3), Range: 60 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 11 Negative damage (8 + 3 Dex); target loses 2 from Current Luck.
- **Margin 2:** 11 Negative damage, DR halved (round up) + Disoriented until end of their next turn; Current Luck reduced by 3; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 11 Negative damage, DR halved (round up) + Dazed for 1 minute; Current Luck reduced by 4; Will save each round to end.
### Special Abilities
Shadow Form: While in dim light or darkness, DR increases to 8 against all non-magical damage and non-Positive energy damage. Positive energy ignores this entirely.
Planar Step (2/short rest): As a [Move] action, steps briefly into the Shadow Plane and re-emerges up to 30 feet away in any dim or dark location it can see. Does not provoke opportunity ### Attacks. Cannot be used in bright light.
Devil's Sight: Sees through magical darkness as if bright light.
Death Sense: Automatically detects creatures below half HP within 60 feet and knows their precise location regardless of concealment or invisibility.
Shadow Ambush: When the Dark Stalker ### Attacks on the same turn it used Planar Step, it has Fortune on that attack.
### Description
Dark Stalkers are the first Shadow creature with genuine tactical sophistication. They manage their Planar Steps as resources — one to open with Shadow Ambush from an unexpected angle, one held for extraction when the engagement turns unfavorable. Death Sense ensures they always know which target is most vulnerable. Shadow Form in darkness gives them DR 8 against mundane ### Attacks, making them effectively immune to conventional weapons while in their preferred environment. A summoner who controls lighting conditions — ensuring the engagement happens in dim light or darkness — will find a Dark Stalker performs at well above its TR. The creature is also the first Shadow creature that actively evaluates when retreat serves its interests better than continued engagement, which it will execute without prompting if the tactical situation warrants it.

## Name: Void Shade
**Tier:** 3
**Origin:** Undead (Shadow)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 4 (concentrated shadow-matter)
**Speed:** 50 ft, Fly 60 ft
**Attributes:** Str 5 / Con 6 / Agil 8 / Dex 3 / Int 6 / Wis 6 / Cha 1 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 6, Stealth 7, Perception 6, Deception 5, Arcane 4
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Devil's Sight, See Invisibility, Perfect Sight 30 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Shadow; Darkvision 120 ft. Shadow Meld (passive). Positive energy vulnerability. Cold Resistance (+2 DR). Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Void Touch [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 6), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 12 damage (7 + 5 Str) + 4 Negative damage (Ignore DR); target loses 4 from Current Luck; target's maximum HP reduced by 8 until long rest (life drain).
- **Margin 2:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 Negative damage; Current Luck reduced by 5; max HP reduced by 12; target Weakened + Sickened for 1 minute; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 12 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 Negative damage; Current Luck reduced by 6; max HP reduced by 16; Weakened + Sickened; target makes Fortitude [Easy] save or gains 1 Exhaustion level.
Shadow Tendril [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 5), Range: 60 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 15 Negative damage (12 + 3 Dex); target loses 3 from Current Luck; max HP reduced by 6.
- **Margin 2:** 15 Negative damage, DR halved (round up); Current Luck reduced by 4; max HP reduced by 10; Weakened for 1 minute; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 15 Negative damage, DR halved (round up); Current Luck reduced by 5; max HP reduced by 14; Weakened + Disoriented for 1 minute; no save.
### Special Abilities
Shadow Form: While in dim light or darkness, DR increases to 10 against all non-magical damage and non-Positive energy damage. Positive energy ignores this entirely.
Life Drain Aura: Creatures within 10 feet that are not Shadow creatures lose 2 from their maximum HP at the start of each of their turns. This maximum HP loss persists until long rest. A creature reduced to 0 maximum HP by this aura dies.
Darkness Shroud (1/encounter) [Combat]: As a [Combat] action, creates a 30-foot diameter of magical darkness centered on itself. This darkness persists for 1 minute and moves with the Void Shade. The Void Shade sees through it normally. Other Shadow creatures see through it.
Planar Step (3/short rest): As a [Move] action, steps briefly into the Shadow Plane and re-emerges up to 30 feet away in any dim or dark location it can see. Does not provoke opportunity ### Attacks. Cannot be used in bright light.
Resilient Defense (2/day): When the Void Shade fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
Void Shades introduce life drain as a sustained battlefield effect rather than a single-hit consequence. The Life Drain Aura erodes maximum HP from everything nearby every round, creating a timer that forces enemies to either engage the Void Shade directly or retreat from its proximity. Darkness Shroud combined with the Void Shade's ability to see through it creates a zone where it fights at full effectiveness against Blinded opponents. Shadow Form at DR 10 in darkness makes conventional weapons nearly useless against it while the darkness is active. A summoner who establishes dim or dark conditions before calling a Void Shade will find it functions at a tier above its rating. One who calls it into a brightly lit environment will find it still capable but operating at a disadvantage it will attempt to remedy by moving toward whatever shadow is available.

## Name: Shadow Lord
**Tier:** 4
**Origin:** Undead (Shadow)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 180 (Con 6 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 5 (ancient shadow-matter)
**Speed:** 60 ft, Fly 80 ft
**Attributes:** Str 6 / Con 6 / Agil 8 / Dex 3 / Int 5 / Wis 5 / Cha 5 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Stealth 8, Perception 7, Deception 6, Arcane 6, Intimidate 6
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft, Devil's Sight, See Invisibility, Perfect sight 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Shadow; Darkvision 120 ft. Shadow Meld (passive). Positive energy vulnerability. Cold Resistance (+2 DR). Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Shadow Rend [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 8 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 10 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (8 + 6 Str) + 4 Negative damage (Ignore DR); target loses 5 from Current Luck; max HP reduced by 12 until long rest.
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 Negative damage; Current Luck reduced by 6; max HP reduced by 18; Weakened + Sickened + Disoriented for 1 minute; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up) + 4 Negative damage; all conditions; max HP reduced by 24; target makes Fortitude [Hard] save or gains 1 Exhaustion level and loses 1 point of Constitution permanently until Major Restoration.
Void Lance [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 3 + Ranged Combat 6), Range: 120 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 18 Negative damage (15 + 3 Dex); Current Luck reduced by 4; max HP reduced by 8.
- **Margin 2:** 18 Negative damage, DR halved (round up); Current Luck reduced by 6; max HP reduced by 14; Weakened + Sickened for 1 minute; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 18 Negative damage, DR halved (round up); all conditions; max HP reduced by 20; target makes Fortitude [Easy] save or Stunned until end of their next turn.
### Special Abilities
Shadow Form: While in dim light or darkness, DR increases to 12 against all non-magical damage and non-Positive energy damage. Positive energy ignores this entirely.
Life Drain Aura: Creatures within 20 feet that are not Shadow creatures lose 4 from their maximum HP at the start of each of their turns. This maximum HP loss persists until long rest. A creature reduced to 0 maximum HP by this aura dies.
Darkness Domain (1/encounter) [Combat]: As 2 [Combat] actions, creates permanent magical darkness in a 60-foot diameter centered on itself. Permanent until dispelled by the Shadow Lord's dismissal. The Shadow Lord and Shadow creatures see through it normally.
Shadow Army (1/day): As a [Combat] action, the Shadow Lord calls 2 Shade Walkers from the Shadow Plane. They appear in unoccupied spaces within 30 feet and act on the Shadow Lord's initiative, following its direction. They are not under the summoner's control. Duration: 1 hour or until dismissed.
Planar Step (at-will): The Shadow Lord steps between shadows freely, teleporting up to 30 feet to any dim or dark location it can see as a [Move] action.
Resilient Defense (3/day): When the Shadow Lord fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
Shadow Lords reshape engagements through darkness rather than through direct force. Darkness Domain creates permanent darkness across a 60-foot diameter, immediately giving the Shadow Lord and any Shadow creatures present a massive advantage against opponents who cannot see in magical darkness. Shadow Army calls additional Shadow creatures under the Shadow Lord's direction, not the summoner's — which is a useful addition to the fight or a complication depending on whether instructions to the Shadow Lord are precise. Life Drain Aura at 20 feet erodes maximum HP at 4 per round from everything nearby. Planar Step at-will makes positioning it very difficult — it is always exactly where it chooses to be. A summoner who deploys one in an environment with any natural darkness and gives it clear instructions will find it turns the entire encounter into a fight that happens on its terms rather than the enemy's.

## Name: The Endless Dark
**Tier:** 5
**Origin:** Undead (Shadow)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 210 (Con 7 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 6 (primordial shadow-matter)
**Speed:** 60 ft, Fly 100 ft
**Attributes:** Str 6 / Con 7 / Agil 6 / Dex 5 / Int 6 / Wis 6 / Cha 5 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Stealth 8, Perception 8, Deception 7, Arcane 7, Intimidate 7
**Senses:** Darkvision unlimited, Devil's Sight, See Invisibility, Perfect Sight 120 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +6 (Agil 6)
**Creature Type:** Shadow; Darkvision unlimited range. Shadow Meld (passive). Positive energy vulnerability. Cold Resistance (+4 DR). Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Void Rend [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 6 + Melee Combat 7), Reach: 15 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 15 damage (9 + 6 Str) + 5 Negative damage (Ignore DR); target loses 6 from Current Luck; max HP reduced by 16 until long rest.
- **Margin 2:** 15 damage, DR halved (round up) + 5 Negative damage; Current Luck reduced by 8; max HP reduced by 24; Weakened + Sickened + Disoriented + Dazed for 1 minute; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 15 damage, DR halved (round up) + 5 Negative damage; all conditions; max HP reduced by 32; target makes Fortitude [Hard] save or loses 2 points of Constitution permanently until Major Restoration; target also loses 1 point of Wisdom permanently until Major Restoration.
Shadow Annihilation [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 5 + Ranged Combat 6), Range: 200 ft, 20-foot diameter.
- **Margin 1:**: 20 Negative damage (15 + 5 Dex); Current Luck reduced by 5; max HP reduced by 12.
- **Margin 2:** 20 Negative damage, DR halved (round up); Current Luck reduced by 8; max HP reduced by 20; Weakened + Sickened; no save.
- **Margin 3:** 20 Negative damage, DR halved (round up); all conditions; max HP reduced by 28; Stunned until end of their next turn; no save.
### Special Abilities
Shadow Form: In dim light or darkness, immune to all non-magical damage and non-Positive energy damage. In bright light, DR 6 applies normally.
Life Drain Aura: Creatures within 30 feet that are not Shadow creatures lose 6 from their maximum HP at the start of each of their turns. This maximum HP loss persists until long rest. A creature reduced to 0 maximum HP by this aura dies.
World Darkness (1/day) [Combat]: As 2 [Combat] actions, the Endless Dark extends its nature across a 120-foot diameter. All light sources within the area (magical and mundane) are suppressed for 1 hour. Positive energy weaves of cost 8 or higher can suppress the darkness within their area while active. The Endless Dark and all Shadow creatures see through the darkness.
Shadow Army (1/day): As a [Combat] action, the Shadow Lord calls 4 Shade Walkers and 2 Dark Stalkers from the Shadow Plane. They appear in unoccupied spaces within 30 feet and act on the Endless Dark's initiative, following its direction. They are not under the summoner's control. Duration: 1 hour or until dismissed.
Planar Step (at-will): The Endless Dark steps between shadows freely, teleporting up to 60 feet to any dim or dark location it can see as a [Move] action.
Consuming Darkness: When a creature dies within 60 feet of the Endless Dark, the Endless Dark regains HP equal to that creature's Constitution score × 4. This triggers regardless of what killed the creature.
Cold Immunity Sharing: The summoner and up to 4 designated allies gain immunity to cold damage while within 60 feet.
Resilient Defense (3/day): When the Endless Dark fails a saving throw, it may choose to succeed instead.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
The Endless Dark is the Shadow Plane's full predatory nature distilled into a single entity. It is old enough to understand precisely what summoners need from it and will follow clear instructions without requiring them to be simplified.

## Name: Ogre
**Tier:** 2
**Origin:** Nephilim (Earth)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 30 (Con 5 × 6, Massive)
**DR:** 2 (thick hide, natural armor)
**Speed:** 40 ft
**Attributes:** Str 6 / Con 5 / Agil 5 / Dex 4 / Int 3 / Wis 6 / Cha 4 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 3, Ranged Combat 2, Might 5, Intimidate 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Nephilim (Giant) — Large size (10×10 ft space, 10 ft reach). Powerful Build: counts as one size larger for carrying, pushing, dragging, lifting. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep.
### Attacks
Greatclub [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 3), Reach: 10 ft (Large creature with long weapon)
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (8 + 6 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up)
Javelin [Combat]: Ranged attack (Dex 4 + Ranged Combat 2), Range: 30/120 ft (Large creature, increased range)
1 success = 12 damage (8 + 4 Dex) (short range)
2 successes = 12 damage, DR halved (round up)
### Special Abilities
Powerful Build: Counts as Huge for carrying capacity, pushing, dragging, lifting
Thick Hide: Natural DR 2 (already included in stat block DR)
### Equipment
Greatclub (two-handed weapon), 3 javelins
### Tactics
Ogres throw javelins as enemies approach, then wade into melee with their greatclub, using their reach to strike before enemies can close. They focus on one target at a time, attempting to crush them with overwhelming strength.

## Name: Earth Sentinel
**Tier:** 4
**Origin:** Elemental (Earth)
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 210 (Con 7 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 8 (stone body, natural armor)
**Speed:** 20 ft., Burrow 20 ft.
**Attributes:** Str 7 / Con 7 / Agil 5 / Dex 4 / Int 3 / Wis 7 / Cha 3 / Luck 7
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Might 6, Perception 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft., Tremorsense 60 ft.
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Elemental (Earth); Immune to poison damage, Poisoned condition, disease, exhaustion, Paralyzed, Petrified, Unconscious. Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep. Mindless: immune to mind-affecting effects (charm, fear, sleep, psychic effects).
### Attacks
Slam [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 7), Reach 15 ft. (Huge creature).
- **Margin 1:**: 18 damage (11 + 7 Str).
- **Margin 2:** 18 damage, DR halved (round up).
### Special Abilities
Earth Glide: The Sentinel burrows through nonmagical unworked earth and stone without disturbing the material it moves through. It leaves no tunnel, no displaced soil, no warning of its passage until it chooses to emerge.
Tremorsense: The Sentinel detects all creatures in contact with the ground within 60 feet. The [Hidden] condition does not apply to creatures detected this way, though they still gain the [Concealed] condition against the Sentinel's ### Attacks.
Siege Monster: The Sentinel deals double damage to objects and structures.
Earth Tremor (Recharge 5–8) [Combat]: Costs 2 actions. The Sentinel drives its fists into the ground, sending violent tremors through the earth in a 60-foot diameter. Every creature on the ground in the area makes a Reflex save opposed by the Sentinel's Con 6 + Wis 3. Creatures that fail take 12 bludgeoning damage and gain the Prone condition. Creatures that succeed take no damage. Flying creatures and those not in contact with the ground are unaffected. The Sentinel may use Earth Tremor even while submerged via Earth Glide; when it does, it erupts from the ground as part of the same action.
Resistances and Vulnerabilities
Resistances: Nonmagical bludgeoning damage reduced by 4 
Vulnerability: The Sentinel takes full damage with no DR reduction from thunder (Sonic) damge. On any hit that deals thunder damage, it must make a Fortitude [Easy] save. On failure, it loses its Earth Glide ability until the start of its next turn.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
The Earth Elemental Sentinel is not a creature that hunts. It guards. Something called it here — a nexus, a sacred site, a ward placed by a long-dead mage — and here it remains, patient in the way that stone is patient, which is to say without any awareness that patience is required.
It does not distinguish between threats and travelers. Anything that enters the area it protects is a threat. It emerges from the ground with no warning, no posturing, no moment of hesitation before the first slam lands.
### Tactics
The Sentinel opens from below, using Earth Glide to position directly beneath the most clustered group before emerging and immediately triggering Earth Tremor as part of the same turn. It follows with ### Attacks against the nearest standing target, prioritizing whatever is closest rather than identifying priority threats. It does not retreat. It does not negotiate.and continues attacking until either the threat is gone or it is destroyed.

## Name: Hag Eye
**Tier:** 2
**Origin:** Fae (Water)
**Size:** Tiny
**HP:** 15 (Con 5 × 3, Fragile)
**DR:** 0
**Speed:** 50 ft.
**Attributes:** Str 4 / Con 5 / Agil 8 / Dex 6 / Int 3 / Wis 5 / Cha 2 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 5, Perception 5, Stealth 6
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft.
**Initiative Bonus:** +8 (Agil 8)
**Creature Type:** Fae; Fortune on saving throws against charm effects. Sustained by magical energy rather than food, drink, or sleep.
**Languages:** None spoken. Communicates directly with its creator hag through a shared sensory link.
### Attacks 
The Hag Eye has no meaningful physical ### Attacks. Str 1 renders it incapable of harming anything with its body. Its entire offensive capability is its gaze.
### Special Abilities
Hag's Sight: The hag that created this creature sees and hears everything the Hag Eye perceives, regardless of distance, provided both are on the same plane. The hag can close or open this link as a [Free] action on her turn.
Unnatural **Speed:** The Hag Eye's movement does not provoke opportunity ### Attacks. It can move through spaces occupied by other creatures without penalty.
Withering Gaze (At-Will) [Combat]: Costs 2 actions. The Hag Eye fixes its gaze on one creature within 60 feet that it can see. The target makes a Will save, opposed by the Hag Eye's Wis 5 + Perception 5). On failure, the target suffers one of the following effects chosen by the Hag Eye at the time of the gaze. A creature that succeeds on its save cannot be targeted by Withering Gaze again for the remainder of the encounter.
Each round on the Hag Eye's turn, it may redirect its gaze to a new target as a [Combat] action (2 actions), leaving the previous target's condition in place until that target recovers. A creature can only be under one gaze effect at a time. 
Asleep: The target gains the Unconscious condition. It wakes immediately if it takes any damage or if an adjacent creature spends a [Combat] action to shake it awake.
Panicked: The target gains the Fleeing condition with the Hag Eye as the source of fear. On each of its turns the target must use its full movement to move away from the Hag Eye by the safest available route. If the target reaches a position at least 60 feet from the Hag Eye where it can no longer see it, the effect ends immediately. The target may make a Will [Easy] save at the end of each of its turns.
Sickened: The target gains the Sickened condition and the Disoriented condition simultaneously. At the end of each of its turns the target may make a Will [Easy] save.
Resistances
Resistances: Nonmagical bludgeoning, piercing, and slashing damage: reduce incoming damage of these types by 3.
Condition Immunities: Exhaustion, Frightened, Grappled, Prone, Restrained, Stunned, Unconscious. 
The Hag Eye's nature as a detached piece of hag magic makes most physical conditions meaningless. It has no legs to trip, no throat to choke, no mind of its own to terrify.
### Description
A Hag Eye is exactly what the name suggests — an eye, plucked from a lesser creature or conjured from hag-magic, animated and sent out into the world as a scout. It moves in silence, leaves no tracks, and reports everything it sees back to the hag who made it. 
Alone it is nearly harmless in the conventional sense. It cannot lift a stone or open a door. What it can do is look at something in a particular way, and the things it looks at have a tendency to fall asleep, or start running, or feel profoundly unwell.
 Most parties encounter a Hag Eye and immediately understand the problem: somewhere within range, a hag is watching through it. Destroying the eye is straightforward given its fragility. Finding the hag watching through it is the actual challenge.
### Tactics
The Hag Eye avoids direct engagement entirely. It uses its extraordinary Agility and Stealth to position where it can observe the party, feeding information back to its creator while staying out of reach.
 When forced to act offensively it prioritizes Asleep on the most dangerous martial threat, then Panicked on whoever is most likely to cause problems while running, then Sickened on spellcasters or anyone left standing. It will not engage a target that has already saved against its gaze.
The Hag Eye flees at the first sign it has been spotted. It is nearly impossible to catch at speed 50 with Unnatural Speed preventing opportunity ### Attacks.

## Name: Korsivar
**Origin:** Native (Elf)
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 48 (Con 4 × 12, Standard)
**DR:** S: 6, P: 5, B: 4 (Hide)
**Speed:** 35 ft.
**Attributes:** Str 3 / Con 4 / Agil 3 / Dex 4 / Int 2 / Wis 6 / Cha 3 / Luck 3
**Skills:** Melee Combat 3, Perception 5, Stealth 5, Wilderness 5, Medicine 3, Empathy 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft.
**Initiative Bonus:** +3 (Agil 3)
**Creature Type:** Humanoid (Elven)
**Background:** Hermit
**Languages:** Local (region-dependent), Hadriaeth
### Attacks
Staff of the Darach [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 3 + Melee Combat 3), Reach 5 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 7 damage (Versatile one-handed: 6 + 1 magic bonus).
- **Margin 2:** 10 damage (7 + 3 Str).
Two-handed:
- **Margin 1:**: 8 damage (7 + 1 versatile bonus).
- **Margin 2:** 11 damage (8 + 3 Str).
The staff is a magic weapon. ### Attacks with it subtract 1 from the skill die result (the +1 attack bonus).
### Special Abilities
Land's Stride: Nonmagical difficult terrain costs Korsivar no extra movement. He moves through nonmagical vegetation without penalty regardless of thorns or similar hazards. Fortune on Reflex saves against magically created or manipulated plants.
Mask of the Wild: Korsivar may attempt to Hide while only lightly obscured by natural phenomena;  foliage, rain, snow, mist. He does not need full cover to attempt a Stealth check in natural environments.
Communion with the Wound (1/long rest): During a short rest, Korsivar communes with the corrupted aspect of nature he serves. He recovers Energy equal to 2x Wisdom score (rounded up). This recovery cannot exceed his maximum Energy pool.
Weaves
Casting Stat: Wisdom 6
Mastery/Potentials: Earth 6/6, Water 4/5, Positive 3/4, Negative 4/4, Space 4/4, Time 3/3, Air 3/3, Fire 1/2
Energy Pool: 42
Targeting Roll: Wisdom + Primary Energy Mastery
Korsivar is a Divine Gift caster devoted to a twisted interpretation of Hirnaloyta; not nature as growth and balance but nature as indifferent predation, rot, and reclamation. His patron's Primary energy is Earth (+2) and Secondary is Positive (+1), though he weaponizes both.
| Mend Flesh | Positive 3 | 1 (Simple) | Touch only; heals 8 HP |

| Revealing Light | Positive 2 + Space 6 = 8 | 2 | Outlined, Fortune on ### Attacks against outlined creatures |
| Ice Shard | Water 2 + Space 1 = 3 | 2 | Close range, 8 cold damage |

| Shatter | Air 4 + Space 4 = 8 | 2 | Medium range, 10 ft. radius, 16 thunder damage |
| Shadow Sphere | Negative 3 + Space 7 = 10 | 2 | Medium range, 15 ft. radius magical darkness |

 
### Equipment
[[Staff of the Darach]]
### Description
### Tactics
Korsivar does not open fights in the open if he can avoid it. He uses Mask of the Wild and the staff's Verdant Veil to position before combat begins, ideally in natural terrain where he can hide between actions.
His opening move is Shadow Sphere dropped on the party's rear — splitting their line of sight and creating chaos — followed immediately by Grasping Vines across the forward group. He then targets isolated or immobilized creatures with Thorn Whip to drag them further out of position while his allies close in.
Barkward goes on himself the moment melee becomes unavoidable, and he will burn staff charges freely to keep his personal Energy pool for offense. He saves Shape Stone for terrain manipulation — blocking exits, trapping individuals, collapsing cover — rather than direct attack. He flees rather than dies. 

## Name: Swamp Beast
**Tier:** 4
**Origin:** Native (Monstrosity)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 240 (Con 8 × 30, Boss)
**DR:** 5 (dense vegetal mass, natural armor)
**Speed:** 20 ft., Swim 20 ft.
**Attributes:** Str 6 / Con 8 / Agil 5 / Dex 4 / Int 3 / Wis 7 / Cha 3 / Luck 7
**Skills:** Melee Combat 7, Stealth 4, Might 5
**Senses:** Blindsight 60 ft. (blind beyond this radius)
**Initiative Bonus:** +5 (Agil 5)
**Creature Type:** Monstrosity
### Attacks
Slam [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 5 + Melee Combat 7), Reach 10 ft. (Large creature)
- **Margin 1:**: 16 damage (10 + 6 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 16 damage, DR halved (round up)
Engulf Trigger: If two Swamp Beast slam ### Attacks hit the same Medium or smaller target in the same turn, the Swamp Beast immediately uses Engulf on that target as a [Free] action.
### Special Abilities
Blindsight: The Swamp Beast perceives its surroundings through vibration and chemical detection within 60 feet. It cannot perceive anything beyond this radius and is effectively blind to it. The [Hidden] condition does not apply to creatures within 60 feet, though they still gain [Concealed] against the Beast's ### Attacks.
Lightning Absorption: The Swamp Beast is immune to lightning damage. Whenever it would take lightning damage, it instead regains HP equal to the damage that would have been dealt. This recovery cannot exceed its maximum HP.
Lightning Surge: When the Swamp Beast absorbs lightning damage, in addition to recovering HP it gains temporary HP equal to half the absorbed amount (rounded down) until the start of its next turn. These temporary HP are lost at the start of its next turn regardless of whether they were depleted.
False Appearance: While the Swamp Beast remains motionless in swamp terrain, it is indistinguishable from a mound of rotting vegetation. A creature that succeeds on a Perception [Hard] check notices something wrong before the Beast moves.
Engulf [Free]: Triggered when two slams hit the same Medium or smaller target. The Swamp Beast engulfs the target, drawing it into its mass. The engulfed creature gains the Blinded, Restrained, and Bound conditions simultaneously and cannot breathe.
At the start of each of the Swamp Beast's turns, an engulfed creature makes a Fortitude save, opposed by the Beast's Con 6 + Wis 3). On failure, the creature takes 12 bludgeoning damage. On success, no damage that turn.
The Swamp Beast can have only one creature engulfed at a time. The engulfed creature moves with the Beast when it moves. To escape, the creature must win an opposed Athletics or Acrobatics check against the Beast's Might 5 + Str 6 on its turn, spending a [Combat] action . An adjacent ally may attempt the same check as a [Combat] action to pull the engulfed creature free.
The engulfed creature is released immediately if the Swamp Beast is reduced to 0 HP.
Spit Acid (Recharge 5–8) [Combat]: Costs 2 actions. The Beast expels a stream of acidic fluid in a 15-foot cone. Every creature in the area makes a Reflex save, opposed by the Beast's Con 6 + Wis 3. Creatures that fail take 28 acid damage (half DR). Creatures that succeed take 14 acid damage.
Resistances and Immunities
Immunities: Lightning damage (see Lightning Absorption), Blinded condition, Deafened condition, Exhaustion
Resistances: Cold damage and fire damage — reduce incoming damage of these types by 4. The Beast's dense wet mass insulates against both extremes.
Lair Feature: Living Swamp
While the Swamp Beast is in its territory and conscious, the swamp around it responds as an extension of its body. At the start of each round, the Beast may cause one of the following effects without spending any of its actions. Only one effect may be active at a time; triggering a new one ends the previous.
Grasping Roots: Up to three roots or vines erupt from the ground at points the Beast can perceive within 60 feet. Each root targets one creature. Each targeted creature makes a Reflex save, opposed by the Beast's Might 5 + Con 6. Creatures that fail are Restrained. Each root is a separate object: HP 12, DR 2, immune to poison and psychic damage. A root disappears when reduced to 0 HP, when the Beast triggers a different lair effect, or when the Beast dies. A Restrained creature is released when its root is destroyed.
Spore Cloud: A cloud of toxic spores fills a 20-foot diameter sphere centered on a point the Beast can perceive within 60 feet. The cloud does not move once placed. Every creature in the cloud when it appears makes a Fortitude save, opposed by the Beast's Con 6 + Wis 3. Creatures that fail take 16 poison damage. Creatures that succeed take 8 poison damage. All creatures within the cloud gain the Blinded condition while inside it. The cloud persists until the Beast triggers a different lair effect or dies.
When the Swamp Beast dies, all active lair effects end immediately and the roots collapse.
Action Points (3): Boss template. Spend at the end of any other creature's turn: Move (1, up to half speed), Attack (1, no Multiple Action Penalty), Detect (1), Special Ability (2). Regains all at the start of its own turn.
### Description
The Swamp Beast is old enough that the swamp has stopped being its habitat and started being its body. 
### Tactics
The Swamp Beast begins most encounters already in position, motionless and indistinguishable from the surrounding mire. It waits until something comes within 10 feet before erupting.
Its priority is Engulf. Once something is engulfed, it releases the spore cloud to deter the target's allies  grinding down the engulfed creature with automatic damage each round while slamming anything that gets close.
Spit Acid is its only ranged option and it uses it on clustered groups or when melee is temporarily unavailable.
Grasping Roots targets ranged attackers or spellcasters the Beast can perceive through Blindsight, pinning them in place. It does not retreat. It does not recognize the concept.

## Name: Swamp Cat
**Tier:** 2
**Origin:** Native (Beast)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 25 (Con 5 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 2 (natural hide)
**Speed:** 40 ft.
**Attributes:** Str 5 / Con 5 / Agil 7 / Dex 5 / Int 3 / Wis 5 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 4, Stealth 5, Perception 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 120 ft., Keen Smell
**Initiative Bonus:** +7 (Agil 7)
**Creature Type:** Beast
### Attacks
Bite [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 4), Reach 5 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (9 + 5 Str).
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up).
Claw [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 7 + Melee Combat 4), Reach 5 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (9 + 5 Str).
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up).
### Special Abilities
Keen Smell: Scent-based Perception checks have Fortune.
Pack ### Tactics: When an ally is within 5 feet of the target, subtract 1 from both attack dice.
Pounce: If the Swamp Cat moves at least 20 feet straight toward a target and then hits with a claw attack on the same turn, the target must make a Fortitude save, opposed by the Cat's Str 5 + Melee Combat 5). On failure, the target gains the Prone condition. If the target is Prone, the Swamp Cat may immediately make one bite attack against it as a [Free] action.
### Tactics
Swamp Cats hunt in pairs or small groups, using the terrain to stalk prey before committing to Pounce. Pack ### Tactics and Pounce together make their opening turn devastating — one cat charges to trigger Prone while the second ### Attacks with subtracted dice against the downed target. They break off if badly wounded, retreating into the swamp to recover.

## Name: Turtle, Giant
**Tier:** 2
**Origin:** Native (Beast)
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 25 (Con 5 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 8 (shell, natural armor) / 3 while Prone (belly exposed)
**Speed:** 30 ft., Swim 40 ft.
**Attributes:** Str 8 / Con 5 / Agil 4 / Dex 4 / Int 3 / Wis 6 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 4, Perception 3
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft.
**Initiative Bonus:** +4 (Agil 4)
**Creature Type:** Beast
### Attacks
Bite [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 4 + Melee Combat 4), Reach 10 ft. (Large creature, long neck).
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (6 + 8 Str).
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up).
### Special Abilities
Amphibious: The Giant Turtle breathes air and water freely.
Shell Defense: The Giant Turtle's DR is 8 against all physical ### Attacks while upright. While Prone, its belly is exposed and DR drops to 3 against all damage types.
Stable: Whenever the Giant Turtle would gain the Prone condition, it makes a Fortitude [Easy] save before the condition applies. On success, the condition is avoided entirely. On failure, the turtle is upside down and cannot right itself without effort.To stand, it must succeed on an Acrobatics [Hard] check and spend its full movement that turn.
Crushing Bite: On Margin 2, the target is Grappled in addition to taking damage. While Grappled this way the turtle can maintain the hold without further attack rolls; the target makes an opposed Athletics or Acrobatics check against the Turtle's Might 5 + Str 6 to escape.
### Tactics
The Giant Turtle is not an aggressive predator. It bites when threatened or when something comes within reach of its neck, and it holds on. Most encounters begin because a party disturbed it. It does not pursue beyond its immediate territory and breaks off if left alone.

## Crocodile
**Tier:** 1/2
**Size:** Large
**HP:** 25 (Con 5 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 2 (scaled hide)
**Speed:** 20 ft., Swim 30 ft.
**Attributes:** Str 8 / Con 5 / Agil 4 / Dex 4 / Int 3 / Wis 6 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 4, Stealth 3, Perception 2
**Senses:** Normal
**Initiative Bonus:** +4 (Agil 4)
**Creature Type:** Beast
**Languages:** None
 
### Attacks
Bite [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 4 + Melee Combat 4), Reach 5 ft.
- **Margin 1:** 14 damage (6 + 8 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up)
- - *Special:** On any hit (- **Margin 1:**+), the target is Grappled. While Grappled this way the Crocodile cannot bite a different target. The Grappled creature makes an opposed Athletics or Acrobatics check against the Crocodile's Might 3 + Str 4 to escape (1 success required). If the Bite Grapples a target, Death Roll replaces Bite for all subsequent ### Attacks against that target while the Grapple holds.
Death Roll [Combat]: Melee attack against the currently Grappled target only (Agil 2 + Melee Combat 3), Reach 5 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (6 + 8 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up)
- - *Special:** The target makes a Fortitude save (Con + Luck, opposed by the Crocodile's Str 4 + Melee Combat 3). On failure, the target takes an additional 4 damage and gains the Disoriented condition until the end of its next turn. If the target is submerged, it also advances one step along the Drowning condition progression.
 
### Special Abilities
Hold Breath: The Crocodile holds its breath for a number of minutes equal to its Constitution score. In combat, this extends to a number of rounds equal to Con × 8 before it must surface.
 
Swamp Stealth: In water or swamp terrain, Stealth checks have Fortune.
 
### Tactics
Crocodiles wait submerged with only eyes and nostrils above the waterline, motionless until something comes within reach. The opening bite is everything — if it lands and Grapples, it drags the target into the water and Death Rolls. On land it is slow and commits fully to whatever it has hold of, rarely releasing a target voluntarily. Against armored targets it is far less effective; its damage at - **Margin 1:** is eaten almost entirely by medium armor.

## Old One Eye
**Tier:** 3
**Size:** Huge
**HP:** 25 (Con 5 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 3 (scaled hide)
**Speed:** 30 ft., Swim 50 ft.
**Attributes:** Str 8 / Con 5 / Agil 4 / Dex 4 / Int 3 / Wis 6 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 4, Stealth 6, Perception 5
**Senses:** Normal (one eye — see below)
**Initiative Bonus:** +4 (Agil 4)
**Creature Type:** Beast
**Languages:** None
 
### Attacks
Bite [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 4 + Melee Combat 4), Reach 10 ft. (Huge creature)
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (6 + 8 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up)
- - *Special:** On any hit (- **Margin 1:**+), the target is Grappled and Restrained simultaneously. While Grappled this way Old One Eye cannot bite a different target. The Grappled creature makes an opposed Athletics or Acrobatics check against Old One Eye's Might 5 + Str 6 to escape (Hard task — 2 successes).
Death Roll [Combat]: Melee attack against the currently Grappled target only (Agil 4 + Melee Combat 6), Reach 5 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (6 + 8 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up)
- - *Special:** The target makes a Fortitude save (Con + Luck, opposed by Old One Eye's Str 6 + Melee Combat 6). On failure, the target takes an additional 6 damage and gains the Disoriented condition until the end of its next turn. If the target is submerged, it also advances one step along the Drowning condition progression.
Tail [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 4 + Melee Combat 4), Reach 15 ft. (Huge creature, sweeping tail)
- Can only target creatures not currently Grappled by Old One Eye.
- **Margin 1:**: 14 damage (6 + 8 Str)
- **Margin 2:** 14 damage, DR halved (round up)
- - *Special:** On Margin 2, the target makes a Fortitude save (Con + Luck, opposed by Old One Eye's Str 6 + Melee Combat 6). On failure, the target gains the Prone condition.
Multiattack [Combat]: Old One Eye makes one bite or death roll attack and one tail attack.
- *Shared Attribute Roll (Agil 4): Roll once, apply to both ### Attacks. Roll Melee Combat skill die separately for each.
- If the bite Grapples a target, the death roll replaces bite in all subsequent turns against that target while the Grapple holds.
 
### Special Abilities
Hold Breath: Old One Eye holds its breath for a number of minutes equal to its Constitution score outside of combat. In combat this extends to Con × 8 rounds before it must surface.
 
One Eye: Old One Eye lost its left eye long ago to something that did not survive the encounter. It has no peripheral vision on its left side. Attackers approaching or attacking from the left flank subtract 1 from both attack dice against it, as it cannot easily track threats from that direction. Old One Eye's Perception checks relying on sight to the left automatically fail.
 
Swamp Stealth: In water or swamp terrain, Stealth checks have Fortune. If Old One Eye is motionless and undetected when combat begins, all creatures that failed to detect it are Surprised on its first turn.
 
Resistances: Nonmagical bludgeoning, piercing, and slashing damage — reduce incoming damage of these types by 3.
 
### Description
Old One Eye has lived in the Alderweed longer than anyone can reliably remember. The locals know it is there the way they know the water is cold — as a fact about the environment rather than a threat to be solved. Children are told to stay away from the deep channels. Most of them listen.
 
The missing eye is the only way to distinguish it from the water itself when it lies still. It does not patrol or hunt with any visible strategy. It simply occupies the swamp, and things that come within range of its jaws stop being a concern.
 
Whatever took its eye left scars that run from the empty socket to the back of its jaw. The scars are old enough that the scales have grown back over them in ridges. Scholars who have seen the marks and survived the encounter suggest the wound came from another predator of significant size. No one has confirmed what kind.
 
### Tactics
 
Old One Eye's entire strategy is the ambush. It lies motionless below the waterline using Swamp Stealth, sometimes for hours, until something comes close enough. The first round is usually decisive — Surprised targets cannot defend, and a Huge creature biting through light armor and immediately beginning Death Roll on the Grappled target while its tail sweeps anyone trying to intervene is genuinely dangerous to a Tier 2–3 party.
 
Once it has something in its jaws it does not let go. Death Roll replaces Bite every subsequent turn against the Grappled target, grinding through HP while the tail handles anyone approaching to help. It ignores threats it cannot reach rather than releasing its current target — creatures more than 15 feet away are simply not its problem until the thing in its mouth stops moving.
 
Its left side is the tactical opening. A coordinated party that positions fighters on Old One Eye's blind flank while a second threat occupies its attention from the right forces it into a choice it does not have the intelligence to resolve well. It will focus on whichever target it already has hold of and trust its DR to absorb what it cannot see coming.
 
It retreats into deep water when reduced below 40 HP, holding its breath and sinking to the bottom. It will not resurface while threats remain visible. Whether the party has the means and the will to follow it down is the question that ends most encounters with Old One Eye.

---

## Haunt
**Tier:** 3
**Size:** Medium
**HP:** 25 (Con 5 × 5, Tough)
**DR:** 0 (incorporeal — physical DR does not apply; see Resistances)
**Speed:** 0 ft., Fly 30 ft. (Hover)
**Attributes:** Str 8 / Con 5 / Agil 4 / Dex 4 / Int 3 / Wis 6 / Cha 3 / Luck 5
**Skills:** Melee Combat 4, Perception 5, Stealth 5, Arcane 3
**Senses:** Blindsight 30 ft., Darkvision 60 ft. (including magical darkness)
**Initiative Bonus:** +4 (Agil 4)
**Creature Type:** Undead
**Languages:** Understands Elven (Hadriaeth). Cannot speak.

### Attacks
Life Drain [Combat]: Melee attack (Agil 4 + Melee Combat 4), Reach 5 ft.
- **Margin 1:**: 14 necrotic damage (6 + 8 Str). The target's maximum HP is reduced by 8. Current HP is unaffected unless it now exceeds the new maximum, in which case reduce current HP to the new maximum.
- **Margin 2:** 14 necrotic damage, DR halved (round up). The target's maximum HP is reduced by 11.

Maximum HP lost to Life Drain restores fully after a long rest. Any magical healing that restores HP simultaneously restores an equal amount of lost maximum HP up to the total drained. A target whose maximum HP is reduced to 0 dies.

### Special Abilities
Undead Traits:
- Immune to poison damage, Poisoned condition, disease, exhaustion
- Immune to the Charmed, Grappled, Paralyzed, Petrified, Prone, Restrained, and Unconscious conditions
- Does not need air, food, drink, or sleep
- Mindless immunity does not apply — the Haunt retains intelligence and volition

Incorporeal Movement: The Haunt moves through other creatures and objects as if they were difficult terrain. It takes 4 force damage if it ends its turn inside an object.

Sunlight Sensitivity: While in direct sunlight, the Haunt has Misfortune on attack rolls and on Perception checks that rely on sight.

Frightful Presence (3/day) [Combat]: Costs 1 action. Every creature of the Haunt's choice within 30 feet that can see it makes a Will save (Wis + Luck, opposed by the Haunt's Cha 4 + Perception 5). Creatures that fail gain the Frightened condition for 1 minute with the Haunt as the source. They may repeat the save at the end of each of their turns (1 success ends the effect). A creature that succeeds on its save, or recovers from the condition, cannot be affected by this Haunt's Frightful Presence for 24 hours.

Silver and Magic: Nonmagical, non-silver weapons deal no damage to the Haunt. Magical weapons and silver weapons deal damage normally. This is not a DR reduction — nonmagical non-silver damage simply does not register.

### Resistances and Immunities

Immunities: Necrotic damage, poison damage, nonmagical non-silver bludgeoning, nonmagical non-silver piercing, nonmagical non-silver slashing

Resistances: Acid, cold, fire, lightning, and thunder damage — reduce incoming damage of these types by 4. Magical and silver bludgeoning, piercing, and slashing — reduce incoming damage by 4.

### Weaves
Casting Stat: Wisdom 5
Targeting Roll: Wisdom + primary Energy Mastery

The Haunt does not channel magic through study or faith. Its weaves are the residue of whatever it was in life, leaking outward as instinct. It cannot explain what it does. It simply does it.

| Weave | Energy | Actions | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|
| Fog Cloud | Air 2 + Space 4 = 6 | 2 | At will |
| Invisibility | Space 4 + Time 3 = 7 | 2 | At will |
| Minor Illusion | Space 3 + Time 2 = 5 | 2 | At will |
| Phantom Image | Space 4 + Time 2 = 6 | 2 | At will |
| Complex Illusion | Space 6 + Time 3 = 9 | 2 | 1/encounter |

Energy Pool: 32. The Haunt's pool does not recover mid-encounter. Its at-will designation reflects that these weaves are cheap enough relative to its pool that it can cast them freely across a standard encounter without running dry — not that they cost nothing.

Fog Cloud: Creates a heavily obscured sphere 40 feet diameter at medium range (60 ft.). All creatures inside gain the Concealed condition against all attackers. The Haunt can see through its own fog using Blindsight within 30 feet.

Invisibility: The Haunt gains the Hidden condition. The effect ends if it takes a [Combat] action or casts a weave other than Fog Cloud, which it can maintain while invisible since Fog Cloud requires no ongoing action to sustain once placed.

Minor Illusion and Phantom Image: Function as written in the weave list. The Haunt uses these to create sounds, figures, and movement — a voice from the wrong direction, the shape of something familiar in the fog, footsteps retreating down a corridor that has no exit.

### Description
A Haunt is what remains when something died with unfinished business and the pneuma could not let go. It understands Elven but cannot speak — whatever part of its former self held language is gone. What remains is hunger, patience, and the specific kind of cunning that belongs to predators that have been hunting the same territory for a very long time.

It does not attack immediately. It watches. The fog goes up first, then the illusions start — sounds that don't match their apparent sources, shapes glimpsed at the edge of torchlight, the sense that something is just behind you in a room you know is empty. By the time a creature realizes what is happening, the Haunt is already within reach.

What it wants is unclear. The life drain suggests sustenance, but the Haunt does not seem to need it the way living things need food. It may simply be habit — the echo of something it did in life, continuing past the point where life gave it meaning.

### Tactics
The Haunt opens at range, placing Fog Cloud to fragment the party and eliminate sightlines. It then uses Invisibility and moves into the fog where its Blindsight functions and the party's vision does not. Phantom Image and Minor Illusion run simultaneously to pull individual creatures away from the group — a familiar voice, a figure retreating into the dark, the sound of someone calling for help from the wrong direction.

Once a target is isolated the Haunt uses Frightful Presence before closing to Life Drain range. A Frightened creature trying to flee has already moved away from its allies. The Haunt follows, draining maximum HP with each hit, and retreats back into the fog before the rest of the party can respond.

It prioritizes one target at a time and does not switch until that target is dead or the tactical situation forces it.

Sunlight is its only reliable deterrent. A party that can create or sustain a Magical Radiant Light effect of sufficient energy inside the fog forces the Haunt to disengage or fight at Misfortune — its preferred environment stops being safe. It will withdraw rather than fight on those terms, returning when the light source expires.

Silver weapons and magical ### Attacks are the only physical options. A party without either cannot damage it through conventional means and should treat the encounter as a puzzle rather than a fight until they find another approach.
